### [1] [A Fast and Energy-Efficient Latch-Based Memristive Analog Content-Addressable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11847)
*Paul-Philipp Manea,Aishwarya Natarajan,Jim Ignowski,John Paul Strachan,Luca Buonanno*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强闩锁比较器的忆阻器模拟内容可寻址存储器(SALM aCAM)单元，通过动态电流竞争比较器替代传统静态分压结构，在22 nm FD-SOI工艺下实现33%的读能耗降低、近零静态搜索功耗，并消除了增益与串扰限制，支持大规模阵列扩展和数据集感知的能效-延迟权衡优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统6T2M结构的aCAM存在静态搜索功耗大、电压增益有限以及匹配线串扰严重的问题，限制了模拟精度和可扩展性，亟需一种新型电路架构突破这些瓶颈。

Method: 提出SALM aCAM单元，用动态电流竞争比较器替代静态电压分压；引入顺序/并行锁存共享机制；开发基于22 nm FD-SOI工艺SPICE查找表的电路精确行为模型；集成到X-TIME决策树编译器中实现数据集感知的能效优化框架。

Result: 相比6T2M，读能耗降低33%；在代表性工作负载下可实现最高50%的能耗降低（3倍延迟代价）；在高维数据集上保持接近软件的精度，而基线设计因增益不足和累积串扰导致精度下降。

Conclusion: SALM通过动态比较器架构有效解决了传统aCAM的静态功耗、增益和串扰问题，兼顾精度与能效，为大规模边缘AI和嵌入式智能中的联想计算提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Analog content-addressable memories (aCAMs) based on memristors provide a promising pathway toward energy-efficient large-scale associative computing for Edge AI and embedded intelligence applications. They have been successfully applied to decision-tree inference and extend the capabilities of compute-in-memory (CIM) architectures beyond conventional vector-matrix multiplication. However, conventional designs such as the 6T2M architecture suffer from static search power, limited voltage gain, and pronounced match-line crosstalk, constraining analog precision and scalability. We introduce a strong-arm latched memristor (SALM) aCAM cell that replaces static voltage division with a dynamic current-race comparator, enabling high regenerative gain, intrinsic result latching, and near-zero static search power. Compared to 6T2M, SALM reduces read energy by 33% at identical latency while eliminating the gain and crosstalk limitations that prevent 6T2M from scaling to large arrays. SALM further enables scalable sequential and parallel latch sharing, and a dataset-aware optimization framework exposes an explicit energy-latency tradeoff, achieving up to 50% energy reduction at 3x latency across representative workloads. To enable architectural exploration, we develop a circuit-accurate behavioral model derived from SPICE lookup tables in 22 nm FD-SOI technology, capturing match-line dynamics and crosstalk. Integrated into the X-TIME decision-tree compiler, this framework demonstrates that SALM maintains near-software accuracy for high-dimensional datasets, whereas baseline designs degrade due to limited gain and cumulative crosstalk.

</details>

### [2] [ECHO: Continuous Hierarchical Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10993)
*Yanbin Hu,Jin Cui,Jiayi Lu,Ruixuan Yang,Jun Ye,Boran Zhao,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ECHO框架，通过双曲自编码器将VLA模型隐藏状态映射到连续层次空间，构建语义记忆树实现高效检索与背景巩固机制，在LIBERO-Long上相比基线提升12.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强架构多采用线性或扁平化存储，缺乏操作类别的结构先验和层次化组织，导致长程任务中经验检索效率低、对新任务组合的泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出ECHO框架：1) 使用双曲自编码器将VLA隐藏状态映射到连续层次空间；2) 利用双曲度量和蕴涵约束机制将经验向量组织为语义记忆树，支持自上而下检索；3) 背景巩固机制通过几何插值和结构分裂持续优化记忆树，支持虚拟记忆合成。

Result: 在LIBERO-Long上相比π₀基线绝对提升12.8%的执行成功率；在跨套件未见长程任务上改善组合泛化能力；初步真实世界实验验证有效性。

Conclusion: 层次化记忆组织能有效提升VLA模型在长程操作任务中的性能，双曲空间为经验结构化和高效检索提供了理想几何基础。

Abstract: Memory capacity is a critical factor determining the performance of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in long-horizon manipulation tasks. Existing memory-augmented architectures primarily rely on linear or flat storage, lacking structural priors for manipulation categories and hierarchical organization. This deficiency hinders efficient experience retrieval and limits generalization to unseen long-horizon task compositions. Inspired by the hierarchical organization of human experience, we propose ECHO (Experience Consolidation and Hierarchical Organization), a novel memory framework operating within a Continuous Hierarchical Space. By employing a hyperbolic autoencoder, ECHO maps VLA hidden states into this space. Leveraging hyperbolic metrics and entailment constraint mechanisms, experience vectors are organized into a semantic memory tree that supports efficient top-down retrieval. In parallel, a background consolidation mechanism continuously refines the memory tree through geometric interpolation and structural splitting, supporting virtual memory synthesis in the continuous space. We integrate ECHO into the $π_0$ foundation model. Evaluations on LIBERO and preliminary real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, notably achieving a 12.8% absolute improvement in execution success rate over the $π_0$ baseline on LIBERO-Long, while improving compositional generalization on cross-suite unseen long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [3] [ForceFlow: Learning to Feel and Act via Contact-Driven Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11048)
*Shuoheng Zhang,Yifu Yuan,Hongyao Tang,Yan Zheng,Qiaojun Yu,Pengyi Li,Guowei Huang,Helong Huang,Xingyue Quan,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceFlow是一个基于流匹配(flow matching)的力感知反应式框架，采用非对称多模态融合架构将力信号作为全局调节信号，并通过视觉-触觉两阶段任务分解机制，在六类真实接触丰富操作任务中相比强基线ForceVLA实现了37%的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模仿学习方法在接触丰富操作任务中面临复杂接触动力学带来的高精度力反馈与控制挑战，尽管已有工作尝试将力/力矩传感集成到策略中，但如何在多模态观测下构建简单有效的框架、实现鲁棒泛化仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出ForceFlow框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 接触阶段策略设计——采用非对称多模态融合架构将力作为全局调节信号，结合联合预测范式增强对瞬时力与历史信息的理解；(2) 任务级层次分解——将操作分为视觉主导的接近阶段（基于VLM的指向目标定位）和触觉主导的交互阶段（力驱动接触执行），并通过Vision-to-Force (V2F)切换机制显式解耦空间泛化与接触调节。

Result: 在六类真实接触丰富操作任务中，ForceFlow相比强基线ForceVLA实现37%的成功率提升，同时保持显著更低的成本。此外，框架展现出精确的力信号预测能力，在接触力自调节和零样本分布外泛化方面表现优异。

Conclusion: ForceFlow通过非对称多模态力融合与视觉-触觉两阶段解耦策略，有效解决了接触丰富操作中力-运动深度耦合与泛化难题，证明了力作为全局调节信号和任务层次分解在提升操作鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Existing imitation learning methods enable robots to interact autonomously with the physical environment. However, contact-rich manipulation tasks remain a significant challenge due to complex contact dynamics that demand high-precision force feedback and control. Although recent efforts have attempted to integrate force/torque sensing into policies, how to build a simple yet effective framework that achieves robust generalization under multimodal observations remains an open question. In this paper, we propose ForceFlow, a force-aware reactive framework built upon flow matching. For contact-stage policy design, we investigate force signal fusion mechanisms and adopt an asymmetric multimodal fusion architecture that treats force as a global regulatory signal, combined with a joint prediction paradigm that enhances the policy's understanding of instantaneous force and historical information, thereby achieving deep coupling between force and motion. For task-level hierarchical decomposition, we divide manipulation into a vision-dominant approach stage (VLM-based pointing for target localization) and a touch-dominant interaction stage (force-driven contact execution), with a Vision-to-Force (V2F) handover mechanism that explicitly decouples spatial generalization from contact regulation. Experimental results across six real-world contact-rich tasks demonstrate that ForceFlow achieves a 37% success rate improvement over the strong baseline ForceVLA while maintaining significantly lower cost. Moreover, ForceFlow exhibits accurate force signal prediction and demonstrates superior performance in contact force self-regulation and zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization.

</details>


### [4] [Cooperative Robotics Reinforced by Collective Perception for Traffic Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11972)
*Mohammad Khoshkdahan,John Pravin Arockiasamy,Andy Flores Comeca,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协作式人形机器人作为主动交通调节者的概念，通过双摄像头基础设施单元和V2X通信实现非视距交叉口的安全防护，机器人可发出类似人类的停止手势并物理阻挡危险合并行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非视距交叉口因驾驶员视野受限导致事故频发，现有V2X警告和集体感知方案受限于低渗透率和驾驶员忽视问题，无法有效影响未联网车辆。

Method: 系统包含双摄像头基础设施单元（检测车辆位置、速度、运动）和搭载V2X的机器人。采用集体感知消息、协作感知消息和分散式环境通知消息三种通信机制，通过融合模块构建实时路况视图，定义危险区域预测碰撞风险，机器人执行人类化停止手势并物理阻断。

Result: 在鹿特丹未来移动公园的实测表明，视觉与V2X融合感知能使机器人提前检测接近车辆，可靠预测危险并防止真实NLOS条件下的不安全合流。

Conclusion: 该机器人方案弥补了集体感知无法影响未联网车辆的空白，为低V2X渗透率场景下的交叉口安全提供了有效的物理干预手段。

Abstract: Collisions at non-line-of-sight (NLOS) intersections remain a major safety concern because drivers have limited visibility of approaching traffic. V2X based warnings can reduce these risks, yet many vehicles are not equipped with V2X and drivers may ignore in vehicle alerts. Collective perception (CP) can compensate for low V2X penetration by extending the awareness of connected vehicles, but it cannot influence unconnected vehicles. To fill this gap, our work introduces a complementary concept that adds a cooperative humanoid robot as an active traffic moderator capable of physically stopping a vehicle that attempts to merge into an unseen traffic stream. The system operates on two parallel perception pathways. A dual camera infrastructure unit detects the position, speed and motion of approaching vehicles and transmits this information to the robot as a collective perception message (CPM). The robot also receives cooperative awareness messages (CAM) from connected vehicles through its onboard V2X unit and can act as a relay for decentralized environmental notification messages (DENM) when safety events originate elsewhere along the road. A fusion module combines these streams to maintain a robust real time view of the main road. A Zone of Danger (ZoD) is defined and used to predict whether an approaching vehicle creates a collision risk for a merging road user. When such a risk is detected, the robot issues a human-like STOP gesture and blocks the merging path until the hazard disappears. The full system was deployed at the Future Mobility Park (FMP) in Rotterdam. Experiments show that the combined vision and V2X perception allows the robot to detect approaching vehicles early, predict hazards reliably and prevent unsafe merges in real world NLOS conditions.

</details>


### [5] [SEVO: Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation for Robust VLA Manipulation via Active Illumination and Data-Centric Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11114)
*Tianchonghui Fang,Yuan Zhuang,Fei Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEVO是一种数据驱动的视觉增强方法，通过体固相机、红光照明和YOLO语义分割叠加三个机制改进观测质量，结合多样化数据采集协议，在不修改策略架构的情况下，将低成本机械臂在跨环境操作任务中的抓取成功率从30-35%提升至75-85%，验证了观测设计与数据多样性比模型缩放更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA和模仿学习策略在实际部署中面临严重的跨环境泛化失败问题：在受控实验室环境中成功率很高，但迁移到新环境时性能急剧下降（从75%降至30-35%）。社区实践者报告了近零的迁移表现，亟需一种不依赖模型缩放的低成本解决方案。

Method: SEVO包含三大观测机制：(1)体固相机阵列覆盖完整操作空间；(2)红色光谱主动光照归一化物体外观；(3)实时YOLO分割叠加提供背景不变的语义线索。核心在于多样化数据采集协议：系统性地改变光照、背景和干扰物进行遥操作数据收集。目标为透明水瓶等视觉挑战性物体，在两种移动平台上进行数百次真实机器人实验。

Result: 在训练环境中，ACT策略达到95%抓取成功率，SmolVLA达到83%；迁移到新环境时分别保持85%和75%。而不使用SEVO时，相同策略在训练环境中仅为75%/70%，在新环境中骤降至30-35%。

Conclusion: 精心设计的观测系统与数据采集多样性，而非模型参数规模扩展，才是实现低成本机器人在日常家庭环境中可靠运行的关键因素。SEVO的方法论适用于已有的各种VLA策略架构，具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and imitation-learning policies trained via community toolchains on low-cost hardware frequently fail when deployed outside the training environment. Existing evaluations, including the original ACT and SmolVLA benchmarks, demonstrate high success rates under controlled, fixed backgrounds, yet community practitioners report near-zero transfer to new environments. We present SEVO (Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation), a data-centric approach that improves cross-environment manipulation robustness without modifying the policy architecture. SEVO transforms the raw RGB camera stream through three mechanisms: (1) body-fixed cameras whose combined fields of view cover the full manipulation workspace, (2) active red-spectrum illumination that physically normalizes object appearance, and (3) real-time YOLO segmentation overlay that provides a background-invariant semantic cue. Critically, we show that a diversified data collection protocol (systematically varying lighting, backgrounds, and distractors during teleoperation) is the single most important factor for generalization. We target transparent water bottles, objects that visually blend with their surroundings, and select a simple pick-and-place task to enable hundreds of controlled real-robot trials across two mobile platforms. The full pipeline achieves 95% grasp success with ACT and 83% with SmolVLA in the training environment, transferring to novel environments at 85% and 75%. Without SEVO, the same policies achieve only 75%/70% in training and collapse to 30-35% in novel environments. Our results demonstrate that principled observation design and environmental diversity during data collection, not model scaling, enable low-cost robots to operate reliably in everyday household environments.

</details>


### [6] [Computational Design of a Low-Visibility UAV Using a Human-Aligned Perceptual Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11296)
*Jingxian Wang,Chen Yu,David Matthews,Emma Alexander,Sam Kriegman,Michael Rubenstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Phantom Twist——一种通过高速旋转和运动模糊实现低视觉可见性的单桨无人机，并开发了两阶段自动化设计流程来优化组件布局，最终通过实物制造和飞行测试验证了其稳定性和相比传统四旋翼显著降低的视觉感知度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼无人机视觉特征明显，容易被察觉，限制了其在需要隐蔽性的应用场景中的使用。本文旨在通过高速旋转产生运动模糊来降低无人机的视觉可见性。

Method: 提出一种两阶段自动化设计流程，优化电池、控制PCB、电机-螺旋桨组件和配重等功能组件的布局。优化目标是最小化人类对齐感知指标（LPIPS）衡量的可见性，同时严格满足稳定飞行所需的惯性和气动约束。

Result: 通过实物制造和多个原型飞行测试，验证了设计流程能产生稳定可控的无人机，且优化后的无人机相比传统四旋翼具有显著降低的视觉可感知性。

Conclusion: Phantom Twist通过高速旋转和运动模糊策略结合自动化优化设计，成功实现了低视觉可见性的单桨无人机，为隐蔽飞行器设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Phantom Twist, a type of single-propeller UAV designed to achieve low visibility through high-speed spinning and the exploitation of motion blur. We develop a two-stage automated design pipeline that optimizes the placement of functional components including batteries, control PCB, motor-propeller assembly, and counterweights. The pipeline minimizes visibility as measured by a human-aligned perceptual metric (LPIPS) while strictly satisfying inertial and aerodynamic constraints required for stable flight. We validate this approach through fabrication and flight testing of multiple prototypes. These tests confirm that our pipeline produces stable, controllable designs and that the optimized UAV exhibits significantly reduced visual perceptibility compared to conventional quadcopters.

</details>


### [7] [ASIP-Planner: Adaptive Planning for UAV Surface Inspection in Partially Known Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11119)
*Hanyu Jin,Zhefan Xu,Haoyu Shen,Xinming Han,Kanlong Ye,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向部分已知结构化室内环境的自适应无人机检测框架，通过基于分段的全局覆盖规划器与面向检测的局部视角自适应模块相结合，在存在未预见障碍物时维持近完全覆盖并缩短航迹长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内基础设施检测（如隧道、工业设施）依赖先验地图进行无人机覆盖规划，但实际环境中临时结构或设备等未预见障碍物会导致视点遮挡，降低检测质量。现有覆盖规划方法假设环境完全已知，无法适应执行中的环境差异。

Method: 提出自适应无人机检测框架：(1) 全局规划器将平面检测目标按表面对齐聚类，生成朝向一致性更好的紧致视点序列；(2) 局部规划器在线生成无碰撞轨迹并调整观测方向，在保持规划轨迹结构的同时缓解遮挡导致的覆盖损失。

Result: 随机场景仿真表明全局规划器在实现近完全覆盖的同时航迹长度优于代表性基线；真实飞行实验验证了框架可生成可用于下游分析的检测数据。

Conclusion: 所提框架在部分已知的结构化室内环境中提升了检测效率与适应性。

Abstract: Indoor infrastructure inspection, such as tunnels and industrial facilities, requires systematic surface coverage to ensure that all inspection targets are properly observed. Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer an alternative to manual inspection by conducting map-guided surface inspection using prior structural models. However, in practice, indoor inspection often relies on floorplan-derived reference maps that may not reflect unforeseen obstacles, such as temporary structures or equipment, leading to occluded viewpoints and degraded inspection quality. Existing coverage planning methods typically assume a fully known inspection environment and perform deterministic global viewpoint optimization based on accurate prior maps, making them vulnerable to environmental discrepancies during execution. This work presents an adaptive UAV inspection framework for partially known structured indoor environments. The proposed method integrates a segment-based global coverage planner with an inspection-oriented local view-angle adaptation module. The global planner organizes planar inspection targets into surface-aligned clusters to generate compact viewpoint sequences with improved orientation consistency. The local planner generates collision-free trajectories and adjusts the viewing direction online to mitigate occlusion-induced coverage loss while preserving the planned trajectory structure. The simulation results across randomized scene configurations demonstrate that the proposed global planner achieves near-complete coverage while reducing trajectory length compared to representative baselines. Real-world flight experiments further validate that the framework produces usable inspection data for downstream analysis. These results indicate that the proposed framework improves inspection efficiency and adaptability in partially known structured indoor environments.

</details>


### [8] [Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting for Predictive 3D Representation in Language-Guided Pick-and-Place Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11144)
*Kaixin Jia,Jiacheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为预测性高斯泼溅（Forecast-GS）的预测性3D表示框架，用于语言条件的机器人操作。该框架通过显式建模任务完成状态，在真实世界的拾放操作任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作系统的局限在于它们通常仅基于当前场景进行推理，并未显式建模任务完成后的状态。当成功依赖于在部分观测条件下满足空间和语义目标时，机器人需要评估候选动作是否能导致可行的、与任务一致的结果，现有方法缺乏这种预测能力。

Method: Forecast-GS框架通过语言指令引导，预测任务完成后的3D高斯溅射表示，从而显式建模任务完成状态，并基于该预测进行动作评估和候选选择。

Result: 在Cutter-to-Box、Apple-to-Bowl和Sponge-to-Tray三个真实世界拾放任务上，Forecast-GS自动候选选择分别达到21/25、23/25和16/25的成功率，优于ReKep基线的15/25、19/25和10/25。人工辅助设置进一步提升至23/25、24/25和19/25。

Conclusion: 显式预测任务完成后的3D状态能够实现更可靠的动作评估；自动与人工辅助选择之间的差距表明鲁棒的最终状态排序仍是全自主操作的重要挑战。Forecast-GS在语言理解、3D感知和机器人操作规划之间提供了可解释的桥梁。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting (Forecast-GS), a predictive 3D representation framework for language-conditioned robotic manipulation. While recent manipulation systems have made progress by grounding language instructions into robot affordances, value maps, or relational keypoint constraints, they usually reason over the current scene and do not explicitly model the task-completed state. This limitation is critical when success depends on satisfying spatial and semantic goals under partial observations, where the robot must evaluate whether a candidate action leads to a feasible task-consistent outcome.
  We validate Forecast-GS on real-world pick-and-place manipulation tasks, including Cutter-to-Box, Apple-to-Bowl, and Sponge-to-Tray. For each task, we conduct 25 real-world trials under varied initial object configurations using the same robot platform and sensing setup. Forecast-GS with automatic candidate selection achieves success rates of 21/25, 23/25, and 16/25 on the three tasks, respectively, outperforming the ReKep baseline, which achieves 15/25, 19/25, and 10/25. A diagnostic human-assisted setting further improves success rates to 23/25, 24/25, and 19/25, suggesting that candidate generation is effective while automatic ranking remains imperfect. These results suggest that explicitly forecasting task-completed 3D states enables more reliable action evaluation, while the gap between automatic and human-assisted selection indicates that robust final-state ranking remains an important challenge for fully autonomous manipulation. Overall, Forecast-GS provides an interpretable bridge between language understanding, 3D perception, and robotic manipulation planning.

</details>


### [9] [Distributed Pose Graph Optimization via Continuous Riemannian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11210)
*Jaeho Shin,Maani Ghaffari,Yulun Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李群上二阶连续时间动力系统的分布式位姿图优化（PGO）框架，通过将位姿变量建模为受阻尼的大质量粒子，利用阻尼欧拉-泊松方程和半隐式几何积分器设计优化算法，在分布式多机器人场景下实现低通信开销的同步和异步优化，理论保证了能量耗散，实验优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式PGO方法在同步/异步通信下收敛速度慢、通信开销高，缺乏统一的几何框架来统一现有算法（如黎曼梯度下降和高斯-牛顿），并缺乏对延迟通信下邻居预测的理论保证。

Method: 将PGO问题建模为李群上的二阶阻尼动力学系统，使用阻尼欧拉-泊松方程进行描述；采用半隐式几何积分器进行离散化；通过块对角质量矩阵和阻尼矩阵实现完全分布式并行计算；利用状态和速度建模实现基于物理的邻居预测以处理通信延迟。

Result: 在基准PGO数据集上，所提方法在同步和异步两种模式下均显著优于现有最先进的分布式基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于几何动力学的框架不仅统一了多种现有优化算法，还通过物理启发的预测机制有效缓解了延迟通信对分布式优化的影响，并提供能量耗散的理论保证，为分布式PGO提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present a framework for distributed Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) by formulating the problem as a second-order continuous-time dynamical system evolving on Lie groups. By modeling pose variables as massive particles subject to damping, the equilibrium points of the resulting Riemannian dynamics coincide with first-order critical points of the original PGO problem. Using the governing damped Euler--Poincaré equations and a semi-implicit geometric integrator, we design an optimization algorithm that generalizes existing algorithms such as Riemannian gradient descent and Gauss--Newton. In multi-robot settings, we present a fully distributed and parallel method based on block-diagonal mass and damping matrices, where each robot solves an ordinary differential equation for its own poses with minimal communication overhead. Moreover, modeling both state and velocity enables principled neighbor prediction that significantly improves convergence under delayed communication. Theoretically, we present an analysis and establish sufficient condition that ensures energy dissipation under the employed geometric discretization scheme. Experiments on benchmark PGO datasets demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art distributed baselines in both synchronous and asynchronous regimes.

</details>


### [10] [Kairos: A Scalable Serving System for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11381)
*Yinwei Dai,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Landon Cox,Xenofon Foukas,Bozidar Radunovic,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Kairos是首个面向物理AI的多机器人服务系统，通过将生成-执行循环作为一等公民并积极参与执行阶段，相比现有数字AI服务方案，将端到端任务延迟降低31.8%-66.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI的推理特性（多轮推理与动作执行交错、每轮生成动作块、异步交织执行）与数字AI截然不同，现有数字AI服务系统无法满足其需求，阻碍了物理AI的广泛采用。

Method: 提出Kairos系统，将生成-执行循环（generate-execute loop）视为一等公民，使系统在执行阶段主动参与，实现推理与执行的高效异步交织。

Result: 在多种物理AI模型和机器人上，相比最先进的数字AI服务方案，平均端到端任务延迟降低31.8%-66.5%，且收益随机器人集群规模扩大而增加。

Conclusion: 物理AI需要专门的推理服务系统，Kairos通过将执行阶段纳入系统设计，有效解决了多机器人场景下的延迟问题，且其优势随规模扩展而放大。

Abstract: Physical AI is experiencing rapid growth with frontier foundation models increasing its capabilities across general environments. Physical AI tasks are characterized by inference properties that are markedly different from digital AI. They consist of multiple rounds of inference and action execution, generating a chunk of actions in each inference round, and asynchronously interleaving inference and execution. This makes existing digital AI serving systems unsuited for physical AI; a shortcoming that is critical for enabling their wide adoption, considering their size and the scale of the robot fleets they have to serve. To fill this gap, we design Kairos, the first multi-robot serving system that makes the generate-execute loop a first-class citizen, with active involvement in the execution phase. Across a wide range of physical AI models and robots, Kairos reduces the average end-to-end task latency by 31.8--66.5% over state-of-the-art digital AI serving practices, with gains scaling with the robot fleet size.

</details>


### [11] [Control of Fully Actuated Aerial Vehicles: A Comparison of Model-based and Sensor-based Dynamic Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12071)
*Ali Sidar Yilmaz,Buday Turan,Lukas Pries,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次在固定倾角全驱动六旋翼上实验验证了几何非线性动态逆（NDI）与增量式非线性动态逆（INDI）两种控制策略。在相同外环结构和执行频率下，INDI在参数失配、阵风扰动、传感器退化及较低控制频率的位置跟踪方面更具优势，但几何NDI在低控制频率下的姿态跟踪更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全驱动多旋翼平台解耦了平推力生成与飞行器姿态，使性能限制从姿态权限转向执行器动力学与控制效能，但不同反演策略（基于模型与基于测量）间的性能权衡尚未在真实全驱动平台上得到系统实验验证。

Method: 在固定倾角全驱动六旋翼上，保持相同外环结构及500 Hz执行频率，对比基于模型的几何NDI和基于传感器的INDI两种控制器。设计五项实验：标称条件下姿态阶跃跟踪、转子力系数50%失配、外部侧向载荷悬停抗扰、阵风扰动下航点跟踪、控制频率降低及注入传感器退化。

Result: INDI在参数失配、阵风扰动和传感器退化下优势明显，在控制频率扫描中保持更低的位置误差；但几何NDI在降低控制频率时姿态跟踪性能更优。

Conclusion: INDI与几何NDI间存在权衡：基于测量的INDI对模型不确定性和干扰更具鲁棒性，而基于模型的NDI在低控制频率下姿态跟踪更好。这是首次对具有解耦平动/转动动力学的全姿态跟踪INDI控制器进行实验验证，为全驱动无人机鲁棒控制与快速部署提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Fully actuated multirotor platforms decouple translational force generation from vehicle attitude, enabling independent control of position and orientation and shifting performance limitations from attitude authority to actuator dynamics and control effectiveness. This paper compares a model-based nonlinear dynamic inversion controller (geometric NDI) with a sensor-based incremental dynamic inversion controller (INDI) on a fixed-tilt fully actuated hexarotor. Both controllers share an identical outer-loop structure and are both executed at 500 Hz; therefore, performance differences can be attributed primarily to the inversion strategy. Controller performance is evaluated in five experiments covering attitude step tracking under nominal conditions and under a 50% mismatch in the rotor force coefficient, hover disturbance rejection under an external lateral load, waypoint tracking in the presence of wind gust disturbances, reduced control frequency, and injected sensor degradation. The results show that INDI offers clear advantages under parameter mismatch, gust disturbances, and sensor degradation, and maintains lower position errors across the controller-frequency sweep. However, its advantages are not universal: geometric NDI yields better attitude tracking at reduced control frequencies. To the authors' best knowledge, this work presents the first experimental validation of a full pose tracking INDI controller with decoupled translational and rotational dynamics. These findings highlight the trade-off between measurement-based and model-based inversion for robust control and rapid deployment of fully actuated UAVs.

</details>


### [12] [Overcoming Dynamics-Blindness: Training-Free Pace-and-Path Correction for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11459)
*Yanyan Zhang,Chaoda Song,Vikash Singh,Xinpeng Li,Kai Ye,Zhe Hu,Zhongzhu Pu,Yu Yin,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Pace-and-Path Correction，一种无需训练的闭式推理时操作符，通过正交分解为节奏通道和路径通道，在单步二次代价优化下动态校正分块动作VLA模型，在动态环境基准测试中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流VLA模型基于单帧观测训练，对时间动态结构敏感，在非平稳场景下性能严重退化。现有方法要么需要昂贵重训练，要么存在延迟瓶颈和时序不一致问题。

Method: 提出训练无关、闭式推理时操作符Pace-and-Path Correction。从单一二次代价函数出发，联合最小化得到正交分解解：节奏通道沿计划方向压缩执行，路径通道施加正交空间偏移，共同吸收分块窗口内的感知动态。

Result: 在MoveBench基准上，该方法在纯动态环境中成功率达28.8%，在静动态混合环境中达25.9%，优于现有训练无关方法和动态自适应方法。

Conclusion: 训练无关的推理时校正可有效解决VLA模型在动态场景下的时序盲区问题，避免重训练代价的同时实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve remarkable flexibility and generalization beyond classical control paradigms. However, most prevailing VLAs are trained under a single-frame observation paradigm, which leaves them structurally blind to temporal dynamics. Consequently, these models degrade severely in non-stationary scenarios, even when trained or finetuned on dynamic datasets. Existing approaches either require expensive retraining or suffer from latency bottlenecks and poor temporal consistency across action chunks. We propose Pace-and-Path Correction, a training-free, closed-form inference-time operator that wraps any chunked-action VLA. From a single quadratic cost, joint minimization yields a unified solution that decomposes orthogonally into two distinct channels. The pace channel compresses execution along the planned direction, while the path channel applies an orthogonal spatial offset, jointly absorbing the perceived dynamics within the chunk window. We evaluate our approach on a comprehensive diagnostic benchmark MoveBench designed to isolate motion as the sole controlled variable. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-free wrappers and dynamic-adaptive methods and improves success rates by up to 28.8% and 25.9% in absolute terms over foundational VLA models in dynamic-only and static-dynamic mixed environments, respectively.

</details>


### [13] [Learning What Matters: Adaptive Information-Theoretic Objectives for Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12084)
*Youwei Yu,Jionghao Wang,Zhengming Yu,Wenping Wang,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出QOED方法，通过Fisher信息矩阵的特征空间分析识别可观测子空间和可辨识参数方向，并修改探索目标以强调这些方向同时抑制非关键参数的干扰效应，在仿真和真实世界的导航与操作任务中分别提升35.23%和21.98%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计可学习的信息论目标进行机器人探索仍具挑战性，因为难以确定收集的数据能揭示哪些信息；高维机器人系统中许多参数方向弱可观测或不可辨识，即使选择可辨识方向，被忽略的方向仍会影响探索并扭曲信息度量。

Method: QOED方法：(i) 对Fisher信息矩阵进行特征空间分析以识别可观测子空间和可辨识参数方向；(ii) 修改探索目标以强调这些方向同时抑制非关键参数的干扰效应；在有限干扰和关键与非关键方向耦合受限的条件下，提供理想信息目标的常数因子近似。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的导航与操作任务中，可辨识方向选择带来35.23%的性能提升，干扰抑制带来21.98%的性能提升；作为模型策略优化的探索目标时，QOED进一步提升了策略性能，超越已有的RL基线。

Conclusion: QOED通过可辨识方向选择和干扰抑制有效提升了机器人探索效率，在导航和操作任务中表现出显著性能改进，并能在模型策略优化中超越已有RL基线。

Abstract: Designing learnable information-theoretic objectives for robot exploration remains challenging. Such objectives aim to guide exploration toward data that reduces uncertainty in model parameters, yet it is often unclear what information the collected data can actually reveal. Although reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize a given objective, constructing objectives that reflect parametric learnability is difficult in high-dimensional robotic systems. Many parameter directions are weakly observable or unidentifiable, and even when identifiable directions are selected, omitted directions can still influence exploration and distort information measures. To address this challenge, we propose Quasi-Optimal Experimental Design (Q{\footnotesize OED}), an adaptive information objective grounded in optimal experimental design. Q{\footnotesize OED} (i) performs eigenspace analysis of the Fisher information matrix to identify an observable subspace and select identifiable parameter directions, and (ii) modifies the exploration objective to emphasize these directions while suppressing nuisance effects from non-critical parameters. Under bounded nuisance influence and limited coupling between critical and nuisance directions, Q{\footnotesize OED} provides a constant-factor approximation to the ideal information objective that explores all parameters. We evaluate Q{\footnotesize OED} on simulated and real-world navigation and manipulation tasks, where identifiable-direction selection and nuisance suppression yield performance improvements of \SI{35.23}{\percent} and \SI{21.98}{\percent}, respectively. When integrated as an exploration objective in model-based policy optimization, Q{\footnotesize OED} further improves policy performance over established RL baselines.

</details>


### [14] [Offline Policy Evaluation for Manipulation Policies via Discounted Liveness Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11479)
*Hao Wang,Joshua Bowden,Colton Crosby,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于活性的Bellman算子框架，用于从稀疏奖励中进行离线策略评估，通过保守的不动点价值函数有效缓解有限视界截断偏差，并在模拟和真实操作任务中优于TD(0)和蒙特卡洛等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代操控系统中的策略评估面临奖励稀疏、任务进展非单调（因恢复行为）以及评估轨迹有限长带来的截断偏差问题，有限视界破坏了基于贝尔曼方程/最优性原理的无限视界假设，标准方法不再适用。

Method: 将策略评估建模为任务完成问题，提出基于活性的贝尔曼算子（liveness-based Bellman operator），推导保守的不动点价值函数，该函数对有限视界截断具有鲁棒性；给出了算子的理论性质（包括压缩保证），并展示了其如何编码任务进展同时缓解截断偏差。

Result: 在两项模拟操控任务（使用VLA模型和扩散策略）以及一项布料折叠任务（使用人类示教）上进行了评估，结果表明所提方法更准确地反映任务进展，显著减少了截断偏差，性能优于TD(0)和蒙特卡洛策略评估等经典基线。

Conclusion: 基于活性的贝尔曼算子框架能有效解决有限视界下稀疏奖励策略评估中的截断偏差问题，为机器人策略评估提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Policy evaluation is a fundamental component of the development and deployment pipeline for robotic policies. In modern manipulation systems, this problem is particularly challenging: rewards are often sparse, task progression of evaluation rollouts are often non-monotonic as the policies exhibit recovery behaviors, and evaluation rollouts are necessarily of finite length. This finite length introduces truncation bias, breaking the infinite-horizon assumptions underlying standard methods relying on Bellman equations/principle of optimality. In this work, we propose a framework for offline policy evaluation from sparse rewards based on a liveness-based Bellman operator. Our formulation interprets policy evaluation as a task-completion problem and yields a conservative fixed-point value function that is robust to finite-horizon truncation. We analyze the theoretical properties of the proposed operator, including contraction guarantees, and show how it encodes task progression while mitigating truncation bias. We evaluate our method on two simulated manipulation tasks using both a Vision-Language-Action model and a diffusion policy, and a cloth folding task using human demonstrations. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach more accurately reflects task progress and substantially reduces truncation bias, outperforming classical baselines such as TD(0) and Monte Carlo policy evaluation.

</details>


### [15] [Coordinated Diffusion: Generating Multi-Agent Behavior Without Multi-Agent Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11485)
*Lasse Peters,Laura Ferranti,Javier Alonso-Mora,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoDi框架，通过耦合独立训练的单智能体扩散策略（无需多智能体示范数据），利用用户定义的成本函数实现多智能体协调行为，并在理论和实验上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体模仿学习面临数据瓶颈：联合状态-动作空间随智能体数量指数增长，收集协调的多智能体示范数据成本极高。探索如何利用易获取的单智能体示范数据学习多智能体策略。

Method: 提出Coordinated Diffusion (CoDi)框架，将独立预训练的单智能体扩散策略通过用户定义的多智能体成本函数耦合。推导新的扩散采样方案：得分函数分解为独立单智能体预训练基础策略 + 成本驱动的引导项（无梯度估计，适用于黑箱/不可微成本函数）。

Result: 仿真和双机械臂操作实验表明：CoDi能从单智能体数据中发现鲁棒的协调行为，比多智能体基线更数据高效；揭示了联合引导、基础策略支持和成本设计的重要性。

Conclusion: 示范数据与成本函数存在互补作用：单智能体示范需覆盖目标多智能体行为的支撑集，成本函数需从单策略乘积空间中促进期望行为。CoDi为无需多智能体示范的协调策略学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Imitation learning powered by generative models has proven effective for modeling complex single-agent behaviors. However, teaching multi-agent systems, like multiple arms or vehicles, to coordinate through imitation learning is hindered by a fundamental data bottleneck: as the joint state-action space grows exponentially with the number of agents, collecting a sufficient amount of coordinated multi-agent demonstrations becomes extremely costly. In this work, we ask: how can we leverage single-agent demonstration data to learn multi-agent policies? We present Coordinated Diffusion (CoDi), a framework that couples independently trained single-agent diffusion policies through a user-defined multi-agent cost function, without requiring any coordinated demonstrations. We derive a new diffusion-based sampling scheme wherein the diffusion score function decomposes into independent, single-agent pre-trained base policies plus a cost-driven guidance term that coordinates these base policies into cohesive multi-agent behavior. We show that this guidance term can be estimated in a gradient-free manner, making CoDi applicable to black-box, non-differentiable cost functions without additional training. Theoretically and empirically, we analyze the conditions under which this composition can faithfully approximate a target multi-agent behavior. We find a complementary role for demonstration data versus the cost function: single-agent demonstrations must cover the support of the desired multi-agent behavior, while the cost function must promote desired behavior from this product of single-agent policies. Our results in simulation and hardware experiments of a two-arm manipulation task show that CoDi discovers robust coordinated behavior from single-agent data, is more data-efficient than multi-agent baselines, and highlights the importance of joint guidance, base policy support, and cost design.

</details>


### [16] [RIO: Flexible Real-Time Robot I/O for Cross-Embodiment Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11564)
*Pablo Ortega-Kral,Eliot Xing,Arthur Bucker,Vernon Luk,Junseo Kim,Owen Kwon,Angchen Xie,Nikhil Sobanbabu,Yifu Yuan,Megan Lee,Deepam Ameria,Bhaswanth Ayapilla,Jaycie Bussell,Guanya Shi,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RIO是一个开源Python框架，提供灵活轻量的组件用于机器人控制、遥操作、数据格式化、传感器配置和策略部署，支持多种硬件平台和形态（单臂、双臂、人形），验证了VLA部署流程，并公开了所有代码和细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多任务、多形态的机器人基础模型研究受限于碎片化的基础设施，大多数机器人代码高度特定于用户设定的具体配置，导致复用、回收或共享代码的开销巨大，通用跨形态机器人能力难以实现。

Method: 设计并实现了RIO框架，提供抽象层以支持用户自由选择并轻松切换不同组件（控制、遥操作、数据格式化、传感器配置、策略部署），并在三种形态（单臂、双臂、人形）和四种硬件平台上验证VLA部署工作流。

Result: 使用RIO收集的遥操作数据，成功微调了π_{0.5}和GR00T等最先进VLA模型，在拾放、折叠、刷碗等家务任务上取得验证，展示了跨形态和硬件的泛化能力。

Conclusion: RIO通过开源框架降低了跨形态机器人研究的工程开销，有望加速社区在真实机器人硬件上的学习研究进展。

Abstract: Despite recent efforts to collect multi-task, multi-embodiment datasets, to design recipes for training Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs), and to showcase these models on different robot platforms, generalist cross-embodiment robot capabilities remains a largely elusive ideal. Progress is limited by fragmented infrastructure: most robot code is highly specific to the exact setup the user decided on, which adds major overhead when attempting to reuse, recycle, or share artifacts between users. We present RIO (Robot I/O), an open source Python framework that provides flexible, lightweight components for robot control, teleoperation, data formatting, sensor configuration, and policy deployment across diverse hardware platforms and morphologies. RIO provides abstractions that enable users to make any choice and to switch between them, with minimal reconfiguration effort. We validate RIO on VLA deployment workflows across three morphologies (single-arm, bimanual, humanoid) and four hardware platforms with varying grippers and cameras. Using teleoperated data collected with RIO, we fine-tune state-of-the-art VLAs including $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T on household tasks such as pick-and-place, folding, and bowl scrubbing. By open sourcing all our efforts, we hope the community can accelerate their pace of robot learning on real-world robot hardware. Additional details at: https://robot-i-o.github.io

</details>


### [17] [Sampling-Based Follow-the-Leader Motion Planning for Manipulator-Mounted Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11618)
*Chengnan Shentu,Nicholas Baldassini,Oluwagbotemi D. Iseoluwa,Radian Gondokaryono,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对机械臂搭载的连续体机器人的跟随引导运动规划器，通过解耦全局形状搜索与基座姿态计算实现高效规划，理论保证分辨率完备性，实验验证零尖端误差与1.9%平均形状偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FTL方法通常假设固定基座或单自由度插入机构，限制了在机械臂搭载CR的实际系统中的应用，需要一种能联合考虑机器人构型和基座姿态的规划方法。

Method: 通过解耦全局形状搜索与基座姿态确定：离线生成候选路径段，在线通过闭式几何构造计算基座姿态（避免迭代优化），支持通用前向模型，将大部分计算移至离线。

Result: 120条仿真路径上实现0%尖端误差、1.9%平均形状偏差（相对于机器人长度）、100%成功率；在6-DOF腱驱动CR安装在串行机械臂上验证实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证理论完备性的同时，有效实现了机械臂搭载CR的FTL运动规划，兼顾了精度与效率。

Abstract: Follow-the-leader (FTL) motion exploits the unique morphology of continuum robots (CRs) to navigate confined spaces by having the body retrace the path of the tip. While extensively studied, existing FTL methods typically assume a fixed base or a single degree-of-freedom insertion mechanism, limiting their applicability to practical systems in which CRs are mounted on robotic manipulators with fully actuated SE(3) base pose. This paper presents a sampling-based motion planner for FTL motion of manipulator-mounted CRs that jointly considers robot configuration and base pose. The key idea is to decouple global shape search from base pose determination by computing the base pose through a closed-form geometric construction, thereby avoiding iterative optimization during online planning. The approach supports general forward models and enables efficient planning by shifting the majority of computation offline. We establish theoretical guarantees including resolution complete shape search and converging tip tracking throughout waypoint traversal and interpolation. Experiments on 120 simulated paths over 3 test classes demonstrate 0% tip error and 1.9% mean shape deviation (w.r.t. robot length) at 100% success rate. We validate the practicality of our approach on a 6-DOF tendon-driven CR mounted on a serial manipulator. Code and visualization available at https://continuumroboticslab.github.io/sb-ftl-cr-planner/.

</details>


### [18] [A Proprioceptive-Only Benchmark for Quadruped State Estimation: ATE, RPE, and Runtime Trade-offs Between Filters and Smoothers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11674)
*Ylenia Nisticò,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Joan Solà,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在GrandTour数据集上对比了三类四足机器人本体状态估计器（MUSE、IEKF、IS），从长期精度（ATE）、短期精度（RPE）和计算时间三个维度给出性能基准，所有评估代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为四足机器人领域的从业者提供三种主流本体状态估计器在统一软硬件平台上的精度与计算开销对比，帮助根据应用约束选择合适的估计器。

Method: 在CYN-1序列上，采用ATE评估长期精度、平移和旋转RPE评估短期精度，并在固定硬件/软件栈上测量每次更新的计算时间，进行公平对比分析。

Result: 三种方法在RPE上表现接近；IEKF和IS的ATE低于MUSE；运行结果凸显了精度与延迟之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为四足机器人状态估计器选择提供了准确性和计算成本的简明快照，所有代码和文档已开源以确保可复现性。

Abstract: We compare three state-of-the-art proprioceptive state estimators for quadruped robots: MUSE [1], the Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF) [2], and the Invariant Smoother (IS) [3], on the CYN-1 sequence of the GrandTour Dataset [4]. Our goal is to give practitioners clear guidance on accuracy and computation time: we report long-term accuracy (Absolute Trajectory Error, ATE), short-term accuracy (translational and rotational Relative Pose Error, RPE), and per-update computation time on a fixed hardware/software stack. On this dataset, RPEs are broadly similar across methods, while IEKF and IS achieve a lower ATE than MUSE. Runtime results highlight the accuracy-latency trade-offs across the three approaches. In the discussion, we outline the evaluation choices used to ensure a fair comparison and analyze factors that influence short-horizon metrics. Overall, this study provides a concise snapshot of accuracy and cost, helping readers choose an estimator that fits their application constraints, with all evaluation code and documentation released open-source at https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/state_estimation_benchmark for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [19] [Rainbow Deep Q-Learning with Kinematics-Aware Design for Cooperative Delta and 3-RRS Parallel Robot Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11697)
*Hassen Nigatu,Gaokun Shi,Jituo Li,Wang Jin,Lu Guodong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Rainbow DQN的运动学感知深度强化学习框架，用于Delta并联机器人与3-RRS并联机构协同 peg-in-hole 操作。通过几何设计优化扩大无奇异工作空间，结合两阶段课程学习，在仿真中实现了稳定的策略收敛和可靠的插入操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Delta并联机器人与3-RRS并联机构协同完成peg-in-hole任务时存在的运动学奇异、工作空间受限以及强化学习探索不安全等问题。

Method: 1) 几何设计优化阶段：优化3-RRS几何参数以最大化无奇异工作空间并改善条件数；2) MDP建模：12维状态向量 + 12离散动作集 + 成形奖励函数；3) 基于Rainbow DQN的两阶段课程学习框架（融合Double Q-learning、Dueling架构、优先回放、多步回报、噪声线性层、分布式价值头）。

Result: 在高保真运动学仿真中，该方法相较于vanilla DQN和经典采样规划器，实现了更稳定的策略收敛、更可靠的插入操作以及更少的约束违反。

Conclusion: 通过几何设计优化与Rainbow DQN的协同设计，有效提升了并联机器人协同装配任务的学习效率与安全性，验证了运动学感知强化学习框架的优越性。

Abstract: This paper presents a kinematics-aware deep reinforcement learning framework based on Rainbow Deep Q-Networks (DQN) for cooperative peg-in-hole manipulation by a Delta parallel robot and a 3-RRS (Revolute--Revolute--Spherical) parallel manipulator. A key contribution is the integration of a geometric design-optimization stage that precedes learning: the 3-RRS geometry is tuned to maximize the singularity-free workspace and improve conditioning, which in turn enlarges the safe region in which the reinforcement learning policy can explore. Together the two manipulators expose a 6~degree-of-freedom (DoF) controllable subspace (three Delta translations, two 3-RRS rotations, and one 3-RRS vertical translation); the peg-in-hole task is invariant to rotation about the peg axis, so the task-relevant manifold is five dimensional. The cooperative insertion problem is cast as a Markov Decision Process with a 12-dimensional state vector and a discrete action set containing $6 \times 2 = 12$ incremental commands (one positive and one negative per controlled DoF). A shaped reward combines dense proximity guidance, penalties for kinematic and workspace violations, and sparse bonuses for successful insertions. The Rainbow DQN -- integrating double Q-learning, dueling architecture, prioritized replay, multi-step returns, noisy linear layers for exploration, and a distributional value head -- is trained with a two-stage curriculum. The co-designed framework is validated in a high-fidelity kinematic simulator, where it achieves stable policy convergence, reliable insertions, and reduced constraint violations compared against a vanilla DQN agent and a classical sampling-based planner.

</details>


### [20] [Introducing Environmental Constraints to Grasping Strategies for Paper-Like Flexible Materials Using a Soft Gripper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11714)
*Yi Dong,Yang Li,Jinjun Duan,Zhendong Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对类纸柔性材料的抓取问题，利用环境约束和通用软体抓手，提出了系统化的抓取策略，建立了力学与运动学模型，并通过实验评估了不同材料与工作条件下的抓取力和成功率，总结了各策略的适用工作空间与特性，为家庭服务机器人抓取平面柔性物体提供了参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类纸材料相较于布料对应力更敏感，物理性质的微小变化会显著影响抓取效果，现有研究缺乏针对该类材料的系统性抓取策略。

Method: 基于已有抓取策略中的操作基元，提出利用环境约束的系统化抓取策略，分析其力学与运动学模型，并构建评估系统测量抓取力与成功率进行实验验证。

Result: 明确了不同策略的特定工作空间和特性，能够满足多样化任务需求。

Conclusion: 所提出的策略可应用于家庭服务机器人抓取平面柔性物体，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of flexible objects is widely required in both industrial and service applications. Among such objects, paper-like materials exhibit distinct mechanical characteristics compared to cloth, being more sensitive to compressive stress, where minor variations in physical properties can significantly affect grasping. This study systematically investigates grasping strategies for paper-like materials using a universal soft gripper by exploiting environmental constraints. Based on manipulation primitives employed in existing grasping strategies, we proposed systematic grasping strategies for flexible materials by exploiting environmental constraints and analyzed their mechanical and kinematic models. To investigate the influence of materials and working conditions on grasping, an evaluation system for measuring grasping force and success rate was defined and experimentally evaluated. Finally, we summarized the specific workspaces and characteristics of different strategies that can satisfy various task requirements and lead to potential applications in household service robots for grasping planar flexible objects.

</details>


### [21] [DreamAvoid: Critical-Phase Test-Time Dreaming to Avoid Failures in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11750)
*Xianzhe Fan,Yuxiang Lu,Shenyuan Gao,Xiaoyang Wu,Ruihua Han,Manling Li,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamAvoid通过测试时"梦境"框架，让VLA模型在精细操作的关键阶段自主预测并避开失败，结合边界学习范式，利用Dream Trigger（关键阶段检测）、Action Proposer（候选动作采样）和Dream Evaluator（短时未来评估）选择最优动作，显著提升操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在精细操作中因缺乏对关键阶段失败的显式感知，微小动作错误会迅速累积为不可逆失败，而现有模型主要依赖成功示范训练，无法应对失败情况。

Method: 提出DreamAvoid框架，包含：(1) Dream Trigger检测是否进入关键阶段；(2) Action Proposer从VLA中采样多个候选动作块；(3) Dream Evaluator（在混合数据上联合训练）"梦境"候选动作的短时未来并评估其价值，选择最优动作。同时引入自主边界学习范式来精炼对成功/失败边界的理解。

Result: 在真实世界操作任务和仿真基准上进行了广泛评估，结果表明DreamAvoid能有效避免失败，提升整体任务成功率。

Conclusion: DreamAvoid通过测试时"梦境"机制和边界学习，有效弥补了VLA模型在关键阶段对失败的感知缺失，显著提高了精细操作的鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often brittle in fine-grained manipulation, where minor action errors during the critical phases can rapidly escalate into irrecoverable failures. Since existing VLA models rely predominantly on successful demonstrations for training, they lack an explicit awareness of failure during these critical phases. To address this, we propose DreamAvoid, a critical-phase test-time dreaming framework that enables VLA models to anticipate and avoid failures. We also introduce an autonomous boundary learning paradigm to refine the system's understanding of the subtle boundary between success and failure. Specifically, we (1) utilize a Dream Trigger to determine whether the execution has entered a critical phase, (2) sample multiple candidate action chunks from the VLA via an Action Proposer, and (3) employ a Dream Evaluator, jointly trained on mixed data (success, failure, and boundary cases), to "dream" the short-horizon futures corresponding to the candidate actions, evaluate their values, and select the optimal action. We conduct extensive evaluations on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks. The results demonstrate that DreamAvoid can effectively avoid failures, thereby improving the overall task success rate. Our code is available at https://github.com/XianzheFan/DreamAvoid.

</details>


### [22] [NavOL: Navigation Policy with Online Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11762)
*Xiaofei Wei,Chun Gu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavOL是一种在线模仿学习范式，通过预训练的导航扩散策略与模拟器交互，在滚动更新循环中收集专家演示并自我更新，无需奖励设计即可高效训练鲁棒的导航策略，在多个仿真基准和真实实验中取得了持续的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线模仿学习存在分布偏移和复合误差问题，强化学习需要奖励工程且学习效率低，因此需要一种无需奖励设计、能缓解分布偏移且学习高效的导航策略学习方法。

Method: 提出NavOL在线模仿学习范式，基于预训练的导航扩散策略，在滚动更新循环中：策略在模拟器中执行并查询具有全局环境特权信息的全局规划器获取最优路径段作为真值标签，再用在线收集的观测-轨迹对训练策略。基于IsaacLab实现快速并行渲染和域随机化，扩展至50个场景、8块RTX 4090 GPU，每小时收集超2000条新轨迹。

Result: NavOL在包括NavDP基准和自行提出的室内视觉导航基准在内的仿真基准以及精心设计的真实世界实验中，均表现出一致的性能提升，证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: NavOL通过在线模仿学习范式，有效解决了离线模仿学习的分布偏移问题和强化学习的奖励设计难题，实现了高效、鲁棒的导航策略学习。

Abstract: Learning robust navigation policies remains a core challenge in robotics. Offline imitation learning suffers from distribution shift and compounding errors at rollout, while reinforcement learning requires reward engineering and learns inefficiently. In this paper, we propose NavOL, an online imitation learning paradigm that interacts with a simulator and updates itself using expert demonstrations gathered online. Built upon a pretrained navigation diffusion policy that maps local observations to future waypoints, NavOL trains in a rollout update loop: during rollout, the policy acts in the simulator and queries a global planner which has privileged access to the global environment for the optimal path segment as ground truth trajectory labels; during update, the policy is trained on the online collected observation trajectory pairs. This online imitation loop removes the need for reward design, improves learning efficiency, and mitigates distribution shift by training on the policy own explored rollouts. Built on IsaacLab with fast, high-fidelity parallel rendering and domain randomization of camera pose and start-goal pairs, our system scales across 50 scenes on 8 RTX 4090 GPUs, collecting over 2,000 new trajectories per hour, each averaging more than 400 steps. We also introduce an indoor visual navigation benchmark with predefined start and goal positions for zero-shot generalization. Extensive evaluations on simulation benchmarks, including the NavDP benchmark and our proposed benchmark, as well as carefully designed real-world experiments, demonstrate the effectiveness of NavOL, showing consistent performance gains in online imitation learning.

</details>


### [23] [See What Matters: Differentiable Grid Sample Pruning for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11817)
*Yixu Feng,Zinan Zhao,Yanxiang Ma,Chenghao Xia,Chengbin Du,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可微分网格采样器(GridS)，通过几何感知的连续token重采样，在保留关键空间信息的同时将视觉token压缩至10%以下，实现76% FLOPs降低且成功率不下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型计算成本高，token剪枝方法在激进压缩时会丢弃接触点等关键几何细节，导致性能严重下降，需要在压缩率和性能之间妥协。

Method: 提出可微分网格采样器(GridS)，作为VLA视觉编码器的即插即用模块，通过自适应预测最小有效坐标集并进行可微分插值特征提取，实现任务感知的连续token重采样。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试和真实机器人平台上，使用不到10%的原始视觉token，FLOPs降低76%且成功率无下降，达到迄今报告的最低可行视觉token数。

Conclusion: 通过几何感知的连续重采样打破了token剪枝中压缩率与性能之间的权衡，实现了高效VLA模型部署。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in robotics manipulation, yet their high computational cost hinders real-time deployment. Existing token pruning methods suffer from a fundamental trade-off: aggressive compression using pruning inevitably discards critical geometric details like contact points, leading to severe performance degradation. This forces a compromise, limiting the achievable compression rate and thus the potential speedup. We argue that breaking this trade-off requires rethinking compression as a geometry-aware, continuous token resampling in the vision encoder. To this end, we propose the Differentiable Grid Sampler (GridS), a plug-and-play module that performs task-aware, continuous resampling of visual tokens in VLA. By adaptively predicting a minimal set of salient coordinates and extracting features via differentiable interpolation, GridS preserves essential spatial information while achieving drastic compression (with fewer than 10% original visual tokens). Experiments on both LIBERO benchmark and a real robotic platform demonstrate that validating the lowest feasible visual token count reported to date, GridS achieves a 76% reduction in FLOPs with no degradation in the success rate. The code is available at https://github.com/Fediory/Grid-Sampler.

</details>


### [24] [Mapping Embodied Affective Touch Strategies on a Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11825)
*Qiaoqiao Ren,Omar Eldardeer,Francesca Cocchella,Rea Francesco,Alessandra Sciutti,Tony Belpaeme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过32名参与者与全身触觉感知机器人iCub的交互实验，发现情感触摸表达强烈依赖于身体区域和物理约束，不同身体部位（自由触摸、仅手臂、仅躯干）在触摸位置、运动特征和压力特征上表现出不同的情感特异性模式，且触摸策略在自由和受限条件下不能直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI研究通常只关注一两个孤立身体部位，限制了对情感触摸如何在全人形机器人身体上泛化的理解。本研究旨在系统探究身体区域和空间约束如何共同塑造情感触摸表达。

Method: 32名参与者与配备全身分布式触觉传感器的iCub机器人交互，在三种条件下（自由触摸、仅手臂触摸、仅躯干触摸）表达八种情感，分析触摸位置和动力学特征。

Result: 身体区域和空间约束共同塑造了触摸位置和动力学；自由触摸中参与者偏好社交可及的上身区域；情感相关变化在仅手臂条件下更体现在运动特征，仅躯干条件下更体现在压力特征；触摸策略在自由和受限条件下不能直接迁移；交互后参与者对机器人的亲近感增加，约30%报告社会关系感知发生变化。

Conclusion: 情感触摸表达强烈依赖于身体区域，并受具身约束塑造，不同身体部位的触摸策略存在差异且不能直接迁移。

Abstract: Affective touch in human-robot interaction is shaped not only by emotional intent, but also by robot embodiment, including touch location, physical constraints, and perceived agency or social role. Existing HRI studies typically focus on one or two isolated body parts, limiting understanding of how affective touch generalises across the full humanoid body. We present a study with 32 participants interacting with the iCub robot, which is equipped with full-body distributed tactile sensors. Participants expressed eight emotions under three conditions: free touch, arm-only touch, and torso-only touch. Results show that body region and spatial constraints jointly shaped both touch location and dynamics. In free touch, participants preferred socially accessible upper-body regions, while less frequently touched areas showed stronger emotion-specific selectivity. Emotion-related variation was more evident in motion features for arm-only touch and pressure features for torso-only touch. Touch strategies also did not transfer directly between free and constrained conditions, even within the same coarse body region. Participants reported increased closeness to the robot after interaction, with around 30 percent reporting a change in perceived social relationship. Together, these findings show that affective touch expression is strongly body-region dependent and shaped by embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [25] [Learning Action Manifold with Multi-view Latent Priors for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11832)
*Junjin Xiao,Dongyang Li,Yandan Yang,Shuang Zeng,Tong Lin,Xinyuan Chang,Feng Xiong,Mu Xu,Xing Wei,Zhiheng Ma,Qing Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对VLA模型中的空间感知和操作挑战，提出多视图扩散模型合成潜在新视角，设计几何引导门控Transformer（G3T）对齐多视图特征并过滤遮挡噪声，以及动作流形学习（AML）直接在有效动作流形上预测动作，在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真机任务上取得了优于SOTA的成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型存在单目输入的深度模糊性和动作学习效率低下的问题，现有方法难以有效处理空间感知和高效动作预测。

Method: 1）利用预训练的多视图扩散模型合成潜在新视角；2）提出Geometry-Guided Gated Transformer (G3T)在3D几何引导下对齐多视图特征并自适应过滤遮挡噪声；3）提出Action Manifold Learning (AML)在有效动作流形上直接预测动作，避免对非结构化目标（如噪声、速度）的低效回归。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真机任务上，所提方法在成功率和鲁棒性上优于现有SOTA基线。

Conclusion: 多视图几何引导与动作流形学习能有效提升VLA模型的空间感知和动作学习能力。

Abstract: This paper tackles spatial perception and manipulation challenges in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. To address depth ambiguity from monocular input, we leverage a pre-trained multi-view diffusion model to synthesize latent novel views and propose a Geometry-Guided Gated Transformer (G3T) that aligns multi-view features under 3D geometric guidance while adaptively filtering occlusion noise. To improve action learning efficiency, we introduce Action Manifold Learning (AML), which directly predicts actions on the valid action manifold, bypassing inefficient regression of unstructured targets like noise or velocity. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-robot tasks show our method achieves superior success rate and robustness over SOTA baselines. Project page: https://junjxiao.github.io/Multi-view-VLA.github.io/.

</details>


### [26] [From Reaction to Anticipation: Proactive Failure Recovery through Agentic Task Graph for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11951)
*Sheng Xu,Ruixing Jin,Huayi Zhou,Bo Yue,Guanren Qiao,Yunxin Tai,Yueci Deng,Kui Jia,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AgentChord是一种受人类预期规划能力启发的智能系统，通过将操作任务建模为有向任务图，并在执行前预先添加预期性恢复分支，实现了对操作失败的即时响应，无需在线重规划，显著提升了成功率和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作框架在处理动态和非结构化环境中的任务失败时，通常采用逐步的检测-推理-恢复流水线，这种反应式规划方式存在高延迟和鲁棒性不足的问题。受人类能够预期并主动规划潜在失败的能力启发，作者旨在构建一个能预先编排恢复策略的系统。

Method: AgentChord将操作任务建模为有向任务图，并通过三类专门智能体协同工作：(1) Composer构建名义任务图；(2) Arranger为图添加预期性恢复分支（anticipatory recovery branches），指定上下文感知的纠正行为；(3) Conductor编译并协调可执行转换，使用低延迟监控器检测偏差并触发预编译的恢复动作，无需在线重规划。

Result: 在多种长时域双臂操作任务上的实验表明，AgentChord显著提高了任务成功率和执行效率。

Conclusion: AgentChord通过事前预期失败并预编译恢复策略的方式，推进了真实机器人系统的可靠性和自主性，为处理操作任务中的不可避免失败提供了一种高效的范式。

Abstract: Although robotic manipulation has made significant progress, reliable execution remains challenging because task failures are inevitable in dynamic and unstructured environments. To handle such failures, existing frameworks typically follow a stepwise detect-reason-recover pipeline, which often incurs high latency and limited robustness due to delayed reasoning and reactive planning. Inspired by the human capability to anticipate and proactively plan for potential failures, we introduce AgentChord, an agentic system that models a manipulation task as a directed task graph. Before execution, this graph is enriched with anticipatory recovery branches that specify context-aware corrective behaviors, enabling immediate and targeted responses when failures occur. Specifically, AgentChord operates through a choreography of specialized agents: a composer that structures the nominal task graph, an arranger that augments the graph with anticipatory recovery branches, and a conductor that compiles and coordinates executable transitions using low-latency monitors to detect deviations and trigger pre-compiled recoveries without re-planning. Empirical studies on diverse long-horizon bimanual manipulation tasks demonstrate that AgentChord substantially improves success rates and execution efficiency, advancing the reliability and autonomy of real-world robotic systems. The project page is available at: https://shengxu.net/AgentChord/.

</details>


### [27] [Closing the Motion Execution Gap: From Semantic Motion Task Constraints to Kinematic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12053)
*Simon Stelter,Vanessa Hassouna,Malte Huerkamp,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Giskard框架，通过Motion Statecharts（可执行符号表示）和统一可微运动学世界模型，弥合高层符号任务描述与可执行机器人运动之间的鸿沟。该框架基于lMPC的任务函数方法实现运动执行，在八种不同机器人平台上验证了跨平台可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Motion Execution Gap，即高层符号任务描述（使用语义约束）与可执行机器人运动之间的脱节问题。

Method: 提出了Motion Statecharts作为可执行的符号表示，支持运动约束、监视器或嵌套状态图在并行和序列中的任意排列；使用统一可微运动学世界模型实现世界中心运动规范和跨本体泛化；通过基于lMPC的任务函数方法实现运动执行，利用加加速度边界确保任务切换时的平滑过渡。

Result: 在八种不同机器人平台（运行于多样化环境）上展示了跨平台可迁移性。

Conclusion: 提出的Giskard框架有效弥合了符号任务与执行运动之间的鸿沟，并具有良好的跨平台通用性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the Motion Execution Gap, the disconnect between high-level symbolic task descriptions using semantic constraints and executable robot motions. Motion Statecharts are introduced as an executable symbolic representation for complex motions. They allow the arbitrary arrangement of motion constraints, monitors or nested statecharts in parallel and sequence. World-centric motion specification and generalization across embodiments are enabled through the use of a unified differentiable kinematic world model of both, robots and environments. Motion execution is realized through a lMPC-based implementation of the task-function approach, in which smooth transitions during task switches are ensured using jerk bounds. Cross-platform transferability was demonstrated by deploying the method on eight robot platforms, operating in diverse environments. The proposed framework is called Giskard and is available open source: https://github.com/cram2/cognitive_robot_abstract_machine.

</details>


### [28] [World Action Models: The Next Frontier in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12090)
*Siyin Wang,Junhao Shi,Zhaoyang Fu,Xinzhe He,Feihong Liu,Chenchen Yang,Yikang Zhou,Zhaoye Fei,Jingjing Gong,Jinlan Fu,Mike Zheng Shou,Xuanjing Huang,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了世界动作模型（WAMs）这一新兴范式，它统一了预测性状态建模与动作生成，从VLA模型发展到联合建模未来状态和动作的分布。文章系统梳理了WAMs的定义、分类（级联式与联合式）、数据生态和评估协议，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型学习从观察到动作的反应式映射，缺乏对物理世界在干预下如何演变的显式建模，而将世界模型集成到动作生成中的工作（即WAMs）虽日益增多，但文献分散于不同架构、学习目标和应用场景，缺乏统一的概念框架。

Method: 通过正式定义WAMs并厘清相关概念，追溯其起源，提出级联式（Cascaded WAMs）和联合式（Joint WAMs）的结构化分类体系，进一步按生成模态、条件机制和动作解码策略细分，并系统分析数据生态（遥操作、便携式人类演示、仿真、互联网规模自我中心视频）和评估协议（视觉保真度、物理常识、动作合理性）。

Result: 首次系统性地综述了WAMs领域，提供了清晰的概念定义和分类学框架，揭示了关键架构范式及其权衡，并识别了开放挑战和未来机遇。

Conclusion: WAMs作为将世界模型与动作生成统一的新范式，在具身智能领域具有重要前景，但目前研究尚处于早期阶段，需要在架构设计、数据利用和评估标准方面进一步探索。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved strong semantic generalization for embodied policy learning, yet they learn reactive observation-to-action mappings without explicitly modeling how the physical world evolves under intervention. A growing body of work addresses this limitation by integrating world models, predictive models of environment dynamics, into the action generation pipeline. We term this emerging paradigm World Action Models (WAMs): embodied foundation models that unify predictive state modeling with action generation, targeting a joint distribution over future states and actions rather than actions alone. However, the literature remains fragmented across architectures, learning objectives, and application scenarios, lacking a unified conceptual framework. We formally define WAMs and disambiguate them from related concepts, and trace the foundations and early integration of VLA and world model research that gave rise to this paradigm. We organize existing methods into a structured taxonomy of Cascaded and Joint WAMs, with further subdivision by generation modality, conditioning mechanism, and action decoding strategy. We systematically analyze the data ecosystem fueling WAMs development, spanning robot teleoperation, portable human demonstrations, simulation, and internet-scale egocentric video, and synthesize emerging evaluation protocols organized around visual fidelity, physical commonsense, and action plausibility. Overall, this survey provides the first systematic account of the WAMs landscape, clarifies key architectural paradigms and their trade-offs, and identifies open challenges and future opportunities for this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [29] [X-Imitator: Spatial-Aware Imitation Learning via Bidirectional Action-Pose Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12162)
*Kai Xiong,Hongjie Fang,Lixin Yang,Cewu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出X-Imitator双路径框架，将空间感知与动作生成建模为双向耦合循环，通过互惠条件预测实现连续相互优化，在24个模拟和3个真实任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将空间感知与动作执行视为解耦或单向过程，限制了机器人掌握复杂操作任务的能力，亟需一种能有效处理两者相互影响的框架。

Method: 提出X-Imitator，一种双路径框架，将空间感知与动作生成建模为紧密耦合的双向循环，通过当前位姿预测与过去动作的互惠条件作用，实现空间推理与动作生成的连续相互优化，并设计为模块化架构可无缝集成到多种视觉运动策略中。

Result: 在24个模拟任务和3个真实世界任务上的实验表明，该框架显著优于原始策略和以往使用显式位姿引导的方法。

Conclusion: X-Imitator通过双向耦合建模有效解决了空间感知与动作生成间的交互问题，为机器人操作任务提供了更强大的解决方案。

Abstract: Effectively handling the interplay between spatial perception and action generation remains a critical bottleneck in robotic manipulation. Existing methods typically treat spatial perception and action execution as decoupled or strictly unidirectional processes, fundamentally restricting a robot's ability to master complex manipulation tasks. To address this, we propose X-Imitator, a versatile dual-path framework that models spatial perception and action execution as a tightly coupled bidirectional loop. By reciprocally conditioning current pose predictions on past actions and vice versa, this framework enables continuous mutual refinement between spatial reasoning and action generation. This joint modeling exactly mimics human internal forward models. Designed as a modular architecture, the system can be seamlessly integrated into various visuomotor policies. Extensive experiments across 24 simulated and 3 real-world tasks demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms both vanilla policies and prior methods utilizing explicit pose guidance. The code will be open sourced.

</details>


### [30] [From Imagined Futures to Executable Actions: Mixture of Latent Actions for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12167)
*Yajie Li,Bozhou Zhang,Chun Gu,Zipei Ma,Jiahui Zhang,Jiankang Deng,Xiatian Zhu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MoLA（混合潜在动作），通过混合预训练逆动力学模型将生成的未来视频转化为可执行的动作表示，解决视频生成模型与机器人控制之间的视觉真实性与控制相关性不匹配问题，在仿真和真实机器人操作任务中取得了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型预测的未来观测过于注重视觉保真度而非控制相关的因果状态转移，导致将预测帧直接用于策略执行时存在间接性和不稳定性，缺乏能够桥接视频想象与策略执行的控制导向接口。

Method: MoLA利用混合预训练逆动力学模型从生成视频中推断混合潜在动作，这些模型分别捕捉语义、深度和光流等互补模态线索，将视觉转换转化为结构化的、物理基础的动作表示。

Result: 在LIBERO、CALVIN、LIBERO-Plus等仿真基准及真实机器人操作任务上，任务成功率、时间一致性和泛化能力均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: MoLA作为一种控制导向的接口，有效弥合了视频想象与策略执行之间的鸿沟，为利用生成模型进行机器人操作提供了一种更稳定、控制相关的表示方式。

Abstract: Video generation models offer a promising imagination mechanism for robot manipulation by predicting long-horizon future observations, but effectively exploiting these imagined futures for action execution remains challenging. Existing approaches either condition policies on predicted frames or directly decode generated videos into actions, both suffering from a mismatch between visual realism and control relevance. As a result, predicted observations emphasize perceptual fidelity rather than action-centric causes of state transitions, leading to indirect and unstable control. To address this gap, we propose MoLA (Mixture of Latent Actions), a control-oriented interface that transforms imagined future videos into executable representations. Instead of passing predicted frames directly to the policy, MoLA leverages a mixture of pretrained inverse dynamics models to infer a mixture of latent actions implied by generated visual transitions. These modality-aware inverse dynamics models capture complementary semantic, depth, and flow cues, providing a structured and physically grounded action representation that bridges video imagination and policy execution. We evaluate our approach on simulated benchmarks (LIBERO, CALVIN, and LIBERO-Plus) and real-world robot manipulation tasks, achieving consistent gains in task success, temporal consistency, and generalization.

</details>


### [31] [DexTwist: Dexterous Hand Retargeting for Twist Motion via Mixed Reality-based Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12182)
*Dongmyoung Lee,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对灵巧遥操作中接触式旋转操作（如拧瓶盖、转钥匙、旋螺栓）因具身差距导致指尖切向滑动、螺丝轴漂移等问题，提出DexTwist功能重定向框架，通过检测三脚捏合、估计操作者意图的旋量轴与扭转幅度，并结合实时残差关节空间优化，提升转角跟踪与旋量轴稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动学相似性最小化的重定向方法在接触式旋转操作中失败，原因在于具身差距（连杆长度、关节轴/限位、指尖几何不匹配）导致直接姿态模仿引发指尖切向滑动而非稳定物体旋转，造成旋量轴漂移、接触滑动和抓取不稳定。

Method: 提出DexTwist功能扭转重定向框架：检测三脚捏合手势，估计操作者意图的旋量轴与扭转幅度，执行实时残差关节空间优化，该优化跟踪旋转进度同时正则化机器人三脚几何结构，最小化由转角、旋量轴一致性、指尖闭合度和三脚稳定性定义的目标函数。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明，与基于向量的重定向基线相比，DexTwist在转角跟踪和旋量轴稳定性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: DexTwist通过功能级（旋量/扭转）而非运动学级重定向，有效弥合了接触式旋转操作中的具身差距，为MR灵巧遥操作提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Dexterous teleoperation via Mixed Reality (MR)-based interfaces offers a scalable paradigm for transferring human manipulation skills to dexterous robot hands. However, conventional retargeting approaches that minimize kinematic dissimilarity (e.g., joint angle or fingertip position error) often fail in contact-rich rotational manipulation, such as cap opening, key turning, and bolt screwing. This failure stems from the embodiment gap: mismatched link lengths, joint axes/limits, and fingertip geometry can cause direct pose imitation to induce tangential fingertip sliding rather than stable object rotation, resulting in screw axis drift, contact slip, and grasp instability. To address this, we propose DexTwist, a functional twist-retargeting framework for MR-based dexterous teleoperation. DexTwist detects a tripod pinch, estimates the operator's intended screw axis and twist magnitude, and applies a real-time residual joint-space refinement that tracks turning progress while regularizing the robot tripod geometry. The refinement minimizes a virtual-object objective defined by turning angle, screw axis consistency, fingertip closure, and tripod stability. Simulation and real-world experiments show that DexTwist improves turning angle tracking and screw axis stability compared with a vector-based retargeting baseline.

</details>


### [32] [TMRL: Diffusion Timestep-Modulated Pretraining Enables Exploration for Efficient Policy Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12236)
*Matthew M. Hong,Jesse Zhang,Anusha Nagabandi,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一框架CSP+TMRL，通过扩散噪声注入和动态时间步调节，弥合行为克隆预训练与强化学习微调之间的探索鸿沟，实现高效的机器人策略微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的机器人策略使用行为克隆(BC)会产生狭窄的动作分布，缺乏下游强化学习探索所需的覆盖范围，导致微调效率低下。

Method: 1) CSP(Context-Smoothed Pre-training)：在预训练时将前向扩散噪声注入策略输入，在精确模仿与广泛动作覆盖之间构建连续谱。2) TMRL(Timestep-Modulated Reinforcement Learning)：在微调时通过调节扩散时间步动态控制探索程度。

Result: 可无缝集成状态、3D点云、图像VLA等多种策略输入；提升RL微调样本效率；在复杂真实世界操作任务中一小时以内完成微调。

Conclusion: CSP+TMRL统一框架有效解决了BC预训练到RL微调的探索瓶颈，实现了高效的机器人策略微调。

Abstract: Fine-tuning pre-trained robot policies with reinforcement learning (RL) often inherits the bottlenecks introduced by pre-training with behavioral cloning (BC), which produces narrow action distributions that lack the coverage necessary for downstream exploration. We present a unified framework that enables the exploration necessary to enable efficient robot policy finetuning by bridging BC pre-training and RL fine-tuning. Our pre-training method, Context-Smoothed Pre-training (CSP), injects forward-diffusion noise into policy inputs, creating a continuum between precise imitation and broad action coverage. We then fine-tune pre-trained policies via Timestep-Modulated Reinforcement Learning (TMRL), which trains the agent to dynamically adjust this conditioning during fine-tuning by modulating the diffusion timestep, granting explicit control over exploration. Integrating seamlessly with arbitrary policy inputs, e.g., states, 3D point clouds, or image-based VLA policies, we show that TMRL improves RL fine-tuning sample efficiency. Notably, TMRL enables successful real-world fine-tuning on complex manipulation tasks in under one hour. Videos and code available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/tmrl/.

</details>


### [33] [SI-Diff: A Framework for Learning Search and High-Precision Insertion with a Force-Domain Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12247)
*Yibo Liu,Stanko Oparnica,Simon Shewchun-Jakaitis,Guoyi Fu,Jie Wang,Jun Yang,Anand Jagannathan,Tony Hong-Yau Lo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SI-Diff框架，通过力域扩散策略统一解决机器人在接触丰富的装配任务中的搜索与高精度插入问题，将xy偏移容忍度从2mm提升至5mm，并具备零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将搜索与高精度插入作为两个独立任务处理，需要切换模型或权重，缺乏统一的框架来同时支持这两种具有不同动作模式的任务，不利于构建智能装配系统。

Method: 提出SI-Diff框架，核心包括：(1)力域扩散策略，用于学习搜索与高精度插入；(2)模式条件机制，使单一策略能捕捉不同的动作行为；(3)搜索教师策略，可生成多样化的示范轨迹，用于训练模型学习从触觉和末端速度观测到有效动作行为的映射。

Result: 相比最先进的基线TacDiffusion，SI-Diff将xy偏移容忍度从2mm扩展到5mm；同时在未见过的形状上展现出强大的零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: SI-Diff通过统一的扩散策略框架成功整合了装配中的搜索与插入任务，显著提升了鲁棒性并具备良好的泛化能力，为智能装配系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich assembly is fundamental in robotics but poses significant challenges due to uncertainties in relative poses, such as misalignments and small clearances in peg-in-hole tasks. Existing approaches typically address search and high-precision insertion separately, because these tasks involve distinct action patterns. However, supporting both tasks within a single model, without switching models or weights, is desirable for intelligent assembly systems. In this work, we propose SI-Diff, a framework that learns both search and high-precision insertion through a force-domain diffusion policy. To this end, we introduce a new mode-conditioning mechanism that enables the policy to capture distinct action behaviors under a single framework. Moreover, we develop a new search teacher policy that can generate diverse trajectories. By training on successful and efficient demonstrations provided by the teacher policy, the model learns the mapping from tactile and end-effector velocity observations to effective action behaviors. We conduct thorough experiments to show that SI-Diff extends the tolerance to x-y misalignments from 2 mm to 5 mm compared to the state-of-the-art baseline, TacDiffusion, while also demonstrating strong zero-shot transferability to unseen shapes.

</details>


### [34] [Real-Time Whole-Body Teleoperation of a Humanoid Robot Using IMU-Based Motion Capture with Sim2Sim and Sim2Real Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12347)
*Hamza Ahmed Durrani,Suleman Khan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于IMU动捕服的全身遥操作系统，将人体运动实时映射到Unitree G1人形机器人上，无需离线缓冲或学习组件，实现了从仿真到真实环境的稳定、低延迟全身动作复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身遥操作面临运动学不匹配、惯性传感器噪声累积、控制延迟和仿真到现实迁移等挑战，亟需一套稳定、低延迟的实用解决方案。

Method: 使用Virdyn IMU全身动捕服采集人体运动数据，设计自定义运动处理、运动学重定向和控制流程，实现连续低延迟的实时全身遥操作，先在MuJoCo仿真中验证（sim2sim），再直接部署到物理机器人（sim2real）。

Result: 系统成功复现了行走、站立、坐姿、转身、鞠躬及协调的全身表达性动作等多种运动，在仿真和实物机器人上均实现了稳定同步的遥操作。
