*Tom Sander,Hongyan Chang,Tomáš Souček,Tuan Tran,Valeriu Lacatusu,Sylvestre-Alvise Rebuffi,Alexandre Mourachko,Surya Parimi,Christophe Ropers,Rashel Moritz,Vanessa Stark,Hady Elsahar,Pierre Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TextSeal是一种基于Gumbel-max采样的LLM水印技术，通过双密钥生成、熵加权评分和多区域定位实现强检测性能，支持推理优化且无额外开销，在检测强度上优于SynthID-text，对稀释具有鲁棒性，理论无失真，并通过多语言人类评估验证了质量无损，同时具有"放射性"——水印信号可通过模型蒸馏传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM水印方法在检测强度、输出多样性保持、鲁棒性以及推理效率方面存在不足，需要一种更优的水印方案来实现可靠的内容溯源和未经授权使用的检测。

Method: 基于Gumbel-max采样，引入双密钥生成以恢复输出多样性，采用熵加权评分和多区域定位增强检测能力；支持投机解码和多token预测等推理优化，无额外推理开销。

Result: 在检测强度上严格优于SynthID-text等基线方法；对稀释（人机混合文本）具有鲁棒性，能保持可信的局部检测；6000次A/B比较、5种语言的多语言人类评估显示无感知质量差异；水印信号可通过模型蒸馏传递，实现未经授权使用的检测。

Conclusion: TextSeal是一种理论无失真、检测能力强、鲁棒性好且支持推理优化的LLM水印方案，不仅适用于内容溯源，还可用于检测模型的未经授权使用。

Abstract: We introduce TextSeal, a state-of-the-art watermark for large language models. Building on Gumbel-max sampling, TextSeal introduces dual-key generation to restore output diversity, along with entropy-weighted scoring and multi-region localization for improved detection. It supports serving optimizations such as speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, and does not add any inference overhead. TextSeal strictly dominates baselines like SynthID-text in detection strength and is robust to dilution, maintaining confident localized detection even in heavily mixed human/AI documents. The scheme is theoretically distortion-free, and evaluation across reasoning benchmarks confirms that it preserves downstream performance; while a multilingual human evaluation (6000 A/B comparisons, 5 languages) shows no perceptible quality difference. Beyond its use for provenance detection, TextSeal is also ``radioactive'': its watermark signal transfers through model distillation, enabling detection of unauthorized use.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Avoiding Cross-Datacenter Collective Congestion via Disaggregated Buffering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11852)
*Mariano Scazzariello,Noga H. Rotman,Dima Gavrilenko,Sajy Khashab,Alexander Shpiner,Matty Kadosh,Marco Chiesa,Dejan Kostic,Mark Silberstein*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Spillway是一种透明网内机制，通过在目标数据中心的交换机解耦缓冲区中缓冲丢包，并在拥塞缓解后排出，解决了跨DC集合通信与内部流量碰撞导致的性能下降问题，最高可减少14%的迭代时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM训练跨多个数据中心时，跨DC集合通信与目标DC内部流量不可避免发生碰撞，而毫秒级拥塞控制环路响应过慢，导致严重丢包和拥塞崩溃，成为被忽视的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出Spillway，一种透明网内机制，在目标数据中心的交换机解耦缓冲区中缓冲被丢弃的数据包，待拥塞缓解后再排出，无需修改端主机或训练框架。

Result: 通过大规模端到端仿真和硬件原型验证，Spillway消除了由集合通信碰撞导致的性能下降，迭代时间最多减少14%。

Conclusion: Spillway有效解决了跨DC训练中集合通信与内部流量碰撞这一被忽视的瓶颈问题，且无需对现有系统进行修改。

Abstract: LLM training at the scale of tens of thousands of GPUs now spans multiple datacenters (DC), making cross-DC collectives over long-haul links unavoidable. A critical and overlooked bottleneck arises when these collectives collide with intra-DC traffic at the destination - a common pattern in real workloads. The multi-millisecond congestion control loop is too slow to react, triggering severe packet loss and congestion collapse.
  We present Spillway, a transparent in-network mechanism that buffers dropped packets in switch-disaggregated buffers in a destination data center and drains them once congestion subsides. Through large-scale end-to-end simulations and a hardware prototype, we show that Spillway eliminates performance degradation from collective collisions, reducing iteration time by up to 14 %, without changes to end hosts or training frameworks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [Enabling Performant and Flexible Model-Internal Observability for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11093)
*Nengneng Yu,Sixian Xiong,Yibo Zhao,Wei Wang,Zaoxing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DMI-Lib 是一个高速深度模型检查器，通过异步可观测性子系统（基于 Ring² GPU-CPU 内存抽象和策略控制的主机后端）将内部状态观测与推理热路径解耦，在离线批处理中仅引入 0.4%-6.8% 开销，在线服务中平均 6% 开销，延迟开销相比现有基线降低 2-15 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理时工作负载越来越依赖对模型内部状态的及时访问，但现有方法将观测与推理路径耦合，导致性能开销大、难以在保持服务优化的同时支持丰富观测点。

Method: 提出 DMI-Lib，将内部可观测性作为一等系统原语，通过 Ring²（GPU-CPU 内存抽象）实现张量的异步捕获和暂存，配合策略控制的主机后端导出状态，从而与推理热路径解耦。

Result: 离线批推理开销 0.4%-6.8%，在线服务平均 6% 开销，延迟开销比现有基线降低 2-15 倍。

Conclusion: DMI-Lib 能够在保持服务优化和 GPU 内存预算约束下，以极低性能损失实现丰富的模型内部状态观测，已开源。

Abstract: Today's inference-time workloads increasingly depend on timely access to a model's internal states. We present DMI-Lib, a high-speed deep model inspector that treats internal observability as a first-class systems primitive, decoupling it from the inference hot path via an asynchronous observability substrate built from Ring^2, a GPU-CPU memory abstraction for capturing and staging tensors, and a policy-controlled host backend that exports them. DMI-Lib enables the placement of observation points across a rich space of internal signals and diverse inference backends while preserving serving optimizations and adhering to tight GPU memory budgets. Our experiments demonstrate that DMI-Lib incurs only 0.4%--6.8% overhead in offline batch inference and an average of 6% in moderate online serving, reducing latency overhead by 2x-15x compared to existing baselines with similar observability features. DMI-Lib is open-sourced at https://github.com/ProjectDMX/DMI.

</details>


### [75] [FERMI: Exploiting Relations for Membership Inference Against Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11527)
*Abtin Mahyar,Masoumeh Shafieinejad,Yuhan Liu,Xi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有成员推断攻击主要针对单表设定，忽略了真实数据中的多表关系结构。本文提出FERMI方法，通过在训练攻击模型时利用目标表关联的父表等辅助信息，在推理时仅使用目标表记录属性值的情况下，将单表特征与关系成员信号相结合，显著提升对表格扩散模型的成员推断攻击性能（白盒设置下TPR@0.1FPR提升达53%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型越来越多地用于共享敏感表格数据，但其隐私保护能力存疑。现有成员推断攻击仅考虑单表设定，忽略了真实数据中多表关系结构带来的隐私风险，亟需评估此类场景下的隐私泄露问题。

Method: 提出FERMI方法，在攻击模型训练阶段利用目标表关联的父表等辅助信息，将单表特征与关系成员信号进行特征映射融合；在推理阶段攻击者仅需观察目标表中目标记录的属性值，即可实施成员推断攻击。

Result: 在三种表格扩散架构和三个真实关系数据集上，FERMI相比单表基线持续提升攻击性能：白盒设置下TPR@0.1FPR提升高达53%，黑盒设置下提升22%。

Conclusion: 多表关系结构中的辅助信息能够显著增强成员推断攻击的效果，真实世界表格数据共享时的隐私风险比单表设定下评估的更为严峻。

Abstract: Diffusion models are the leading approach for tabular data synthesis and are increasingly used to share sensitive records. Whether they actually protect privacy has become a pressing question. Membership inference attacks are the standard tool for this purpose, yet existing attacks assume a single-table setting and ignore the multi-relational structure of real sensitive data. A core challenge in assessing privacy risks from membership inference attacks in multi-table settings is how to leverage auxiliary information from relations associated with the target table, such as its parent tables. Particularly, we study a practical setting in which such auxiliary information is available only when training the attack model. At inference time, the attacker observes only the attribute values of the target record from the target table. We propose FERMI (FEature-mapping for Relational Membership Inference), which resolves this gap by enriching single-table features with relational membership signal. Across three tabular diffusion architectures and three real-world relational datasets, FERMI consistently improves attack performance over single-table baselines, with TPR@$0.1$FPR rising by up to 53% over the single-table baseline in the white-box setting and 22% in the black-box setting.

</details>


### [76] [Newton's Lantern: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Finetuning AC Power Flow Warm Start Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11102)
*Shourya Bose,Helgi Hilmarsson,Dhruv Suri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了牛顿-拉夫逊法求解交流潮流问题的迭代次数下界取决于热启动误差的方向而非大小，并指出当雅可比矩阵最小奇异值较小时该下界失效，揭示了监督回归在电压崩溃附近的失败模式。由此提出Newton's Lantern微调流程，结合群体相对策略优化和基于迭代次数的奖励模型，在IEEE 118、GOC 500和GOC 2000节点基准上实现了唯一收敛于所有测试快照且平均迭代次数最小的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经热启动方法在重载场景（接近电压崩溃）下泛化性能差，缺乏理论解释。本文旨在从理论上揭示该失败模式的根源，并据此设计更鲁棒的求解方法。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明牛顿-拉夫逊迭代次数下界取决于热启动误差方向而非幅值，并指出当潮流雅可比矩阵最小奇异值趋近于零时该下界退化为平凡界。2) 提出Newton's Lantern微调流程：结合群体相对策略优化（GRPO）与基于扰动预测的奖励模型，以迭代次数为监督信号进行微调。

Result: 在IEEE 118-bus、GOC 500-bus和GOC 2000-bus基准测试中，Newton's Lantern是唯一在所有测试快照上均收敛的方法，且平均迭代次数最小。

Conclusion: 理论揭示了监督回归在电压崩溃附近失败的根本原因（雅可比矩阵奇异导致下界失效），基于强化学习的微调方法Newton's Lantern显著提升了神经热启动的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Neural warm starts can sharply reduce the number of Newton-Raphson iterations required to solve the AC power flow problem, but existing supervised approaches generalize poorly on heavily loaded instances near voltage collapse. We prove a lower bound on the Newton-Raphson iteration count that depends on the direction of the warm start error rather than on its magnitude, and show as a corollary that the bound becomes vacuous as the smallest singular value of the power-flow Jacobian shrinks, identifying the failure mode of supervised regression near the saddle-node bifurcation. Motivated by this analysis, we introduce Newton's Lantern, a finetuning pipeline that combines group relative policy optimization with a learned reward model trained on perturbations of the base model's predictions, using the iteration count itself as the supervisory signal. Across IEEE 118-bus, GOC 500-bus, and GOC 2000-bus benchmarks, Newton's Lantern is the only method that converges on every test snapshot while attaining the smallest mean iteration count.

</details>


### [77] [EpiCastBench: Datasets and Benchmarks for Multivariate Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11598)
*Madhurima Panja,Danny D'Agostino,Huitao Li,Tanujit Chakraborty,Nan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiCastBench，一个包含40个多变量疫情数据集的大规模基准框架，并系统评估了15种多变量预测模型，填补了疫情预测领域缺乏高质量多变量基准的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的公共卫生决策日益重要，但疫情预测领域缺乏高质量的多变量基准数据集和标准化评估体系，限制了稳健预测方法的开发。

Method: 构建了包含40个多变量疫情数据集的EpiCastBench框架，涵盖多种传染病和地理区域；建立统一评估标准（预测时域、预处理流程、多指标及统计显著性检验）；对15种多变量预测模型（从统计基线到深度学习及基础模型）进行全面评估。

Result: 提供了公开可用的40个多变量疫情数据集，完成了15种预测模型的系统性基准测试，所有代码和数据集已在Kaggle和GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: EpiCastBench为疫情预测研究提供了标准化基准平台，有助于推动多变量预测方法在公共卫生领域的应用与发展。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of data-driven decision-making in public health has established epidemic forecasting as a critical area of research. Recent advances in multivariate forecasting models better capture complex temporal dependencies than conventional univariate approaches, which model individual series independently. Despite this potential, the development of robust epidemic forecasting methods is constrained by the lack of high-quality benchmarks comprising diverse multivariate datasets across infectious diseases and geographical regions. To address this gap, we present EpiCastBench, a large-scale benchmarking framework featuring 40 curated (correlated) multivariate epidemic datasets. These publicly available datasets span a wide range of infectious diseases and exhibit diverse characteristics in terms of temporal granularity, series length, and sparsity. We analyze these datasets to identify their global features and structural patterns. To ensure reproducibility and fair comparison, we establish standardized evaluation settings, including a unified forecasting horizon, consistent preprocessing pipelines, diverse performance metrics, and statistical significance testing. By leveraging this framework, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation of 15 multivariate forecasting models spanning statistical baselines to state-of-the-art deep learning and foundation models. All datasets and code are publicly available on Kaggle (https://www.kaggle.com/datasets/aimltsf/epicastbench) and GitHub (https://github.com/aimltsf/EpiCastBench).

</details>


### [78] [Steering Without Breaking: Mechanistically Informed Interventions for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10971)
*Hanhan Zhou,Shamik Roy,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 离散扩散语言模型在并行去噪生成文本时，现有控制方法采用均匀干预调度，导致生成质量下降，尤其在多属性控制时问题加剧。本文发现不同属性的"承诺"时间分布各异（如主题在去噪前2%即确定，情感在20%过程中逐渐形成），因此提出自适应调度器，仅在属性活跃形成阶段施加干预，实现了更精确的多属性控制（三属性联合控制达到93%强度，比最强基线高15个百分点），且不牺牲生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散语言模型的受控生成方法直接沿用自回归模型的均匀干预策略，在所有去噪步骤施加相同强度的控制。作者发现这种均匀调度会显著降低生成质量，且在同时控制多个属性时问题更加严重。为了诊断这一失败原因，作者训练了稀疏自编码器来观察模型内部表征。

Method: 1) 在四个不同规模的DLM（124M-8B参数）上训练稀疏自编码器，发现不同属性的承诺（commitment）时间分布不同——主题在去噪前2%即确定，情感在20%过程中逐渐形成。2) 提出自适应调度器，仅在属性活跃形成的时间步集中施加干预，其余步骤不受干扰。3) 给出了成本-控制权衡的闭式解：自适应调度的优势由承诺分布的单一离散统计量决定。

Result: 在四个DLM和七个控制任务上，自适应调度实现了精确控制且没有均匀干预带来的质量退化。特别是在同时控制三个属性的挑战性任务中，达到了93%的控制强度，比最强基线高出15个百分点，同时保持了生成质量。

Conclusion: 均匀干预策略不适合离散扩散模型的受控生成，因为不同属性的承诺时间分布各异。自适应调度器通过仅在属性活跃形成阶段施加干预，能够在实现高精度多属性控制的同时避免生成质量下降，是一种更优的调度策略。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising all positions in parallel, offering an alternative to autoregressive models. Controlled generation methods for DLMs, imported from autoregressive models, apply uniform intervention at every denoising steps. We show this uniform schedule degrades quality, and the damage compounds when multiple attributes are steered jointly. To diagnose the failure, we train sparse autoencoders on four DLMs (124M-8B parameters) and find that different attributes commit on distinct schedules, varying in timing, sharpness, and magnitude. For instance, topic commits within the first 2\% of denoising, whereas sentiment emerges gradually over 20\% of the process. Consequently, uniform intervention wastes steering capacity on steps where the target attribute has already solidified or has yet to emerge. We propose a novel adaptive scheduler that concentrates interventions on the steps where an attribute is actively forming and leaves the rest of generation untouched. The cost-control trade-off admits a closed-form characterization: the advantage of adaptive over uniform scheduling is governed by a single dispersion statistic of the commitment distribution. Across four DLMs and seven steering tasks, our method achieves precise control without the degradation typical of uniform interventions. Especially on challenging simultaneous three-attribute control, it reaches up to 93\% steering strength, beating the strongest baseline by up to 15\% points while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [79] [More Than Meets the Eye: A Semantics-Aware Traffic Augmentation Framework for Generalizable Website Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11402)
*Youquan Xian,Xueying Zeng,Lingjia Meng,Lei Cui,Runhan Song,Wei Wang,Zhengquan Ding,Peng Liu,Zhiyu Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SATA框架，通过语义增强和数据增强技术解决网站指纹识别在跨地理和时间分布下的泛化问题，在开放世界场景下ACC提升90.81%，AUROC提升48.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的网站指纹识别方法在封闭世界数据集上表现良好，但在真实环境下（尤其是地理和时间偏移）泛化能力差。根本原因在于应用层资源组成可变性和跨层封装导致的可观察特征不稳定这两个挑战相互耦合，造成底层应用语义与可观察流量特征之间的系统性偏移。

Method: SATA分为两步：1）应用层语义增强：基于协议规则扩展每个流中的资源组成模式和帧序列模式；2）跨层特征对齐：通过知识蒸馏将增强后的帧序列特征与包长度序列特征对齐，实现增强语义与可观察序列的跨层特征对齐。

Result: SATA能够生成训练集中不存在但测试集中真实存在的流量模式，在多样复杂场景下显著提升主流模型性能。开放世界设置下，ACC提升90.81%，AUROC提升48.37%。

Conclusion: SATA通过语义感知的流量增强和跨层特征对齐，有效解决了网站指纹识别在真实环境下的泛化问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning-based website fingerprinting has emerged as an effective technique for inferring the websites users visit. Although existing methods achieve strong performance on closed-world datasets, they often fail to generalize to real-world environments, especially under geographic and temporal shifts. This limitation fundamentally stems from the coupled effects of two key challenges: application-layer resource composition variability and observable feature instability induced by cross-layer encapsulation. Intertwined, these factors induce systematic shifts between underlying application semantics and observable traffic features. To address the above challenges, we propose SATA , a semantics-aware traffic augmentation framework. Specifically, SATA first performs application-layer semantic augmentation based on protocol rules, expanding the resource composition patterns within each flow and frame sequence patterns under protocol constraints. Based on these augmented frame sequences, we further introduce a cross-layer feature alignment mechanism via knowledge distillation. It aligns frame sequence with packet-length sequence features, enabling cross-layer feature alignment between enhanced semantics and observable sequences. Extensive experiments show that SATA successfully generates traffic patterns that are absent from the training set but genuinely exist in the test set, and significantly improves the performance of mainstream models across diverse and complex scenarios. In particular, in open-world settings, SATA improves ACC by 90.81% and AUROC by 48.37%. The source code of the prototype system is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SATA-B6C2/.

</details>


### [80] [Vertex-Softmax: Tight Transformer Verification via Exact Softmax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10974)
*Navid Rezazadeh,Arash Gholami Davoodi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Transformer注意力机制中的softmax函数认证验证问题，提出了Vertex-Softmax方法，严格证明了softmax在区间约束下的最优解位于约束盒子的顶点，并通过阈值结构定理将候选解压缩为线性数量（对数线性复杂度）。该方法在形式化最优性意义上是最紧的基于分数区间的边界，并集成到CROWN验证器中，在多个数据集上显著提升了认证率并大幅收紧下界，以极低计算成本匹配或超越alpha-CROWN和分支定界基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer注意力认证验证器在处理softmax函数时，独立于下游目标进行松弛，导致可避免的松弛（slack），即未能利用下游目标信息来收紧边界，从而影响认证精度。

Method: 1) 理论证明：证明softmax在分数区间约束下的精确最优解位于约束盒子的顶点（vertex）；2) 阈值结构定理：在对目标系数排序后，最优解仅来自线性数量的候选点，由此设计Vertex-Softmax原语，实现对数线性复杂度；3) 形式最优性证明：证明Vertex-Softmax是基于分数区间唯一能获得的最紧可靠边界，明确指出需要分数相关性或分数-值耦合等额外结构才能进一步改进；4) 集成到CROWN风格的验证器中，提供形式化可靠性保证。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和CIFAR-10的注意力模型上，Vertex-Softmax显著提升了认证率（certified rates），大幅收紧了下界（lower bounds），并以极低的计算成本一致地匹配或超越了alpha-CROWN和分支定界（branch-and-bound）基线方法。

Conclusion: Vertex-Softmax是最优的基于分数区间的softmax边界方法，为Transformer注意力认证提供了理论基础和实践改进，同时指明了进一步提升所需超越的额外信息结构（分数相关性、分数-值耦合）。

Abstract: Certified verification of transformer attention requires bounding the softmax function over interval constraints on the pre-softmax scores. Existing verifiers relax softmax ndependently of the downstream objective, leaving avoidable slack. We prove that the exact optimum of this score-box problem is attained at a vertex of the constraint box, and establish a threshold structure theorem showing that, after sorting the objective coefficients, the optimum lies among only linearly many candidates, yielding the Vertex-Softmax primitive with log-linear complexity in the sequence length. We further prove a formal optimality result showing that Vertex-Softmax is the tightest sound bound obtainable from score intervals alone, characterizing precisely what additional structure (score correlations, score-value coupling) is needed for further improvement. Integrated into a CROWN Convex Relaxation based Optimization for Worst-case Neurons)-style verifier with a formal soundness guarantee, Vertex-Softmax significantly improves certified rates and substantially tightens lower bounds across MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 attention models, while consistently matching or outperforming alpha-CROWN and branch-and-bound baselines at a fraction of their cost.

</details>


### [81] [SkillGen: Verified Inference-Time Agent Skill Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10999)
*Yuchen Ma,Yue Huang,Han Bao,Haomin Zhuang,Swadheen Shukla,Michel Galley,Xiangliang Zhang,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillGen是一个多智能体框架，能从基础智能体生成的轨迹中自动合成可审计、可重用的技能，通过对成功和失败轨迹的对比归纳及基于干预的效果验证，显著提升LLM智能体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量技能目前仍主要依赖手工编写，缺乏自动化的技能生成方法，因此需要一种能自动从智能体交互轨迹中合成可重用、可审计技能的方法。

Method: SkillGen采用多智能体框架，通过对比归纳（contrastive induction）分析成功与失败轨迹，识别可重用的成功模式、反复出现的失败模式及其差异；生成候选技能后迭代优化；核心创新在于将技能建模为干预（intervention），通过在同一实例上对比有无技能的结果来实证验证技能的净效果，同时衡量修复（修复基线失败）和回归（破坏基线成功）两方面。

Result: 在广泛的智能体和数据集上，SkillGen一致提升了留出性能，优于现有技能生成基线，且生成的技能可跨模型迁移。

Conclusion: SkillGen通过对比归纳和干预式验证，实现了高质量的自动化技能生成，提升了LLM智能体的可控性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Skills are a promising way to improve LLM agent capabilities without retraining, while keeping the added procedure reusable and controllable. However, high-quality skills are still largely written by hand. We introduce SkillGen, a multi-agent framework that synthesizes a single auditable skill from trajectories generated by a base agent. The output is a human-readable artifact that can be inspected before use. Rather than merely summarizing trajectories, SkillGen leverages contrastive induction over both successful and failed trajectories to identify reusable success patterns, recurring failure modes, and behaviors that appear in nearby successes but are missing from failures. SkillGen then generates candidate skills and iteratively refines the skill. A key novelty in SkillGen is that we model agent skills as interventions to empirically verify the net effect of skills on the overall performance. Specifically, we compare outcomes on the same instances with and without the skill, so that we account for both repairs (cases where the skill fixes a baseline failure) and regressions (cases where the skill breaks a baseline success). Across a broad range of agents and datasets, SkillGen consistently improves held-out performance, outperforms existing skill-generation baselines, and produces skills that transfer across models.

</details>


### [82] [$ξ$-DPO: Direct Preference Optimization via Ratio Reward Margin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10981)
*Zhengyuan Fan,Zhonghua Wu,Yuxuan Du,Qun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ξ-DPO，通过比率奖励边际重新定义偏好优化目标，解决了SimPO中超参数β和γ难以联合调优的问题，使边际具有可解释性且无需反复试错调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SimPO中的超参数β和γ联合调优困难，因为其边际公式在不同数据集的奖励差距结构下难以解释，导致调参依赖反复试错。

Method: 首先通过等价变换将偏好优化目标从最大化奖励差距的似然转变为最小化奖励差距与最优边际之间的距离；然后将奖励重新定义为选择响应与拒绝响应的比率形式，消除β的影响，得到有界且可解释的比率奖励边际ξ。

Result: ξ具有明确的语义解释，表示选择与拒绝响应之间期望的相对分离程度，且可从初始奖励差距分布中直接确定，无需反复试错调参。

Conclusion: ξ-DPO通过可解释的比率奖励边际有效解决了SimPO超参数调优难题，提升了偏好优化的实用性和效率。

Abstract: Reference-free preference optimization has emerged as an efficient alternative to reinforcement learning from human feedback, with Simple Preference Optimization(SimPO) demonstrating strong performance by eliminating the explicit reference model through a simple objective. However, the joint tuning of the hyperparameters $β$ and $γ$ in SimPO remains a central challenge. We argue that this difficulty arises because the margin formulation in SimPO is not easily interpretable across datasets with different reward gap structures. To better understand this issue, we conduct a comprehensive analysis of SimPO and find that $β$ implicitly controls sample filtering, while the effect of $γ$ depends on the reward gap structure of the dataset. Motivated by these observations, we propose $ξ$-DPO: Direct preference optimization via ratio reward margin. We first reformulate the preference objective through an equivalent transformation, changing the optimization target from maximizing the likelihood of reward gaps to minimizing the distance between reward gaps and optimal margins. Then, we redefine the reward in a ratio form between the chosen and rejected, which effectively cancels the effect of $β$ and yields a bounded and interpretable margin. This margin is called the ratio reward margin and is denoted by $ξ$. Unlike the margin $γ$ in SimPO, $ξ$ explicitly represents the desired relative separation between chosen and rejected responses and can be determined from the initial reward gap distribution, avoiding repeated trial-and-error tuning. ....

</details>


### [83] [GRAFT-ATHENA: Self-Improving Agentic Teams for Autonomous Discovery and Evolutionary Numerical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11117)
*Juan Diego Toscano,Zhaojie Chai,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRAFT-ATHENA，一个自我改进的智能体框架，通过将组合决策空间分解为因子化概率树，使参数足迹从指数级降至线性级，并利用度量空间中的方法嵌入实现跨领域经验积累。在物理信息机器学习基准和实际工程问题上，该框架超越了人类和现有智能体基线，能自主扩展动作空间、提出正则化约束并发现新数值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体AI框架将每个问题孤立处理，缺乏跨领域共享的机制来积累方法学经验，无法利用过去问题的知识指导新问题的求解。

Method: 提出GRAFT（图归约为自适应因子化树），将组合决策空间投影为因子化概率树，每个方法对应一条路径，参数规模从指数降至线性。在此基础上构建GRAFT-ATHENA框架，通过度量空间中的方法指纹嵌入，使新问题能从相似历史问题中学习。

Result: 在物理信息机器学习基准上超越人类和现有智能体基线；成功解决Apollo指令舱高超声速流场重建、剪切稀化血细胞流变学恢复等复杂工程问题；自主提出不适定逆问题的正则化约束；发现新的数值方法（如具有指数收敛速度的谱PINN）。

Conclusion: GRAFT-ATHENA为自主实验室提供了基础，这类实验室能随着解决每个问题而不断增强自身能力。

Abstract: Scientific discovery can be modeled as a sequence of probabilistic decisions that map physical problems to numerical solutions. Recent agentic AI systems automate individual scientific tasks by orchestrating LLM-driven planners, solvers, and evaluators. Each method is a combination of methodological actions, with many viable combinations for any given problem and structural dependencies between choices. However, existing frameworks treat each problem in isolation, with no shared substrate to accumulate methodological experience across domains. Here we show that GRAFT-ATHENA, a self-improving agentic framework, learns from past problems and autonomously expands its own action space across diverse domains. GRAFT (Graph Reduction to Adaptive Factored Trees) projects combinatorial decision spaces into factored probabilistic trees in which each method is a single path, taking the parameter footprint from exponential to linear. In the lineage of classical Bayesian networks, the factorization is an $I$-map of the policy, and the resulting paths embed as unique fingerprints in a metric space whose closeness lets each new problem learn from similar past ones. On canonical physics-informed machine learning (PIML) benchmarks, GRAFT-ATHENA improves over human and prior agentic baselines, and on production solvers, it tackles complex engineering problems such as reconstructing Mach-10 flow over the Apollo Command Module from a 1968 report and recovering shear-thinning blood-cell rheology. Notably, the system grows its own knowledge substrate, autonomously proposing regularization constraints for ill-posed inverse problems and discovering new numerical methods such as a spectral PINN with exponential convergence. These results provide a foundation for autonomous laboratories that grow more capable with every problem they solve.

</details>


### [84] [TMPO: Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization for Diverse and Efficient Diffusion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10983)
*Jiaming Li,Chenyu Zhu,Zhiyuan Ma,Nanxi Yi,Youjun Bao,Li Sun,Quanying Lv,Xiang Fang,Daizong Liu,Jianjun Li,Kun He,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹匹配策略优化（TMPO），通过将标量奖励最大化替换为轨迹级奖励分布匹配，采用Softmax轨迹平衡目标和动态随机树采样，在保持生成多样性的同时优化奖励，在多种对齐任务中实现9.1%的多样性提升并达到奖励与多样性的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的扩散模型对齐方法存在严重的奖励破解问题，导致生成多样性和质量下降、视觉模式坍塌。根本原因是这些方法具有模式寻求（mode-seeking）特性，仅最大化期望奖励而缺乏对可接受轨迹概率分布的有效约束。

Method: TMPO提出三方面创新：1）用轨迹级奖励分布匹配替代标量奖励最大化；2）引入Softmax轨迹平衡（Softmax-TB）目标，将K条轨迹的策略概率匹配到奖励诱导的玻尔兹曼分布，该目标具有前向KL散度的模式覆盖特性；3）提出动态随机树采样（Dynamic Stochastic Tree Sampling），使轨迹共享去噪前缀并在动态调度步骤分支，减少冗余计算。

Result: 在人类偏好、组合生成和文本渲染等多种对齐任务中，TMPO相比最先进方法将生成多样性提升9.1%，在所有下游任务和效率指标上取得竞争性表现，实现了奖励与多样性的最优平衡。

Conclusion: TMPO通过分布匹配范式有效解决了扩散模型RL对齐中的奖励破解问题，为生成质量与多样性之间的权衡提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown extraordinary potential in aligning diffusion models to downstream tasks, yet most of them still suffer from significant reward hacking, which degrades generative diversity and quality by inducing visual mode collapse and amplifying unreliable rewards. We identify the root cause as the mode-seeking nature of these methods, which maximize expected reward without effectively constraining probability distribution over acceptable trajectories, causing concentration on a few high-reward paths. In contrast, we propose Trajectory Matching Policy Optimization (TMPO), which replaces scalar reward maximization with trajectory-level reward distribution matching. Specifically, TMPO introduces a Softmax Trajectory Balance (Softmax-TB) objective to match the policy probabilities of K trajectories to a reward-induced Boltzmann distribution. We prove that this objective inherits the mode-covering property of forward KL divergence, preserving coverage over all acceptable trajectories while optimizing reward. To further reduce multi-trajectory training time on large-scale flow-matching models, TMPO incorporates Dynamic Stochastic Tree Sampling, where trajectories share denoising prefixes and branch at dynamically scheduled steps, reducing redundant computation while improving training effectiveness. Extensive results across diverse alignment tasks such as human preference, compositional generation and text rendering show that TMPO improves generative diversity over state-of-the-art methods by 9.1%, and achieves competitive performance in all downstream and efficiency metrics, attaining the optimal trade-off between reward and diversity.

</details>


### [85] [Shaping Zero-Shot Coordination via State Blocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11688)
*Mingu Kang,Sunwoo Lee,Yonghyeon Jo,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出状态阻塞协调（SBC）框架，通过状态阻塞生成虚拟环境族，让智能体体验多种次优合作伙伴策略，从而改进零样本协调（ZSC）性能，在多个基准测试中表现优异，并能泛化到人类合作伙伴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本协调方法主要依赖增加训练时的合作伙伴多样性，但往往无法可靠地泛化到未见过的合作伙伴，需要一种更有效的策略来提升ZSC性能。

Method: 提出状态阻塞协调（SBC）框架：通过状态阻塞生成一系列虚拟环境，在不直接修改原始环境的前提下，让智能体能够体验多种次优合作伙伴策略。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，SBC在零样本协调任务上表现优于现有方法，并且展现出对未知人类合作伙伴的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SBC是一种简单有效的零样本协调框架，通过状态阻塞诱导多样化的交互场景，显著提升了智能体与未见合作伙伴的协调能力。

Abstract: Zero-shot coordination (ZSC) aims to enable agents to cooperate with independently trained partners without prior interaction, a key requirement for real-world multi-agent systems and human-AI collaboration. Existing approaches have largely emphasized increasing partner diversity during training, yet such strategies often fall short of achieving reliable generalization to unseen partners. We introduce State-Blocked Coordination (SBC), a simple yet effective framework that improves ZSC by inducing diverse interaction scenarios without direct environment modification. Specifically, SBC generates a family of virtual environments through state blocking, allowing agents to experience a wide range of suboptimal partner policies. Across multiple benchmarks, SBC demonstrates superior performance in zero-shot coordination, including strong generalization to human partners.

</details>


### [86] [AESOP: Adversarial Execution-path Selection to Overload Deep Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10987)
*Tingxi Li,Mingfang Ji,Ravishka Shemal Rathnasuriya,Simin Chen,Yitao Hu,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现现代ML组合流水线存在一种新的效率攻击面——路径感知攻击，通过操纵上游组件的中间预测来诱导下游组件执行高成本路径，在相同输入和预算下比单模型攻击实现高达2407倍FLOPs膨胀，并提出了AESOP框架进行系统性攻击。即使加入系统级防御，攻击也无法被消除，只会导致系统在吞吐量崩溃和数据丢失之间二选一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习部署日益将多个专用模型组合成动态推理流水线，其中上游组件的中间预测决定了下游组件的工作负载和输入。由于这些流水线运行在硬实时约束下，效率是系统可用性的基本要求。现有针对单模型的效率攻击方法无法利用这种流水线结构中的新攻击面。

Method: 形式化了对抗性路径选择问题，提出了AESOP框架，该框架结合了漏洞引导的路径排序（vulnerability-guided path ranking）和自适应损失加权（adaptive loss weighting）来生成攻击。在五条流水线以及包含批处理、有界缓冲和置信度阈值防御的生产级部署变体上进行评估。

Result: 在白盒设置下实现高达2407倍FLOPs膨胀和419倍延迟膨胀；在灰盒设置下实现58倍FLOPs和17倍延迟膨胀。在系统级防御下，攻击不会被消除而是被重新定向：流水线被迫在吞吐量崩溃（0.578→0.006输入/秒）或丢弃96.7%数据以维持吞吐量之间做出选择。

Conclusion: 路径感知攻击比最强的单模型基线（117倍FLOPs膨胀）高出20倍，这完全归因于攻击的定向位置。这一发现揭示了组合推理流水线中存在一种独特的、此前未被认识的效率脆弱性，对实时ML系统的安全部署构成严重威胁。

Abstract: Modern machine learning deployments increasingly compose specialized models into dynamic inference pipelines, where upstream components produce intermediate predictions that determine the workload and inputs of downstream components. The cost of processing an input is therefore not determined by any single model, but by two coupled factors: the per-inference cost of each invoked component and its workload volume. Because these pipelines run under hard real-time constraints, efficiency is a fundamental requirement for system availability. We show that this structure creates an efficiency-attack surface that existing methods targeting single models cannot exploit: on identical inputs and budgets, path-aware targeting inflates FLOPs by $2,407\times$ while the strongest single-model baseline achieves $117\times$ -- a $20\times$ gap attributable entirely to where the attack is directed. We formalize this as the adversarial path-selection problem and present AESOP, a framework combining vulnerability-guided path ranking with adaptive loss weighting. We evaluate AESOP on five pipelines plus a production-realistic deployment variant with batching, bounded buffering, and confidence-threshold defenses. AESOP achieves up to $2,407\times$ FLOPs and $419\times$ latency inflation in white-box setting and 58$\times$ FLOPs / 17$\times$ latency in gray-box settings. Under system-level defenses, the attack is not neutralized but redirected: pipelines are forced to choose between throughput collapse ($0.578 \to 0.006$ input/s) and $96.7\%$ data loss to sustain throughput.

</details>


### [87] [Test-Time Personalization: A Diagnostic Framework and Probabilistic Fix for Scaling Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10991)
*Linhai Zhang,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM个性化中的测试时个性化（TTP）方法，通过从个性化策略模型中采样N个候选并利用个性化奖励模型选择最优，证明了理论上期望效用随候选数对数增长。通过推导统一缩放定律，识别出用户级崩溃和查询级奖励黑客两种失败模式，并提出基于概率个性化奖励模型来缓解这两种问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法聚焦于构建更好的个性化模型或输入，但将推理视为单次过程，忽略了推理时计算扩展的潜力。

Method: 提出测试时个性化（TTP）框架，采样N个候选并选择最优；推导统一缩放定律分解Best-of-N曲线为四个可测量量；提出概率个性化奖励模型，利用学习到的方差缓解用户级崩溃和查询级奖励黑客。

Result: 理论上证明了oracle选择的期望效用随候选数对数增长；实验验证TTP在多个策略模型和个性化文本生成任务中持续扩展，缩放定律与观察曲线高度吻合。

Conclusion: 测试时计算扩展可以有效提升LLM个性化性能，但需设计合适的奖励模型来避免两种失败模式，概率个性化奖励模型是有效方案。

Abstract: Existing approaches to LLM personalization focus on constructing better personalized models or inputs, while treating inference as a single-shot process. In this work, we study Test-Time Personalization (TTP) along an unexplored axis: scaling inference-time computation by sampling N candidates from a personalized policy model and selecting the best with a personalized reward model. We prove that oracle selection yields expected utility growing logarithmically with the number of sampled candidates, establishing a theoretical ceiling for test-time scaling. However, standard reward models fail to realize this potential. To diagnose why, we derive a unified scaling law that decomposes any reward model's Best-of-N curve into four measurable quantities and reveals two failure modes, user-level collapse (near-constant prediction for some users) and query-level reward hacking (negative correlation with true quality for some queries). Guided by this law, we propose a probabilistic personalized reward model whose learned variance effectively mitigates both failure modes. Experiments confirm both elements of our framework: TTP delivers consistent scaling across multiple policy models and personalized text generation tasks, and our scaling law closely matches observed scaling curves across reward-model variants.

</details>


### [88] [Predicting Decisions of AI Agents from Limited Interaction through Text-Tabular Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12411)
*Eilam Shapira,Moshe Tennenholtz,Roi Reichart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究AI智能体在与未知对手进行自然语言谈判时，能否通过少量交互预测对手的下一步决策。通过将问题建模为目标自适应文本-表格预测任务，并引入LLM-as-Observer方法（使用冻结LLM的隐藏状态作为决策特征），在受控实验中显著优于直接提示等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的AI智能体（如采购助手、买方机器人）需要与未知对手（其LLM、提示词、控制逻辑均隐藏）进行自然语言谈判，每次决策都有金钱后果。现有方法难以在少量交互中准确预测对手行为，因此需要研究如何从有限交互中建模和预测对手决策。

Method: 将问题形式化为目标自适应文本-表格预测任务：每个决策点作为一个表格行，包含结构化游戏状态、报价历史和对话文本；提供目标对手前K轮游戏作为标注的适应示例。模型基于表格基础模型，使用游戏状态特征和基于LLM的文本表示，并创新性地引入LLM-as-Observer：使用冻结的小型LLM读取决策时刻状态和对话，丢弃其输出答案，仅利用隐藏状态作为决策导向特征。在13个前沿LLM智能体上训练，在91个保留的脚手架智能体上测试。

Result: 完整模型在响应预测AUC上比直接LLM-as-Predictor提示和游戏+文本特征基线提升约4个点（K=16时），报价预测误差降低14%。Observer特征的贡献超过了其他特征方案。

Conclusion: 将对手预测建模为目标自适应文本-表格任务能够实现有效的少样本适应；LLM隐藏状态能够暴露直接提示无法获取的决策相关信号，作为特征比直接作为预测器更有效。

Abstract: AI agents negotiate and transact in natural language with unfamiliar counterparts: a buyer bot facing an unknown seller, or a procurement assistant negotiating with a supplier. In such interactions, the counterpart's LLM, prompts, control logic, and rule-based fallbacks are hidden, while each decision can have monetary consequences. We ask whether an agent can predict an unfamiliar counterpart's next decision from a few interactions. To avoid real-world logging confounds, we study this problem in controlled bargaining and negotiation games, formulating it as target-adaptive text-tabular prediction: each decision point is a table row combining structured game state, offer history, and dialogue, while $K$ previous games of the same target agent, i.e., the counterpart being modeled, are provided in the prompt as labeled adaptation examples. Our model is built on a tabular foundation model that represents rows using game-state features and LLM-based text representations, and adds LLM-as-Observer as an additional representation: a small frozen LLM reads the decision-time state and dialogue; its answer is discarded, and its hidden state becomes a decision-oriented feature, making the LLM an encoder rather than a direct few-shot predictor. Training on 13 frontier-LLM agents and testing on 91 held-out scaffolded agents, the full model outperforms direct LLM-as-Predictor prompting and game+text features baselines. Within this tabular model, Observer features contribute beyond the other feature schemes: at $K=16$, they improve response-prediction AUC by about 4 points across both tasks and reduce bargaining offer-prediction error by 14%. These results show that formulating counterpart prediction as a target-adaptive text-tabular task enables effective adaptation, and that hidden LLM representations expose decision-relevant signals that direct prompting does not surface.

</details>


### [89] [RT-Transformer: The Transformer Block as a Spherical State Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11007)
*Peter Racioppo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明Transformer的核心组件（注意力机制、残差连接和归一化）可以从单个几何估计问题中自然导出，而非独立的架构设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Transformer架构中各组件之间的内在几何一致性，解释其为何有效工作，从第一性原理出发理解其设计。

Method: 将潜在状态建模为超球面上的方向，在切平面定义噪声，推导出精度加权的方向推理过程，其中注意力用于聚合证据、残差连接实现增量状态更新、归一化将更新状态投影回超球面。

Result: Transformer的三个核心组件（注意力、残差连接、归一化）可以从同一几何估计问题中自然导出，而非独立的启发式设计。

Conclusion: Transformer架构的设计具有深刻的几何基础，其组件之间存在内在的统一性，这为理解和改进Transformer提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: We show that the core components of the Transformer block -- attention, residual connections, and normalization -- arise naturally from a single geometric estimation problem. Modeling the latent state as a direction on the hypersphere, with noise defined in the tangent plane at the current estimate, yields a precision-weighted directional inference procedure in which attention aggregates evidence, residual connections implement incremental state updates, and normalization retracts the updated state back onto the hypersphere. Together, these components follow from the geometry of the estimation problem rather than being introduced as independent architectural choices.

</details>


### [90] [When and How to Canonize: A Generalization Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11008)
*Yonatan Sverdlov,Benjamin Friedman,Snir Hordan,Nadav Dym*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理论框架来分析通过群平均或规范化实现不变性的模型的泛化误差，建立了不变架构、群平均模型、规范化模型和非不变基线之间的泛化层次关系，并证明规范化模型的误差界介于最优不变模型和最差非不变模型之间。针对排列群，发现字典序规范化的覆盖数随点云维度指数增长，而希尔伯特曲线规范化则保证多项式增长，为后者的实证成功提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管不变架构是处理对称数据的标准方法，但通过群平均或规范化非不变骨架来实现不变性的策略日益受到关注，然而这些替代策略的理论泛化性质仍不清楚，亟需建立严格的理论分析框架。

Method: 通过界定覆盖数来泛化误差的理论框架，建立不同不变性实现策略之间的泛化层次关系，并针对排列群下的点云处理进行具体分析，比较字典序规范化和希尔伯特曲线规范化的覆盖数增长特性。

Result: 建立了严格的泛化层次：规范化模型的误差界最好等于结构不变模型和群平均模型的误差界，最差等于非不变基线的误差界；存在最优规范化和劣质规范化，取决于规范化的正则性；字典序规范化的覆盖数随点云维度指数增长，而希尔伯特曲线规范化保证多项式增长。

Conclusion: 首次为希尔伯特曲线序列化在先进点云架构中的实证成功提供了正式的理论依据，并通过实验支持了理论主张。

Abstract: While invariant architectures are standard for processing symmetric data, there is growing interest in achieving invariance by applying group averaging or canonization to non-invariant backbones. However, the theoretical generalization properties of these alternative strategies remain poorly understood. We introduce a theoretical framework to analyze the generalization error of these methods by bounding their covering numbers. We establish a rigorous generalization hierarchy: the error bounds of canonized models are at best equal to the error bounds of structurally invariant and group-averaged models, and at worst equal to the bounds of non-invariant baselines. Furthermore, we show that there exist optimal canonizations which attain the optimal error bounds, and poor canonizations which attain the non-invariant error bounds, and that this depends on the regularity of the canonization. Finally, applying this framework to permutation groups in point cloud processing, we rigorously prove that the covering number of lexicographical sorting grows exponentially with point cloud dimension, whereas Hilbert curve canonization guarantees polynomial growth. This provides the first formal theoretical justification for the empirical success of Hilbert curve serialization in state-of-the-art point cloud architectures. We conclude with experiments that support our theoretical claims.
  Code is available at https://github.com/yonatansverdlov/Canonization

</details>


### [91] [ACSAC: Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic with Causal Transformer Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11009)
*Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Hongtu Zhou,Hang Yu,Yanping Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ACSAC提出了一种自适应块大小的演员-评论家方法，通过因果Transformer评论家评估不同动作块的期望回报，并在每个块边界自适应选择最优块大小，解决了长时域稀疏奖励任务中固定块大小无法平衡反应速度与时间一致性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域稀疏奖励任务中，固定动作块大小无法自适应平衡反应速度（小块）与时间一致性（大块），需要任务特定的手动调参。

Method: 提出ACSAC，利用因果Transformer评论家评估不同尺寸动作块的期望回报，在每个块边界自适应选择最大化期望回报的块大小，并证明其Bellman算子是压缩映射，不动点为自适应策略的动作价值函数。

Result: 在OGBench的长时域稀疏奖励操作任务中，在离线RL和离线到在线RL设置下均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 自适应块大小策略能有效解决固定块大小的权衡问题，在长时域稀疏奖励任务中优于现有方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon, sparse-reward tasks pose a fundamental challenge for reinforcement learning, since single-step TD learning suffers from bootstrapping error accumulation across successive Bellman updates. Actor-critic methods with action chunking address this by operating over temporally extended actions, which reduce the effective horizon, enable fast value backups, and support temporally consistent exploration. However, existing methods rely on a fixed chunk size and therefore cannot adaptively balance reactivity against temporal consistency. A large fixed chunk size reduces responsiveness to new observations, while a small one produces incoherent motions, forcing task-specific tuning of the chunk size. To address this limitation, we propose Adaptive Chunk Size Actor-Critic (ACSAC). ACSAC leverages a causal Transformer critic to evaluate expected returns for action chunks of different sizes. At each chunk boundary, it adaptively selects the chunk size that maximizes the expected return, supporting flexible, state-dependent chunk sizes without task-specific tuning. We prove that the ACSAC Bellman operator is a contraction whose unique fixed point is the action-value function of the adaptive policy. Experiments on OGBench demonstrate that ACSAC achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon, sparse-reward manipulation tasks across both offline RL and offline-to-online RL settings.

</details>


### [92] [A Comparative Study of Federated Learning Aggregation Strategies under Homogeneous and Heterogeneous Data Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11010)
*Antonios Makris,Christos Dousis,Emmanouil Kritharakis,Stavros Bouras,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦学习中多种主流聚合策略在均匀和异构数据分布下进行了全面的实验比较，分析了不同聚合机制对准确率、损失和系统效率的影响，发现聚合策略的效果因数据集特征和运行条件而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习的性能受到服务器端聚合策略的显著影响，但目前缺乏对不同聚合策略在多种数据分布条件下的系统性实验比较，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在基准图像分类数据集上，对比了多种广泛使用的联邦聚合策略，分别在均匀数据分布和异构数据分布下进行实验，评估其对集中式准确率、损失以及系统级效率指标（聚合时间、训练时间、通信时间）的影响。

Result: 不同聚合策略在不同数据集和数据分布下展现出不同的权衡效果，其有效性随数据集特征和运行条件的变化而变化。

Conclusion: 聚合策略的选择需要根据具体数据集特征和运行条件进行调整，不存在适用于所有场景的最优策略。

Abstract: Federated Learning has emerged as a transformative paradigm for collaborative machine learning across distributed environments. However, its performance is strongly influenced by the aggregation strategy used to combine local model updates at the server, which directly affects learning performance, robustness, and system behavior. This work presents a comprehensive experimental comparison of widely used federated aggregation strategies under both homogeneous and heterogeneous data distributions. Using benchmark image classification datasets, we analyze how different aggregation mechanisms respond to varying degrees of data heterogeneity, examining their impact on centralized accuracy and loss, and system-level efficiency metrics, including aggregation, training, and communication time. The results demonstrate that aggregation strategies exhibit distinct trade-offs across datasets and data distributions, with their effectiveness varying according to dataset characteristics and operating conditions.

</details>


### [93] [LoopUS: Recasting Pretrained LLMs into Looped Latent Refinement Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11011)
*Taekhyun Park,Yongjae Lee,Dohee Kim,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopUS是一个将标准预训练LLM转换为循环架构的后训练框架，通过将模型分解为编码器、循环推理块和解码器，并引入选择性门控、深度监督和自适应早退等机制，在不需从头训练或生成更长推理链的情况下提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环计算方法要么需要从头训练循环模型，要么需要对预训练模型进行破坏性改造，计算成本高昂且可能损害预训练能力。

Method: LoopUS将预训练LLM重构成编码器-循环推理块-解码器架构，包含四个核心组件：(1)基于分阶段表示动态的块分解；(2)输入依赖的选择性门控以缓解隐状态漂移；(3)随机深度监督实现长递归范围内的内存高效学习；(4)用于自适应早退的置信度头。

Result: 通过稳定的隐状态循环，LoopUS在不延长生成轨迹或需要从头循环训练的情况下，提升了推理导向的性能。

Conclusion: LoopUS提供了一种低成本、非破坏性的方案，将标准预训练LLM转换为循环架构以提升推理能力，避免了现有方法的计算开销和性能退化问题。

Abstract: Looped computation shows promise in improving the reasoning-oriented performance of LLMs by scaling test-time compute. However, existing approaches typically require either training recurrent models from scratch or applying disruptive retrofits, which involve substantial computational costs and may compromise pretrained capabilities. To address these limitations, we introduce \textbf{Looped Depth Up-Scaling} (LoopUS), a post-training framework that converts a standard pretrained LLM into a looped architecture. As a key technical contribution, LoopUS recasts the pretrained LLM into an encoder, a looped reasoning block, and a decoder. It operationalizes this latent-refinement architecture through four core components: (1) block decomposition, guided by staged representation dynamics; (2) an input-dependent selective gate to mitigate hidden-state drift; (3) random deep supervision for memory-efficient learning over long recursive horizons; and (4) a confidence head for adaptive early exiting. Collectively, these mechanisms transform a standard non-looped model into a looped form while stabilizing it against both computational bottlenecks and representation collapse. Through stable latent looping, LoopUS improves reasoning-oriented performance without extending the generated traces or requiring recurrent training from scratch. For more details, see https://thrillcrazyer.github.io/LoopUS

</details>


### [94] [Simpson's Paradox in Behavioral Curves: How Aggregation Distorts Parametric Models of User Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11017)
*Chao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了行为曲线建模中聚合数据导致的辛普森悖论：个体用户峰值曝光量约11次，但聚合后显示为34次（Goodreads平台3倍偏差），亚马逊电子商品达5.3倍。这种偏差源于生存偏差而非聚合本身，MovieLens-25M作为阴性对照证实了该机制。研究开发了合成零校准方法以解决32%的个体分类假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐、广告等领域的行为曲线建模（拟合参数函数于参与度-曝光数据）普遍采用聚合方法，但未意识到聚合可能引入系统性偏差。研究者希望通过实证发现这种偏差的存在性、规模及根本原因。

Method: 跨平台实证分析：Goodreads（330万用户，9种体裁）、亚马逊电子商品（1800万评论）、MovieLens-25M（作为阴性对照）。使用参数化行为曲线拟合，对比个体级与聚合级参数估计，并开发合成零校准方法校正分类偏差。

Result: Goodreads个体用户峰值出现在约11次曝光处，而聚合曲线峰值在约34次曝光处（3倍偏差）。亚马逊电子商品显示5.3倍偏差。MovieLens-25M（D≈1）作为阴性对照证实偏差源于生存偏差而非聚合本身。个体分类存在32%假阳性率。

Conclusion: 聚合行为曲线存在系统性的辛普森悖论偏差，主要由差异流失（生存偏差）驱动，而非聚合操作本身。该发现适用于任何在差异流失条件下从聚合曲线估计个体行为参数的场景。

Abstract: Behavioral curve modeling -- fitting parametric functions to engagement-versus-exposure data -- is standard practice in recommendation, advertising, and clinical dosing. We show that aggregation introduces a systematic distortion: Simpson's paradox in behavioral curves. On Goodreads (3.3M users, 9 genres), individual users peak at n* approximately 11 exposures while the aggregate peaks at n* approximately 34 -- a 3x gap driven by survival bias. Amazon Electronics (18M reviews) shows a 5.3x distortion. MovieLens-25M (D approximately 1) serves as a negative control, confirming that survival bias -- not aggregation per se -- is the operative mechanism. The distortion is robust to category granularity, engagement operationalization, and classifier calibration. We develop Synthetic Null Calibration to address a 32% false positive rate in per-user classification. Our findings apply wherever individual behavioral parameters are estimated from aggregate curves under differential attrition.

</details>


### [95] [Efficient LLM Reasoning via Variational Posterior Guidance with Efficiency Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11019)
*Zizhao Chen,Yuying Li,Siting Lin,Lianxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VPG-EA框架，通过变分推断和后验分布引导，突破强化学习方法在压缩推理链时的高质量样本采样瓶颈，在1.5B和7B规模模型上分别提升综合效率指标ε³达8.73%和12.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂推理中依赖思维链，但"过度思考"现象严重降低推理效率。现有强化学习方法通过设计精妙的奖励函数来压缩推理链，导致高质量样本在探索空间中极为稀疏，形成先验策略的采样瓶颈。

Method: 将高效推理形式化为变分推断问题，提出效率感知的证据下界作为理论基础。采用参数共享的双流架构同时实例化后验分布和先验策略；通过交叉视角评估过滤伪高效路径，利用变分蒸馏将后验的高效模式单向迁移至先验策略。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和7B规模上，VPG-EA相较于各自规模的最强基线，综合效率指标ε³分别提升8.73%和12.37%。

Conclusion: 后验分布能在推理时不可用的情况下通过变分蒸馏突破采样瓶颈，显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Although large language models rely on chain-of-thought for complex reasoning, the overthinking phenomenon severely degrades inference efficiency. Existing reinforcement learning methods compress reasoning chains by designing elaborate reward functions, which renders high-quality samples extremely sparse in the exploration space and creates a sampling bottleneck for the prior policy. Inspired by cognitive science, we theoretically prove that a posterior distribution guided by reference answers achieves higher expected utility than the prior distribution, thus capable of breaking through the sampling bottleneck of high-quality samples. However, the posterior distribution is unavailable during inference. To this end, we formalize efficient reasoning as a variational inference problem and introduce an efficiency-aware evidence lower bound as the theoretical foundation. Based on this, we propose the VPG-EA framework. It adopts a parameter-shared dual-stream architecture to instantiate both the posterior distribution and the prior policy; after filtering out pseudo-efficient paths via cross-view evaluation, it unidirectionally transfers the posterior's efficient patterns to the prior policy through variational distillation. Experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and 7B scales demonstrate that VPG-EA improves the comprehensive efficiency metric epsilon cubed by 8.73% and 12.37% over the strongest baselines on each model size, respectively.

</details>


### [96] [Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning: Explicit Dual Ascent using Local Policy Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11020)
*Anish Diwan,Davide Tateo,Christopher E. Mower,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为信任区域逆强化学习（TRIRL）的算法，通过信任区域方法在无需每轮完全求解RL问题的情况下，实现奖励函数和策略的单调改进，弥合了经典IRL与对抗方法的差距，在多任务上性能提升2.4倍，且学得的奖励函数能泛化到系统动力学变化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典IRL（对偶上升法）保证单调性能改进但每轮需完全求解RL问题，计算成本高；对抗方法虽避免此成本但存在训练不稳定和缺乏单调改进保证的问题。本文旨在桥接两者，在无需完全求解RL问题的前提下，实现奖励函数和策略的单调改进。

Method: 核心理论洞察：奖励函数更新的信任区域最优策略，可以是在同一方向上更小更新量的全局最优策略。基于此，算法（TRIRL）通过围绕当前策略进行局部搜索来显式优化对偶目标，避免了每轮完全求解RL问题，同时保持单调改进。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的任务上，TRIRL在聚合四分位间均值上比最先进的模仿学习方法性能提升2.4倍，且学得的奖励函数能够泛化到系统动力学变化场景。

Conclusion: TRIRL成功结合了经典IRL的单调改进保证和对抗方法的高效性，避免了训练不稳定性，学得的奖励函数符合传统IRL定义——可被全局优化以匹配专家演示。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) is typically formulated as maximizing entropy subject to matching the distribution of expert trajectories. Classical (dual-ascent) IRL guarantees monotonic performance improvement but requires fully solving an RL problem each iteration to compute dual gradients. More recent adversarial methods avoid this cost at the expense of stability and monotonic dual improvement, by directly optimizing the primal problem and using a discriminator to provide rewards. In this work, we bridge the gap between these approaches by enabling monotonic improvement of the reward function and policy without having to fully solve an RL problem at every iteration. Our key theoretical insight is that a trust-region-optimal policy for a reward function update can be globally optimal for a smaller update in the same direction. This smaller update allows us to explicitly optimize the dual objective while only relying on a local search around the current policy. In doing so, our approach avoids the training instabilities of adversarial methods, offers monotonic performance improvement, and learns a reward function in the traditional sense of IRL--one that can be globally optimized to match expert demonstrations. Our proposed algorithm, Trust Region Inverse Reinforcement Learning (TRIRL), outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning methods across multiple challenging tasks by a factor of 2.4x in terms of aggregate inter-quartile mean, while recovering reward functions that generalize to system dynamics shifts.

</details>


### [97] [Primal Generation, Dual Judgment: Self-Training from Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11299)
*Yizhu Jiao,Ruixiang Zhang,Richard Bai,Jiawei Han,Ronan Collobert,Yizhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DuST（双重自训练）框架，利用模型自身采样程序在沙盒执行中产生的正确/错误结构信息（即“双重判断空间”）进行自训练。训练目标纯粹是判别式——模型从不因生成正确程序而直接获得奖励。在5个模型（4B-30B）上，DuST一致提升Best-of-4测试时扩展性能，且训练后的单次采样表现可媲美基线模型的Best-of-4性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代码生成训练仅依赖稀疏的执行反馈（通过/不通过），测试时扩展虽能通过多采样和对比提升推理效果，但对比信息在推理后被丢弃。本文认为这些信息定义了更丰富的“双重判断空间”，可提供比单次成功/失败更强的训练信号。

Method: DuST框架：1）从模型当前分布采样候选程序；2）通过沙盒执行标注候选程序；3）保留同时包含成功和失败的候选组；4）使用GRPO训练模型根据执行正确性对候选进行排序。训练目标纯粹是判别式的，不直接奖励生成正确程序。

Result: 在5个模型（两个家族，4B-30B三个规模）上，DuST一致提升Best-of-4测试时扩展性能。在Qwen3-30B-Thinking上，判断质量提升+6.2 NDCG，单样本pass@1提升+3.1，Best-of-4准确率提升+4.1。训练后的单次rollout匹配基线模型的Best-of-4性能。

Conclusion: 双重自训练同时提升了判断和生成能力。SFT在相同排序数据上只提升判断而不提升生成，证实了on-policy RL是将双重空间学习迁移回原始空间生成的机制。

Abstract: Code generation is typically trained in the primal space of programs: a model produces a candidate solution and receives sparse execution feedback, often a single pass/fail bit. Test-time scaling enriches the inference procedure by sampling multiple candidates and judging among them, but the comparative information this process reveals is discarded after inference. We argue that this information defines a dual judgment space that provides a far richer training signal: the model learns not from an isolated success or failure, but from the relative correctness structure across its own plausible attempts, identifying which succeed, which fail, and what distinguishes them. We introduce DuST (Dual Self-Training), a framework for self-training from the dual judgment space. DuST samples candidate programs from the model's own distribution, labels them through sandbox execution, retains groups containing both successes and failures, and trains the model to rank candidates by execution correctness using GRPO. The objective is purely discriminative: the model is never directly rewarded for generating correct programs. Dual self-training improves both judgment and generation. Across five models spanning two families and three scales (4B to 30B), DuST consistently improves Best-of-4 test-time scaling on LiveCodeBench. For Qwen3-30B-Thinking on LiveCodeBench v6, judgment quality improves by +6.2 NDCG, single-sample pass@1 improves by +3.1, and Best-of-4 accuracy improves by +4.1. The trained model's single rollout matches the base model's Best-of-4 performance. SFT on the same ranking data improves judgment without improving generation, confirming that on-policy RL is the mechanism that transfers dual-space learning back into primal generation.

</details>


### [98] [ASD-Bench: A Four-Axis Comprehensive Benchmark of AI Models for Autism Spectrum Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11091)
*Shubhankit Singh,Hassan Shaikh,Kuldeep Raghuwanshi,Keshav Bulia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASD-Bench，一个系统性的表格基准测试框架，用于评估机器学习、深度学习和基础模型在不同年龄组（儿童1-11岁、青少年12-16岁、成人17-64岁）的自闭症谱系障碍筛查表现。基于4,068份AQ-10记录，从预测性能、校准、可解释性和对抗鲁棒性四个维度进行评估，并引入启发式聚合惩罚（HAP）指标。结果显示成人组性能最佳（10/17模型达完美F1和AUC），青少年组最具挑战性（F1上限0.837），特征重要性随年龄阶段变化，且准确率与校准之间存在解耦现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化ASD筛查工具存在三个主要局限：单一架构评估、仅限轴评估、以及几乎只关注成人队列，忽视了早期干预中关键的年龄特异性诊断模式。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供覆盖多年龄段、多模型架构的系统性基准评估。

Method: 构建ASD-Bench基准框架，在三个年龄队列上评估11种模型配置（含XGBoost、AdaBoost、随机森林、逻辑回归、MLP、TabNet、TabTransformer、FT-Transformer、TabPFN v2），从预测性能、校准、可解释性和对抗鲁棒性四个维度分析。引入HAP（启发式聚合惩罚）指标，对假阴性施以更高惩罚并纳入交叉验证方差。使用4,068份AQ-10记录进行实验。

Result: 成人组10/17模型达到完美F1和AUC；青少年组最具挑战性（F1上限0.837），儿童组F1上限0.915。特征重要性随年龄变化：儿童以A9（社交动机）为主，青少年以A5（模式识别）为主，成人呈现更平坦的特征轮廓。准确率与校准解耦：AdaBoost在成人组F1=1.000但ECE=0.302。提供不同年龄段的部署建议。

Conclusion: 本文证明多年龄组、多维度评估对ASD筛查工具开发的重要性。单一指标评估不足以支持临床AI部署。特征层级随年龄的转变提示发育性社交掩饰现象。所有发现应视为基于问卷标签的概念验证，而非临床验证诊断效能。

Abstract: Automated ASD screening tools remain limited by single-architecture evaluations, axis-restricted assessment, and near-exclusive focus on adult cohorts, obscuring age-specific diagnostic patterns critical for early intervention. We introduce ASD-Bench, a systematic tabular benchmark evaluating ML, deep learning, and foundation model configurations across three age cohorts (children 1-11 yr, adolescents 12-16 yr, adults 17-64 yr) on four axes: predictive performance, calibration, interpretability, and adversarial robustness. Applied to a curated v3 dataset of 4,068 AQ-10 records, our benchmark spans classical models (XGBoost, AdaBoost, Random Forest, Logistic Regression), neural networks (MLP), deep tabular transformers (TabNet, TabTransformer, FT-Transformer), and TabPFN v2. We introduce the Heuristic Aggregate Penalty (HAP): a cost-sensitive metric penalising false negatives more heavily and incorporating cross-validation variance for deployment stability. Adult classification yields high performance (10/17 models achieve perfect F1 and AUC), while adolescents present a harder task (F1 ceiling 0.837 vs. 0.915 for children). Feature hierarchies shift across cohorts: A9 (social motivation) dominates for children, A5 (pattern recognition) leads for adolescents, and adults exhibit a flatter importance profile consistent with developmental social masking. Accuracy and calibration are dissociated: AdaBoost achieves F1=1.000 on adults with ECE=0.302, confirming single-metric evaluation is insufficient for clinical AI. Cohort-specific deployment recommendations are provided. All findings should be interpreted as proof-of-concept evidence on questionnaire-derived labels rather than clinically validated diagnostic performance.

</details>


### [99] [Language Modeling with Hyperspherical Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11125)
*Justin Deschenaux,Caglar Gulcehre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S-FLM，一种在超球面中运行的潜在连续流语言模型，通过旋转向量避免独热编码的高维开销，在大词汇量推理任务上显著提升性能，缩小了与掩码扩散模型在标准温度采样下的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散语言模型因因子化分布表达能力弱于自回归模型，而连续流语言模型虽能避免因子化采样，但操作独热向量导致训练成本高昂，且在高维空间中加高斯噪声缺乏语义解释性。

Method: 提出S-FLM，在超球面S^{d-1}上学习速度场，通过旋转向量生成序列，使用交叉熵损失训练，避免了独热向量的物化开销。

Result: S-FLM在大词汇量推理任务上显著优于先前连续流语言模型，在标准温度采样(T=1)下缩小了与掩码扩散模型的差距，但在优化低温度解码(T=0.1)下仍存在差距。

Conclusion: 超球面潜空间中的连续流语言模型是处理大词汇量推理任务的有效方案，但在低温度解码场景下仍需进一步优化以完全匹配掩码扩散模型。

Abstract: Discrete Diffusion Language Models progressed rapidly as an alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, motivated by their parallel generation abilities. However, for tractability, discrete diffusion models sample from a factorized distribution, which is less expressive than AR. Recent Flow Language Models (FLMs) apply continuous flows to language, transporting noise to data with a deterministic ODE that avoids factorized sampling. FLMs operate on one-hot vectors whose dimension scales with the vocabulary size, making FLMs costly to train. Moreover, since all distinct one-hot embeddings are equidistant in $\ell_2$, adding Gaussian noise does not have a clear semantic interpretation (unlike images, where Gaussian noise progressively degrades structure). We introduce $\mathbb{S}$-FLM, a latent FLM in the hypersphere. $\mathbb{S}$-FLM generates sequences by rotating vectors in $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ along a velocity field learned with cross-entropy, avoiding the overhead of materializing one-hot vectors. Previous FLMs match AR in Generative Perplexity (Gen.\ PPL), but samples with high likelihood are not necessarily correct in verifiable domains such as math and code. $\mathbb{S}$-FLM substantially improves continuous flow language models on large-vocabulary reasoning and closes the gap to masked diffusion under standard-temperature sampling ($T=1$), while a gap remains under optimized low-temperature ($T=0.1$) decoding.

</details>


### [100] [Steerable Neural ODEs on Homogeneous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11133)
*Emma Andersdotter,Daniel Persson,Fredrik Ohlsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在同质空间 $M=G/H$ 上的可操控神经常微分方程（Steerable NODEs），通过将特征解释为伴随向量丛的截面，并利用平行输运描述其演化，实现了在齐次空间上学习连续时间等变动力学的几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流形神经常微分方程（NODE）缺乏对特征向量在局部对称群 $H$ 下变换的建模能力，无法保证 $G$-等变性。本文旨在建立一种统一的几何框架，将NODE扩展到齐次空间，使其能够处理向量值特征并保持等变性质。

Method: 将特征解释为 $M=G/H$ 上伴随向量丛的截面，其演化由平行输运描述。模型由两个耦合的ODE组成：一个控制 $M$ 上的流方程，另一个控制特征的操纵方程。要求生成流的向量场和支配平行输运的联络均为 $G$-不变的，从而保证 $G$-等变性。

Result: 展示了可操控NODE能够纳入现有的NODE模型和连续归一化流在Lie群上的应用，验证了框架的通用性和理论基础。

Conclusion: 该框架为在齐次空间上学习连续时间等变动力学的向量值特征提供了几何基础，能够处理更广泛的对称性结构。

Abstract: We introduce steerable neural ordinary differential equations on homogeneous spaces $M=G/H$. These models constitute a novel geometric extension of manifold neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs) that transport associated feature vectors transforming under the local symmetry group $H$. We interpret features as sections of associated vector bundles over $M$, and describe their evolution as parallel transport. This results in a coupled system of ODEs consisting of a flow equation on $M$ and a steering equation acting on features. We show that steerable NODEs are $G$-equivariant whenever the vector field generating the flow and the connection governing parallel transport are both $G$-invariant. Furthermore, we demonstrate how steerable NODEs incorporate existing NODE models and continuous normalizing flows on Lie groups. Our framework provides the geometric foundation for learning continuous-time equivariant dynamics of general vector-valued features on homogeneous spaces.

</details>


### [101] [CORE: Cyclic Orthotope Relation Embedding for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11159)
*Yingqi Zeng,Luying Wang,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE模型，通过将实体和关系嵌入到无边界的环面流形上，并用循环正交区域表示关系，解决了基于几何区域的知识图谱补全模型存在的绝对边界约束和区域无限膨胀问题，在四个基准数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于区域的知识图谱补全模型在优化过程中存在绝对边界约束（导致梯度传导不畅）或关系区域无限膨胀的问题，亟需一种既能避免边界约束又能防止区域无限扩张的表示方法。

Method: 提出CORE模型，将实体和关系嵌入到无边界环面流形上；用循环正交区域（cyclic orthotopes）表示关系，使区域可无缝环绕空间边界以保障梯度平滑传导；引入自适应宽度正则化防止无条件区域扩张。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CORE达到了高度竞争性的性能，显著提升了密集语义环境下的链接预测准确率。

Conclusion: 理论分析证明CORE能够捕获子关系、交集等多种复杂关系模式，实验验证其在知识图谱补全任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph completion (KGC) aims to automatically infer missing facts in multi-relational data by mapping entities and relations into continuous representation spaces. Recent region-based embedding models have shown great promise in capturing complex logical patterns by representing relations as geometric regions. However, these models inevitably suffer from absolute boundary constraints during optimization. Conversely, without such constraints, relation regions expand indefinitely. To address the limitation, we propose \textbf{CORE} (Cyclic Orthotope Relation Embedding), a novel KGC model that embeds entities and relations onto a boundary-less torus manifold.CORE represents relations as cyclic orthotopes on the torus manifold, allowing regions to seamlessly wrap around spatial boundaries to ensure smooth gradient conduction. Furthermore, an adaptive width regularization is introduced to prevent unconditional region expansion. Theoretical analysis proves that CORE can capture various complex relation patterns such as subsumption and intersection. Extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that CORE achieves highly competitive performance, significantly improving link prediction accuracy in dense semantic environments.

</details>


### [102] [Interpretability Can Be Actionable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11161)
*Hadas Orgad,Fazl Barez,Tal Haklay,Isabelle Lee,Marius Mosbach,Anja Reusch,Naomi Saphra,Byron Wallace,Sarah Wiegreffe,Eric Wong,Ian Tenney,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张可解释性研究应通过"可行动性"来评估——即见解能否支持具体决策和干预，并提出了具体的评估框架和五个关键应用领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释性研究快速增长但未能转化为实际影响，缺乏有效的评估标准是核心问题。

Method: 从具体性（concreteness）和验证（validation）两个维度定义可行动的可解释性，分析障碍，识别五个有独特优势的应用领域，并提出评估框架。

Result: 提出了以可行动性为核心目标的评估框架，并识别了五个关键应用领域。

Conclusion: 应将可行动性确立为可解释性研究的核心目标，而非否定探索性研究。

Abstract: Interpretability aims to explain the behavior of deep neural networks. Despite rapid growth, there is mounting concern that much of this work has not translated into practical impact, raising questions about its relevance and utility. This position paper argues that the central missing ingredient is not new methods, but evaluation criteria: interpretability should be evaluated by actionability--the extent to which insights enable concrete decisions and interventions beyond interpretability research itself. We define actionable interpretability along two dimensions--concreteness and validation--and analyze the barriers currently preventing real-world impact. To address these barriers, we identify five domains where interpretability offers unique leverage and present a framework for actionable interpretability with evaluation criteria aligned with practical outcomes. Our goal is not to downplay exploratory research, but to establish actionability as a core objective of interpretability research.

</details>


### [103] [Unlearning with Asymmetric Sources: Improved Unlearning-Utility Trade-off with Public Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11170)
*Ahmed Mehdi Inane,Vincent Quirion,Gintare Karolina Dzugaite,Ioannis Mitliagkas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非对称朗之万遗忘（ALU）框架，通过注入公开数据将认证机器遗忘的噪声成本降低O(1/n_pub²)倍，在保证严格计算优势的同时，实现大规模数据删除下的高效用保持，并首次分析了公开-私有数据分布偏移的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于噪声的认证机器遗忘存在硬天花板：认证遗忘所需噪声量级通常会破坏模型效用，尤其在大规模删除请求场景下。虽然利用公开数据是差分隐私中缓解这一张力的标准技术，但其在遗忘问题中的作用尚未被探索。

Method: 提出Asymmetric Langevin Unlearning (ALU)框架，通过注入公开数据来缓解遗忘的隐私成本。理论上证明公开数据注入将遗忘成本降低O(1/n_pub²)因子，并在分布偏移的现实设定下显式刻画了公开与私有数据源之间偏移对效用的影响。

Result: ALU实现了常数比例数据集的批量遗忘（标准对称方法在此场景下变得不可行），同时保持高效用。基于变分Rényi散度和成员推断攻击的实证评估证实，在合理的分布偏移下，ALU能有效阻止隐私攻击并保持效用。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了新的控制机制：从业者可以通过增加公开数据量来缓解对高噪声（及其伴随的效用损失）的需求。严格保证了相对于重训练的计算优势。

Abstract: Noise-based certified machine unlearning currently faces a hard ceiling: the noise magnitude required to certify unlearning typically destroys model utility, particularly for large-scale deletion requests.
  While leveraging public data is a standard technique in differential privacy to relax this tension, its role in unlearning remains unexplored. We address this gap by introducing Asymmetric Langevin Unlearning (ALU), a framework that uses public data to mitigate privacy costs. We prove that public data injection suppresses the unlearning cost by a factor of $O(1/n_{\mathrm{pub}}^2)$, guaranteeing a strict computational advantage over retraining. This establishes a new control mechanism: practitioners can mitigate the need for high noise-and the associated utility loss-by increasing the volume of public data. Crucially, we analyze the realistic setting of distribution mismatch, explicitly characterizing how shifts between public and private sources impact utility.
  We show that ALU enables mass unlearning of constant dataset fractions -- a regime where standard symmetric methods become impractical -- while maintaining high utility. Empirical evaluations using variational Rényi divergence and membership inference attacks confirm that ALU effectively thwarts privacy attacks while preserving utility under reasonable distribution shifts.

</details>


### [104] [Optimistic Dual Averaging Unifies Modern Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11172)
*Thomas Pethick,Wanyun Xie,Roman Machacek,Volkan Cevher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SODA框架，统一了Muon、Lion、AdEMAMix和NAdam等现代优化器，并提供了一个实用的包装器，通过理论驱动的1/k衰减调度消除权重衰减调参，实验表明无需额外超参数调优即可持续提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（如Muon、Lion、AdEMAMix、NAdam）虽然性能优异，但缺乏统一的视角来理解其内在联系，且权重衰减调参耗时长、影响大。

Method: 提出SODA框架——乐观对偶平均法的推广，将现有优化器统一为乐观实例；并设计SODA包装器，基于理论推导的1/k衰减调度自动处理权重衰减。

Result: 在多种规模和训练周期的实验中都取得了性能提升，且无需任何额外的超参数调优。

Conclusion: SODA提供了优化器的统一理论视角，其包装器可即插即用地提升基础优化器性能，权重衰减的1/k调度具有理论保证和实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce SODA, a generalization of Optimistic Dual Averaging, which provides a common perspective on state-of-the-art optimizers like Muon, Lion, AdEMAMix and NAdam, showing that they can all be viewed as optimistic instances of this framework. Based on this framing, we propose a practical SODA wrapper for any base optimizer that eliminates weight decay tuning through a theoretically-grounded $1/k$ decay schedule. Empirical results across various scales and training horizons show that SODA consistently improves performance without any additional hyperparameter tuning.

</details>


### [105] [Oversmoothing as Representation Degeneracy in Neural Sheaf Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11178)
*Arif Dönmez,Axel Mosig,Ellen Fritsche,Katharina Koch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将神经层扩散（NSD）中的细胞层视为关联箭图的表示，证明了扩散极限下的调和空间具有直和分解结构，并将过平滑解释为表示退化（向低复杂度直和项坍缩）。基于几何不变量理论引入矩映射正则化器以平衡表示几何，并揭示了等维架构中可学习稳定参数的不可行性，而非均匀维度的架构能消除这一障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NSD的扩散极限已知收敛到全局截面空间，但该调和空间的表示论结构尚不明确。需要从代数角度理解过平滑现象，并建立更系统的理论框架。

Method: 将细胞层等价为关联箭图的表示，利用箭图表示的直和分解刻画调和空间结构；借助几何不变量理论（GIT）的稳定性和矩映射原理设计正则化器；分析等维/非等维架构下可学习稳定性参数的可行性。

Result: 过平滑等价于表示退化；矩映射正则化器能偏向平衡表示几何；等维架构（d_v=d_e）存在结构障碍，非均匀维度可消除该障碍；实验验证了打破维度对称性可降低方差或改善验证行为。

Conclusion: 该框架将过平滑重新定义为学习到的表示几何中的退化现象，为理解NSD的表示学习行为提供了代数视角，并给出了实用的架构设计指导。

Abstract: Neural Sheaf Diffusion (NSD) generalizes diffusion-based Graph Neural Networks by replacing scalar graph Laplacians with sheaf Laplacians whose learned restriction maps define a task-adapted geometry. While the diffusion limit of NSD is known to be the space of global sections, the representation-theoretic structure of this harmonic space remains largely implicit. We develop a quiver-theoretic interpretation of NSD by identifying cellular sheaves on graphs with representations of the associated incidence quiver. Under this correspondence, learned sheaf geometries become points in a finite-dimensional representation space. We show that direct-sum decompositions of the underlying incidence-quiver representation induce decompositions of the harmonic space reached in the diffusion limit. This gives an algebraic interpretation of oversmoothing as representation degeneration: learned sheaves may collapse toward low-complexity summands whose global sections fail to preserve discriminative information. Building on this viewpoint, we connect sheaf diffusion to stability and moment-map principles from Geometric Invariant Theory. We introduce moment-map-inspired regularizers that bias restriction maps toward balanced representation geometries, and identify a structural obstruction in equal-stalk architectures: when $d_v = d_e$, admissibility for learnable stability parameters forces the trivial all-object summand onto a stability wall. Non-uniform stalk dimensions remove this obstruction, making adaptive stability meaningful. Experiments on heterophilic benchmarks are consistent with this mechanism: breaking stalk symmetry can reduce variance or improve validation behavior, and adaptive stability becomes more effective in selected rectangular settings. Overall, our framework reframes oversmoothing as a degeneration phenomenon in the representation geometry underlying learned sheaf diffusion.

</details>


### [106] [Behavioral Mode Discovery for Fine-tuning Multimodal Generative Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11387)
*Alberta Longhini,David Emukpere,Jean-Michel Renders,Seungsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督模式发现框架，通过在生成式策略的RL微调中引入互信息作为内在奖励，在提升任务成功率的同时保持行为多模态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式策略（如扩散策略）的RL微调方法虽然能提升任务性能，但常导致行为多样性坍塌至单一奖励最大化模式，丢失了动作分布的多模态特性。

Method: 提出无监督模式发现框架，自动挖掘生成式策略中潜在的行为模式，利用互信息作为内在奖励来正则化RL微调过程，平衡任务成功与行为多样性。

Result: 在机器人操作任务上，该方法相比传统微调方法取得了更高的任务成功率，同时保留了更丰富的多模态动作分布。

Conclusion: 通过互信息正则化的方式能有效缓解RL微调中的模式坍塌问题，实现任务性能与行为多样性的双赢。

Abstract: We address the problem of fine-tuning pre-trained generative policies with reinforcement learning (RL) while preserving the multimodality of their action distributions. Existing methods for RL fine-tuning of generative policies (e.g., diffusion policies) improve task performance but often collapse diverse behaviors into a single reward-maximizing mode. To mitigate this issue, we propose an unsupervised mode discovery framework that uncovers latent behavioral modes within generative policies. The discovered modes enable the use of mutual information as an intrinsic reward, regularizing RL fine-tuning to enhance task success while maintaining behavioral diversity. Experiments on robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms conventional fine-tuning approaches, achieving higher success rates and preserving richer multimodal action distributions.

</details>


### [107] [Muon is Not That Special: Random or Inverted Spectra Work Just as Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11181)
*Zakhar Shumaylov,Nathaël Da Costa,Peter Zaika,Bálint Mucsányi,Alex Massucco,Yoav Gelberg,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Yarin Gal,Philipp Hennig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出Freon和Kaon优化器，挑战了非欧几何优化（如Muon）的成功归因于精确几何结构的观点，证明优化性能主要由局部对齐和下降势控制，而非全局几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前对Muon优化器成功的几何解释（即将其归因于非欧几何结构与二阶方法或线性最小化预言理论），探究优化性能的真正驱动因素。

Method: 1) 提出Freon优化器族，基于Schatten（拟）范数，采用QDWH迭代近似，可自然插值SGD与Muon并外推至拟范数区域；2) 提出Kaon优化器，用随机噪声替代奇异值，缺乏几何结构但性能匹配Muon；3) 通过随机特征模型分析优化器的对齐和下降势两个局部量。

Result: 1) GPT-2上最优Schatten参数严格位于拟范数区域，无法由任何酉不变LMO表示；2) Kaon匹配Muon性能并保留经典收敛保证；3) 优化性能由对齐和下降势这两个局部量控制，Muon成功的原因在于保证了步长最优性而非追踪理想全局几何。

Conclusion: 精确几何结构并非影响优化性能的关键因素，优化性能由局部对齐和下降势主导；Muon的成功源自步长最优性而非全局几何结构。

Abstract: The recent empirical success of the Muon optimizer has renewed interest in non-Euclidean optimization, typically justified by similarities with second-order methods, and linear minimization oracle (LMO) theory. In this paper, we challenge this geometric narrative through three contributions, demonstrating that precise geometric structure is not the key factor affecting optimization performance. First, we introduce Freon, a family of optimizers based on Schatten (quasi-)norms, powered by a novel, provably optimal QDWH-based iterative approximation. Freon naturally interpolates between SGD and Muon, while smoothly extrapolating into the quasi-norm regime. Empirically, the best-performing Schatten parameters for GPT-2 lie strictly within the quasi-norm regime, and thus cannot be represented by any unitarily invariant LMO. Second, noting that Freon performs well across a wide range of exponents, we introduce Kaon, an absurd optimizer that replaces singular values with random noise. Despite lacking any coherent geometric structure, Kaon matches Muon's performance and retains classical convergence guarantees, proving that strict adherence to a precise geometry is practically irrelevant. Third, having shown that geometry is not the primary driver of performance, we demonstrate it is instead controlled by two local quantities: alignment and descent potential. Ultimately, each optimizer must tune its step size around these two quantities. While their dynamics are difficult to predict a-priori, evaluating them within a stochastic random feature model yields a precise insight: Muon succeeds not by tracking an ideal global geometry, but by guaranteeing step-size optimality.

</details>


### [108] [CATS: Cascaded Adaptive Tree Speculation for Memory-Limited LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11186)
*Yuning Han,Yangchenchen Jin,Dylan Zhao,Jingwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CATS是一种针对内存受限设备（如边缘平台）的自推测解码框架，通过级联验证和校正机制，在保持峰值内存占用与目标模型相同的前提下，实现了最高5.08倍的实际加速，并比现有最优方法提升达1.45倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法假设高带宽内存（HBM）足够同时容纳目标模型和辅助草稿模型，但在内存受限的边缘设备上这一假设不成立，需要新的解码方案来缓解内存带宽瓶颈。

Method: 提出CATS框架，基于内存预算和参数卸载模式，在内存受限设备上执行级联验证与校正（cascaded verification and correction），峰值内存占用与仅运行目标模型时相同。

Result: 在真实边缘设备上，CATS在五个基准测试中实现最高5.08倍实际加速，生成质量无下降，并在边缘内存约束下比现有最优方法提升1.45倍。

Conclusion: CATS有效解决了内存受限设备上大语言模型自回归解码的内存带宽瓶颈问题，通过自推测解码和级联验证机制实现了显著加速，且不牺牲生成质量。

Abstract: Auto-regressive decoding in Large Language Models (LLMs) is inherently memory-bound: every generation step requires loading the model weights and intermediate results from memory (e.g., High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) for GPU servers), making throughput bottlenecked by memory bandwidth rather than compute. Speculative decoding addresses this by enabling parallel verification of multiple draft tokens, effectively amortizing the cost of each target-model call. However, existing speculative decoding methods are designed under the assumption that HBM is sufficiently large to hold both the target model and an auxiliary draft model simultaneously -- an assumption that breaks down on memory-constrained devices such as edge platforms with limited DRAM. We analyze the inference bottleneck in this memory-limited regime and propose CATS, a self-speculative decoding framework that conducts cascaded verification and correction based on the memory budget and parameter offloading patterns on memory-limited devices. This design maximizes token acceptance rate and end-to-end speedup while keeping the peak memory footprint on the device equal to that of the target model alone. We evaluate CATS on different models across five benchmarks on real edge devices. CATS can achieve a wall-clock speedup of up to 5.08x with no degradation in generation quality, outperforming the SOTA method by up to 1.45x under edge memory constraints.

</details>


### [109] [Deep Learning for Protein Complex Prediction and Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11189)
*Ziwei Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文利用深度学习研究了蛋白质复合物结构建模与设计中的两个核心问题：捕捉蛋白质结构层级特性的领域特定架构，以及高效搜索蛋白质复合物序列空间以识别相互作用同源物和改进复合物结构预测与序列设计的搜索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质复合物结构的精确建模与设计是计算结构生物学中的核心问题，对于理解细胞功能和开发治疗药物具有重要意义。

Method: 采用深度学习方法，一方面开发能够捕捉蛋白质结构层级特性的领域特定架构，另一方面设计能够高效搜索蛋白质复合物序列空间的搜索算法。

Result: 通过领域特定架构捕获蛋白质结构层级特性，并通过搜索算法高效探索序列空间，从而识别相互作用同源物以改进复合物结构预测和设计蛋白质序列。

Conclusion: 该研究通过深度学习方法在蛋白质复合物结构建模与设计方面取得了进展，为理解细胞功能和开发治疗药物提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: Accurately modeling and designing protein complex structures is a central problem in computational structural biology, with broad implications for understanding cellular function and developing therapeutics.
