Motivation: 极端事件（如野火）预测面临两大挑战：1）事件稀疏导致的长尾分布问题，标准学习目标会忽视少数类（火灾）；2）模型在分布漂移下性能退化。现有方法难以同时应对长尾分布和环境变化。

Method: 1）通过k近邻检索构建与目标环境分布对齐的局部数据集；2）在局部流形上进行混合微调，联合监督学习和偏好优化，并加强对稀有极端事件的关注；3）通过偏好优化细化决策边界，避免异构训练数据中的冲突信号。

Result: 在真实世界野火预测任务中，EAPO取得ROC-AUC 0.7310的稳健性能，在极端事件检测方面显著提升，验证了其在动态环境下的有效性。

Conclusion: EAPO框架有效融合了分布对齐、混合微调和偏好优化，为动态环境下的长尾极端事件预测提供了一种鲁棒且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting rare extreme events such as wildfires from meteorological data requires models that remain reliable under evolving environmental conditions. This problem is inherently long-tailed: wildfire events are rare but high-impact, while most observations correspond to non-fire conditions, causing standard learning objectives to underemphasize the minority class (fire) that matters most. In addition, models trained on historical distributions often fail under distribution shifts, exhibiting degraded performance in new environments. To this end, we propose Environment-Adaptive Preference Optimization (EAPO), a framework that adapts prediction to the target environment with long-tail distribution. Given a new input distribution, we first construct distribution-aligned datasets via $k$-nearest neighbor retrieval. We then perform a hybrid fine-tuning procedure on this local manifold, combining supervised learning with preference optimization, as well as emphasizing on rare extreme events. EAPO refines decision boundaries while avoiding conflicting signals from heterogeneous training data. We evaluate EAPO on a real-world wildfire prediction task with environmental shifts. EAPO achieves robust performance (ROC-AUC 0.7310) and improves detection in extreme regimes, demonstrating its effectiveness in dynamic wildfire prediction systems.

</details>


### [222] [Search Your Block Floating Point Scales!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12464)
*Tanmaey Gupta,Hayden Prairie,Xiaoxia Wu,Reyna Abhyankar,Qingyang Wu,Austin Silveria,Pragaash Ponnusamy,Jue Wang,Ben Athiwaratkun,Leon Song,Tri Dao,Daniel Y. Fu,Chris De Sa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ScaleSearch方法，通过细粒度搜索微缩格式中的尾数位来优化块浮点（BFP）的缩放因子选择，以最小化量化误差；并在此基础上设计了ScaleSearchAttention算法，实现NVFP4低精度注意力加速且性能几乎无损。实验表明ScaleSearch可将NVFP4量化误差降低27%，语言模型PTQ提升最高15个点，ScaleSearchAttention在Llama 3.1 70B上Wikitext-2 PPL提升0.77。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BFP算法基于块内最大幅度使用固定缩放因子，这种选择在量化误差方面可能是次优的。本文旨在通过更优的缩放因子选择策略来降低量化误差，提升低精度推理的性能。

Method: 提出ScaleSearch，利用微缩格式中的尾数位进行细粒度搜索，以最小化给定分布下的量化误差；并基于此设计ScaleSearchAttention，即加速的NVFP4注意力算法，整合了ScaleSearch与现有技术。

Result: ScaleSearch将NVFP4量化误差降低27%；语言模型PTQ在MATH500（Qwen3-8B）上提升最高15个点；ScaleSearchAttention在Llama 3.1 70B上Wikitext-2 PPL提升0.77。

Conclusion: 所提方法在接近基线性能的同时，提供了量化精度的改进，可集成到现有量化方法（如PTQ和低精度注意力）中提升其性能。

Abstract: Quantization has emerged as a standard technique for accelerating inference for generative models by enabling faster low-precision computations and reduced memory transfers. Recently, GPU accelerators have added first-class support for microscaling Block Floating Point (BFP) formats. Standard BFP algorithms use a fixed scale based on the maximum magnitude of the block. We observe that this scale choice can be suboptimal with respect to quantization errors. In this work, we propose ScaleSearch, an alternative strategy for selecting these scale factors: using a fine-grained search leveraging the mantissa bits in microscaling formats to minimize the quantization error for the given distribution. ScaleSearch can be integrated with existing quantization methods such as Post Training Quantization and low-precision attention, and is shown to improve their performance. Additionally, we introduce ScaleSearchAttention, an accelerated NVFP4-based attention algorithm, which uses ScaleSearch and adapted prior techniques to ensure near-0 performance loss for causal language modeling. Experiments show that ScaleSearch reduces quantization error by 27% for NVFP4 and improves language model PTQ by up to 15 points for MATH500 (Qwen3-8B), while ScaleSearchAttention improves Wikitext-2 PPL by upto 0.77 points for Llama 3.1 70B. The proposed methods closely match baseline performance while providing quantization accuracy improvements.

</details>


### [223] [High-arity Sample Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12465)
*Leonardo N. Coregliano,William Opich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了高元样本压缩方案的存在性与高元PAC可学习性之间的关系，证明了非平凡质量的高元样本压缩方案蕴涵高元PAC可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，一系列工作开始研究学习理论概念在乘积空间上的变体，形成所谓的高元学习理论。本文在此基础上进一步探索高元样本压缩方案这一概念，旨在建立其与高元PAC可学习性之间的理论联系。

Method: 本文采用理论分析方法，研究了高元样本压缩方案的概念，并通过数学证明论证了非平凡质量的高元样本压缩方案能够推导出高元PAC可学习性。

Result: 证明了高元样本压缩方案的存在性（非平凡质量）可以蕴涵高元PAC可学习性。

Conclusion: 本文在高元学习理论框架下，建立了样本压缩方案与PAC可学习性之间的理论联系，丰富了高元学习理论的内涵。

Abstract: Recently, a series of works have started studying variations of concepts from learning theory for product spaces, which can be collected under the name high-arity learning theory. In this work, we consider a high-arity variant of sample compression schemes and we prove that the existence of a high-arity sample compression scheme of non-trivial quality implies high-arity PAC learnability.

</details>


### [224] [Solve the Loop: Attractor Models for Language and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12466)
*Jacob Fein-Ashley,Paria Rashidinejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Attractor Models，通过隐式微分求解不动点来替代传统循环Transformer的显式迭代，在训练内存恒定、迭代深度自适应的条件下，在大规模语言模型预训练和推理任务上均超越现有模型，并发现模型能内化迭代计算从而在推理时可去除求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer虽能通过迭代精炼提升性能，但存在训练不稳定、优化部署成本高、受限于固定小深度等问题。作者旨在设计一种可扩展的迭代精炼机制，兼顾训练效率与深度灵活性。

Method: 提出Attractor Model架构：主干模块（backbone）首先生成初始输出嵌入，吸引子模块（attractor module）通过求解不动点来精炼该嵌入，梯度通过隐式微分（implicit differentiation）获得，无需展开计算图，从而训练内存不随有效深度增长，迭代次数由收敛条件自适应决定。

Result: 在语言建模中，相比标准Transformer和稳定循环模型，Attractor Model实现Pareto改进，困惑度最高降低46.6%，下游任务准确率提升19.7%，且训练成本更低；770M参数模型超越1.3B Transformer（训练数据量翻倍）。在推理任务（Sudoku-Extreme 91.4%，Maze-Hard 93.1%）上，仅27M参数和约1000样本即击败Claude和GPT o3等前沿模型及专用递归推理器。此外观察到equilibrium internalization现象：训练后模型初始嵌入已接近不动点，推理时可移除求解器而性能几乎不降。

Conclusion: Attractor Models通过将显式循环转化为可内化的隐式计算，实现了迭代精炼的可扩展训练与自适应深度推理，为构建高效、可伸缩的深度序列模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Looped Transformers offer a promising alternative to purely feed-forward computation by iteratively refining latent representations, improving language modeling and reasoning. Yet recurrent architectures remain unstable to train, costly to optimize and deploy, and constrained to small, fixed recurrence depths. We introduce Attractor Models, in which a backbone module first proposes output embeddings, then an attractor module refines them by solving for the fixed point, with gradients obtained through implicit differentiation. Thus, training memory remains constant in effective depth, and iterations are chosen adaptively by convergence. Empirically, Attractor Models outperform existing models across two regimes, large-scale language-model pretraining and reasoning with tiny models. In language modeling, Attractor Models deliver a Pareto improvement over standard Transformers and stable looped models across sizes, improving perplexity by up to 46.6% and downstream accuracy by up to 19.7% while reducing training cost. Notably, a 770M Attractor Model outperforms a 1.3B Transformer trained on twice as many tokens. On challenging reasoning tasks, we show that our model with only 27M parameters and approximately 1000 examples achieves 91.4% accuracy on Sudoku-Extreme and 93.1% on Maze-Hard, scaling favorably where frontier models like Claude and GPT o3, fail completely, and specialized recursive reasoners collapse at larger sizes. Lastly, we show that Attractor Models exhibit a novel phenomenon, which we call equilibrium internalization: fixed-point training places the model's initial output embedding near equilibrium, allowing the solver to be removed at inference time with little degradation. Together, these results suggest that Attractor Models make iterative refinement scalable by turning recurrence into a computation the model can learn to internalize.

</details>


### [225] [KV-Fold: One-Step KV-Cache Recurrence for Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12471)
*Alireza Nadali,Patrick Cooper,Ashutosh Trivedi,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV-Fold 提出了一种无需训练的、利用 KV 缓存作为折叠累加器的长上下文推理协议，通过逐块递归处理序列，在保持稳定性的同时实现远距离精确信息检索，在 128K 上下文和 511 层深度的针在干草堆测试中达到 100% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文推理方法要么需要模型训练/微调，要么使用有损的流式方法在有限内存下牺牲信息保真度。本文希望探索是否可以在不修改架构或训练的情况下，利用冻结的预训练 Transformer 实现稳定的长上下文推理。

Method: 将 KV 缓存视为左折叠（foldl）中的累加器，按块处理序列：处理当前块时，模型以先前块累积的 KV 缓存为前缀进行注意力计算，然后将新产生的 KV 追加到缓存中传递给下一块，形成块间递归。该过程无需训练，仅依赖于 KV 缓存拼接原语。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-8B 上，针对 16K 到 128K 上下文、深度达 511 层的链式处理，针在干草堆测试达到 100% 精确匹配检索；递归稳定性好，每步漂移先短暂上升后进入平稳平台，该平台对 10,000 倍数值精度变化、不同块大小和模型族均不敏感；全程可在单张 40GB GPU 上运行。

Conclusion: 冻结的预训练 Transformer 本身已支持稳定的 KV 缓存递归形式，KV-Fold 提供了一条无需架构修改或训练即可实现长上下文推理的实用路径。

Abstract: We introduce KV-Fold, a simple, training-free long-context inference protocol that treats the key-value (KV) cache as the accumulator in a left fold over sequence chunks. At each step, the model processes the next chunk conditioned on the accumulated cache, appends the newly produced keys and values, and passes the enlarged cache forward; the same one-step update is applied repeatedly, analogous to foldl in functional programming. Building on the KV cache concatenation primitive introduced for latent multi-agent communication, we repurpose it as a chunk-to-chunk recurrence for long-context inference. When processing chunk t, the model attends to the KV cache carried from earlier chunks as a prefix, reusing its internal state across segments without modifying or retraining the model. Despite its simplicity, the induced recurrence is stable: per-step drift rises briefly and then saturates into a flat plateau that persists across deep chains. This plateau is insensitive to a 10,000x change in numerical precision, robust across chunk sizes, and consistent across model families. At the task level, KV-Fold preserves exact information over long distances. On a needle-in-a-haystack benchmark, it achieves 100% exact-match retrieval across 152 trials spanning contexts from 16K to 128K tokens and chain depths up to 511 on Llama-3.1-8B, while remaining within the memory limits of a single 40GB GPU. Compared to streaming methods, which trade fidelity for bounded memory, KV-Fold maintains long-range retrieval while operating as a sequence of tractable forward passes. Overall, our results show that frozen pretrained transformers already support a stable form of KV-cache recurrence, providing a practical route to long-context inference without architectural changes or training.

</details>


### [226] [MEME: Multi-entity & Evolving Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12477)
*Seokwon Jung,Alexander Rubinstein,Arnas Uselis,Sangdoo Yun,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEME基准测试定义了六个跨越多实体和演化维度的任务，评估六种记忆系统后发现所有系统在依赖推理任务上表现极差（Cascade: 3%, Absence: 1%），提示优化、更深检索、减少填充噪声及更强LLM均无法解决该问题，仅文件型agent配合Claude Opus 4.7能部分弥合差距但成本高出约70倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估单实体更新，缺乏对多实体和动态演化场景下记忆系统能力的全面评估，需要定义更全面的任务空间来测试LLM-based agents在持久环境中的记忆与推理能力。

Method: 定义六个任务涵盖多实体和演化两个维度的全部空间，包括三个先前未评估的任务：Cascade（级联依赖推理）、Absence（缺失依赖推理）和Deletion（删除后状态推理）。在100个受控场景中评估六种跨越三种记忆范式的记忆系统。

Result: 所有系统在依赖推理任务上均崩溃（Cascade平均3%，Absence平均1%），尽管静态检索性能尚可。提示优化、更深检索、减少填充噪声及更强LLM均无法弥合差距，仅文件型agent配合Claude Opus 4.7能部分改善但成本高出约70倍。

Conclusion: 当前依赖推理能力的缺失在实际应用中不可忽视，且现有解决方案在规模化部署时成本过高，表明该问题尚未被有效解决。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly operate in persistent environments where they must store, update, and reason over information across many sessions. While prior benchmarks evaluate only single-entity updates, MEME defines six tasks spanning the full space defined by the multi-entity and evolving axes, including three not scored by prior work: Cascade and Absence (dependency reasoning) and Deletion (post-removal state). Evaluating six memory systems spanning three memory paradigms on 100 controlled episodes, we find that all systems collapse on dependency reasoning under the default configuration (Cascade: 3%, Absence: 1% in average accuracy) despite adequate static retrieval performance. Prompt optimization, deeper retrieval, reduced filler noise, and most stronger LLMs fail to close this gap. Only a file-based agent paired with Claude Opus 4.7 as its internal LLM partially closes the gap, but at ~70x the baseline cost, indicating closure currently depends on configurations that are not practical at scale. Code and data are available on the project page: https://seokwonjung-jay.github.io/meme-eval/.

</details>


### [227] [Beyond GRPO and On-Policy Distillation: An Empirical Sparse-to-Dense Reward Principle for Language-Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12483)
*Yuanda Xu,Hejian Sang,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种奖励密度原则，主张将稀缺的标注数据优先用于最强模型进行稀疏RL探索，再通过密集监督蒸馏到小模型，而非直接在部署模型上使用。在Qwen3和Llama上的数学验证任务中，该方法显著优于直接GRPO训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的做法是将稀缺的真实标注数据直接用于部署模型的训练（如GRPO），但论文认为这是一种低效分配，忽略了奖励密度原则：稀疏的序列级奖励应应用于探索有效的模型，而密集的token级教师奖励应用于将行为压缩到更小的模型。

Method: 提出"奖励密度原则"：使用稀疏RL在最强教师模型上进行行为发现，然后通过密集监督（教师蒸馏）将行为迁移到学生模型。具体包括：前向KL预热（教师rollout）+ OPD（学生rollout），之后可再进行学生侧稀疏RL。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上，RL增强的8B教师通过密集桥蒸馏优于直接GRPO；桥接后的学生侧GRPO将MATH准确率从75.4%提升至78.5%，比匹配的重放控制高2.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 稀缺标注数据不应直接在准备最不充分的策略（小模型）上使用，而应遵循：稀疏奖励用于教师侧发现，密集迁移用于学生压缩，学生侧稀疏奖励仅在桥接之后使用。

Abstract: In settings where labeled verifiable training data is the binding constraint, each checked example should be allocated carefully. The standard practice is to use this data directly on the model that will be deployed, for example by running GRPO on the deployment student. We argue that this is often an inefficient allocation because it overlooks a reward-density principle: sparse sequence-level reward should train models where exploration is productive, while dense token-level teacher reward should be used where the aim is to compress behavior into a smaller model. In this view, GRPO-style sparse RL and OPD-style dense teacher supervision are not separate recipes; they are different reward-density regimes. The allocation rule is simple: use scarce labeled training data upstream on the strongest model that can turn it into reward-shaped behavior, then transfer that behavior downstream as dense supervision. We evaluate this rule on verifiable math with Qwen3 and Llama models. At fixed Qwen3-1.7B deployment-student size, an RL-improved 8B teacher distilled through the dense bridge outperforms direct GRPO on the same student, while transfer from the same teacher before RL underperforms. The bridge is important: a forward-KL warmup on teacher rollouts followed by OPD on student rollouts is consistently strongest on MATH before any post-bridge student-side sparse RL, and also gives the best pre-Stage~3 AIME endpoints for the canonical 8B/14B teachers. The bridge also makes later student-side sparse RL effective: GRPO that is weak on a cold student lifts MATH from $75.4\%$ to $78.5\%$ after the bridge and outperforms a matched replay control by $2.8$ points. The operational principal is to avoid using scarce labeled data on the least prepared policy: use sparse reward for teacher-side discovery, dense transfer for student compression, and student-side sparse reward only after the bridge.

</details>


### [228] [Learning, Fast and Slow: Towards LLMs That Adapt Continually](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12484)
*Rishabh Tiwari,Kusha Sareen,Lakshya A Agrawal,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Matei Zaharia,Kurt Keutzer,Inderjit S Dhillon,Rishabh Agarwal,Devvrit Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出快速-慢速学习框架（FST），将模型参数作为慢权重、优化后的上下文作为快权重，在保持基础模型泛化能力的同时高效吸收任务特定信息，相比纯参数更新（RL）方法实现了3倍样本效率提升、更少灾难性遗忘和更好的持续学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM参数更新（如RL）会导致灾难性遗忘和可塑性丧失，而纯上下文学习虽能快速适应但性能有限。人类在不同时间尺度上学习（如系统1与系统2），受此启发，作者希望结合两种学习方式的优势。

Method: 提出Fast-Slow Training (FST)框架：模型参数作为慢权重（slow weights），优化后的上下文（如文本反馈）作为快权重（fast weights）。快权重吸收任务特定信息，慢权重保持基础模型的一般推理能力。

Result: ① 在推理任务上样本效率提升3倍，且能达到更高性能渐近线；② KL散度降低70%，灾难性遗忘显著减少；③ 保持可塑性：在一个任务上训练后，FST模型适应后续任务的能力优于纯参数更新模型；④ 在持续学习场景中，FST能持续获取新任务，而纯参数RL停滞。

Conclusion: FST框架有效结合了参数更新（慢学习）和上下文学习（快学习）的优势，在样本效率、抗遗忘、可塑性和持续学习方面均优于纯参数更新方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained for downstream tasks by updating their parameters (e.g., via RL). However, updating parameters forces them to absorb task-specific information, which can result in catastrophic forgetting and loss of plasticity. In contrast, in-context learning with fixed LLM parameters can cheaply and rapidly adapt to task-specific requirements (e.g., prompt optimization), but cannot by itself typically match the performance gains available through updating LLM parameters. There is no good reason for restricting learning to being in-context or in-weights. Moreover, humans also likely learn at different time scales (e.g., System 1 vs 2). To this end, we introduce a fast-slow learning framework for LLMs, with model parameters as "slow" weights and optimized context as "fast" weights. These fast "weights" can learn from textual feedback to absorb the task-specific information, while allowing slow weights to stay closer to the base model and persist general reasoning behaviors. Fast-Slow Training (FST) is up to 3x more sample-efficient than only slow learning (RL) across reasoning tasks, while consistently reaching a higher performance asymptote. Moreover, FST-trained models remain closer to the base LLM (up to 70% less KL divergence), resulting in less catastrophic forgetting than RL-training. This reduced drift also preserves plasticity: after training on one task, FST trained models adapt more effectively to a subsequent task than parameter-only trained models. In continual learning scenarios, where task domains change on the fly, FST continues to acquire each new task while parameter-only RL stalls.

</details>


### [229] [Pion: A Spectrum-Preserving Optimizer via Orthogonal Equivalence Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12492)
*Kexuan Shi,Hanxuan Li,Zeju Qiu,Yandong Wen,Simon Buchholz,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Pion是一种基于正交等价变换的谱保持优化器，通过左右正交变换更新权重矩阵，在训练过程中保持奇异值不变，为LLM预训练和微调提供了稳定且具有竞争力的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加法优化器（如Adam、Muon）在训练过程中会改变权重矩阵的谱结构，可能导致训练不稳定或泛化性能下降。作者希望设计一种能够保持权重矩阵奇异值（即谱信息）不变的优化方法。

Method: Pion通过左正交变换和右正交变换来更新每个权重矩阵，而非加性更新。具体步骤包括：推导Pion更新规则，系统检查设计选择，分析收敛行为及关键性质。

Result: 实验结果表明，Pion在LLM预训练和微调任务中均表现出稳定且具有竞争力的性能，能够作为标准优化器的有效替代方案。

Conclusion: Pion通过正交等价变换实现了谱保持优化，为LLM训练提供了一种新颖且有效的优化范式，在保持权重矩阵谱结构的同时取得了良好的收敛性能。

Abstract: We introduce Pion, a spectrum-preserving optimizer for large language model (LLM) training based on orthogonal equivalence transformation. Unlike additive optimizers such as Adam and Muon, Pion updates each weight matrix through left and right orthogonal transformations, preserving its singular values throughout training. This yields an optimization mechanism that modulates the geometry of weight matrices while keeping their spectral norm fixed. We derive the Pion update rule, systematically examine its design choices, and analyze its convergence behavior along with several key properties. Empirical results show that Pion offers a stable and competitive alternative to standard optimizers for both LLM pretraining and finetuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Control Charts for Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11135)
*Hayden Helm,Carey Priebe,Brandon Duderstadt*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了自适应控制图到多智能体系统以实现自动化监控，并通过理论和实证证明：要么系统中的智能体不能学习，要么系统容易受到对抗性攻击——这是一个根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开放环境下多智能体系统的动态监控仅局限于定性检查，缺乏自动化监控方法。

Method: 将过程理论中的自适应控制图扩展到多智能体系统，通过仿真验证其必要性，并利用理论和实证分析其对抗性脆弱性。

Result: 自适应控制图对监控可学习的多智能体系统是必要的，但同时易受到缓慢叛逃的对抗性智能体的攻击。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统控制中存在一个根本性权衡：要么系统内的智能体不能学习，要么系统易受对抗性攻击。

Abstract: Generative agents have proven to be powerful assistants in a wide variety of contexts. Given this success, users are now deploying agents with minimal restrictions in open ended, multi-agent environments. Current methods for monitoring the dynamics of open-ended multi-agent systems are limited to qualitative inspection. In this paper, we extend the process-theoretic notion of adaptive control charts to multi-agent systems to enable automated monitoring. Using simulation, we demonstrate that adaptive control charts are necessary for monitoring multi-agent systems that can learn from their environment. We further demonstrate, both empirically and theoretically, that adaptive control charts are susceptible to adversarial agents that defect sufficiently slowly. These results illustrate a fundamental tradeoff in multi-agent system control: either agents in a system cannot learn or the system is susceptible to adversaries.

</details>


### [231] [Information and Contract Design for Repeated Interactions between Agents with Misaligned Incentives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11294)
*Nanda Kishore Sreenivas,Kate Larson*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究信息不对称和激励错位下多智能体系统的通信与合同设计问题。发送者掌握私人信息但无法行动，接收者依赖该信息做决策。发送者学习最优通信策略，该策略对奖励冲突程度和接收者可观测的环境信息量高度敏感。引入线性合同机制后，发送者能通过定价提取接收者大部分剩余，引发公平性、合同设计与学习的伦理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多独立智能体场景中信息不对称和激励不一致所带来的后果，特别是在发送者持有关键私人信息但无法行动、接收者需依赖该信息做决策的交互模式下，如何实现有效通信与激励协调。

Method: 通过建模发送者-接收者交互框架，分析发送者学习的通信策略及其对奖励冲突程度和接收者环境信息量的敏感性。引入线性合同机制作为激励协调工具，研究其学习动态与分配效应。

Result: 发送者能够学到接收者可靠执行的通信策略；该策略对奖励冲突程度和接收者已有环境信息高度敏感；线性合同下发送者能提取接收者大部分剩余，提升自身奖励但牺牲智能体间的公平性。

Conclusion: 信息不对称下的合同设计可以实现激励协调，但会带来公平性问题，发送者通过定价机制获取不成比例的收益，对多智能体系统中的公平性、合同设计与学习机制提出反思。

Abstract: We study the consequences of information asymmetries and misaligned incentives in settings with multiple independent agents. We model an interaction between a Sender, who holds vital private information but cannot act, and a Receiver, who must make decisions but is dependent on the Sender's information. We find that the Sender learns an optimal communication strategy that the Receiver reliably acts on. Importantly, this strategy is highly sensitive to the degree of conflict in the agents' rewards and the amount of environmental information the Receiver can already observe. We introduce a mechanism allowing the agents to form linear contracts, where a price is established for the information. We demonstrate that the Sender learns to use these payment structures to improve its rewards, though this comes at a cost of "fairness" between agents as the Sender is able to extract much of the Receiver's surplus. This raises questions about fairness, contract design, and learning in the context of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [232] [Predictive Maps of Multi-Agent Reasoning: A Successor-Representation Spectrum for LLM Communication Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11453)
*Ethan David James Park,Dalal Alharthi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于后继表示的多智能体LLM通信图结构诊断方法，通过谱半径、谱隙和条件数三种谱量来预测链式、星型和网状拓扑中的漂移、共识与鲁棒性，在12步状态追踪任务上验证了条件数为完美排序预测器（rs=1.0），并发现谱半径与累积误差完美反相关（rs=-1.0），归因于线性谱对非收缩偏置漂移的盲区，提出了仿射噪声扩展模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前多智能体LLM系统在部署时需要在链式、星型、网状等通信拓扑中做出选择，但缺乏任何预推理诊断手段来评估哪种拓扑会放大漂移、收敛到共识或在扰动下保持鲁棒。现有评估方法只能在事后且针对特定任务给出答案，无法提供通用的结构性先验诊断。

Method: 基于行随机通信算子的后继表示 M = (I - γP)^{-1}，推导链式、星型和网状拓扑在行随机归一化下的闭式谱；将谱半径ρ(M)、谱隙Δ(M)和条件数κ(M)三种谱量与漂移、共识、鲁棒性三种失效模式建立联系；设计12步结构化状态追踪任务，使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct进行100次独立试验验证。

Result: 条件数是经验扰动鲁棒性的完美排序预测器（斯皮尔曼相关系数rs=1.0）；谱隙部分预测共识动力学（rs=0.5）；谱半径与累积误差呈完美反相关（rs=-1.0）。

Conclusion: 谱半径与累积误差的反相关表明线性谱对非收缩的偏置漂移存在盲区；提出的仿射噪声扩展模型恢复了经验排序。该工作可视为多智能体LLM系统在表示驱动的、感知漂移的结构诊断方向上的第一步探索，与经典谱理论与共识理论并行。

Abstract: Practitioners deploying multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems must currently choose between communication topologies such as chain, star, mesh, and richer variants without any pre-inference diagnostic for which topology will amplify drift, converge to consensus, or remain robust under perturbation. Existing evaluation answers these questions only post hoc and only for the task measured. We introduce a structural diagnostic for multi-agent LLM communication graphs based on the successor representation $M = (I - γP)^{-1}$ of the row-stochastic communication operator, and we connect three of its spectral quantities, the spectral radius $ρ(M)$, the spectral gap $Δ(M)$, and the condition number $κ(M)$, to three distinct failure modes. We derive closed-form spectra for the chain, star, and mesh under row-stochastic normalization, and validate the predictions on a 12-step structured state-tracking task with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct over 100 independent trials. The condition number is a perfect rank-order predictor of empirical perturbation robustness ($r_s = 1.0$); the spectral gap partially predicts consensus dynamics ($r_s = 0.5$); and the spectral radius is perfectly \emph{inverted} with respect to cumulative error ($r_s = -1.0$). We trace this inversion to a regime in which linear spectra are blind to non-contracting bias drift, and we propose an affine-noise extension of the predictive map that recovers the empirical ordering. We read this as a first step toward representational, drift-aware structural diagnostics for multi-agent LLM systems, sitting alongside classical spectral and consensus theory.

</details>


### [233] [Distance-Constrained Unlabeled Multi-Agent Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11503)
*Takahiro Suzuki,Yuma Tamura,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究了一种带距离约束的无标签多智能体路径规划问题，证明了其可行性判定为PSPACE完全问题，并提出了基于压缩和配置生成的两类互补算法，实验证明可处理数百个智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准无标签MAPF的可行性可在多项式时间内判定，但实际多智能体协调中要求智能体间保持安全距离（至少r+1），该额外约束使问题复杂度剧增，现有方法无法直接处理。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：(i) 基于归约的最优算法，结合保持可行性的压缩过程；(ii) 基于配置生成器的搜索算法。

Result: 尽管问题为PSPACE完全，但算法在实际上可在合理时间内处理数百个智能体。

Conclusion: 该工作将距离约束引入无标签MAPF，证明其高复杂度，但通过巧妙算法设计实现了实际可扩展性。

Abstract: We study a graph pathfinding problem Distance-$r$ Independent Unlabeled Multi-Agent Pathfinding, finding a set of collision-free paths between two sets where agents must stay at pairwise distance at least $r+1$ at all times. This additional constraint, generalizing collision modeling for classical MAPF, targets aspects of real-world multi-agent coordination. This additional distance constraint makes feasibility (i.e., whether a solution exists) PSPACE-complete, in contrast to standard (unlabeled) MAPF, where it can be decided in polynomial time. We address the challenge via two complementary approaches: (i) reduction-based optimal algorithms with a feasibility-preserving compression procedure, and (ii) a configuration generator-based search. Despite the hardness, empirical results show that our algorithm can handle hundreds of agents in a practical timeframe.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [On Knowledge Compilation For Two-Variable First-Order Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11796)
*Qiaolan Meng,Juhua Pu,Hongting Niu,Yuyi Wang,Yuanhong Wang,Ondřej Kuželka*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究FO2（一阶逻辑二变量片段）在有限域上的命题基化在DNNF类知识编译语言中的可编译性。理论上证明存在FO2句子需要指数级DNNF大小；实践上提出一种利用对称性、基于类型分支和子问题缓存的两阶段编译器，实验表明其效率优于传统基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究FO2的命题基化在知识编译语言（尤其是DNNF）中是否存在紧凑表示，以及能否高效构造，旨在弥合一阶逻辑与命题级知识编译之间的鸿沟。

Method: 首先通过下界证明指出一般情况不可紧凑编译；然后提出两阶段编译器：1）基于一元和二元类型分支（而非具体基原子），类似概率关系模型的提升推理；2）通过高效识别和缓存等价的剩余子问题来优化编译过程。

Result: 下界方面：存在FO2句子在域大小n下需要$2^{Ω(n)}$ DNNF大小；实验方面：所提方法相比直接基化基线，能生成更小的电路且编译速度更快。

Conclusion: FO2的命题基化在一般情况下无法紧凑编译为DNNF，但利用对称性和缓存策略可以在实践中实现高效编译，验证了提升（lifted）思想在知识编译中的有效性。

Abstract: Knowledge compilation transforms logical theories into circuit representations that support efficient reasoning. We study this problem for propositional groundings of FO2, the two-variable fragment of first-order logic over finite domains. Given an FO2 sentence and a domain of size n, its grounding yields a propositional theory over ground atoms. We ask whether such theories admit compact representations in DNNF-based and related knowledge compilation languages, and whether these can be constructed efficiently, both with respect to the domain size n for a fixed sentence. We show first that compact compilation is impossible in general: there exists an FO2 sentence whose grounding over a domain of size n requires DNNF size $2^{Ω(n)}$. On the positive side, we develop a two-stage compiler that exploits the symmetries inherent in the propositional groundings of FO2 sentences. It branches on unary and binary types rather than individual ground atoms, in a similar spirit to lifted inferences for probabilistic relational models. Moreover, it optimizes the compilation process by efficiently identifying and caching residual subproblems that are equivalent with respect to future extensions. Experiments show the practical efficiency of our approach, which often produces smaller circuits and compiles faster than straightforward grounding-based baselines.

</details>


### [235] [sweap: Reactive Synthesis for Infinite-State Integer Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11992)
*Shaun Azzopardi,Luca Di Stefano,Nir Piterman*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: sweap是一个用于无限状态线性整数算术反应式系统合成的工具，采用CEGAR方法，将有限状态合成工具作为黑盒使用，支持多种输入形式，具有双重抽象、非确定性和无界更新等新特性，在实验中优于其唯一竞争对手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，对涉及无限状态空间的规格说明进行反应式合成的兴趣显著增长，需要能够处理无限状态线性整数算术反应式系统的合成工具。

Method: 采用CEGAR方法，将最先进的有限状态合成工具作为黑盒来解决抽象合成问题；引入双重抽象方法以提升证明不可实现性的能力；支持非确定性和无界更新、更通用的变量初始化以及用于优化的等可实现归约。

Result: 实验评估表明，sweap在该领域优于其唯一竞争对手。

Conclusion: sweap是一个成熟的无限状态反应式合成工具，通过CEGAR框架和多种新特性，在无限状态线性整数算术反应式系统合成任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Recent years have seen a significant increase in the interest in reactive synthesis from specifications that relate to infinite state spaces. We present sweap, a tool for synthesis of infinite-state Linear Integer Arithmetic reactive systems. sweap implements a CEGAR approach, relying on state-of-the-art finite-state synthesis tools as black boxes to solve abstract synthesis problems. sweap supports most common input formalisms for infinite-state reactive-synthesis problems: Temporal Stream Logic Modulo Theories, Reactive Program Games, the bespoke input of the ISSY tool, and our own bespoke input. We present a mature version of sweap with novel features: a dual abstraction approach that improves its capabilities in proving unrealisability, support for nondeterministic and unbounded updates, more general initialization of variables, and equirealisable reductions for optimisation. Experimental evaluation shows that sweap outperforms its only competitor in this domain.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Black-Box Optimization of Mixed Binary-Continuous Variables: Challenges and Opportunities in Evolutionary Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12326)
*Md. Robiul Islam Niloy*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了进化模型融合技术（参数空间、数据流空间及混合方法），并首次形式化定义了数据流空间融合为混合二元-连续变量的黑箱优化问题。初步实验表明，尊重二元-连续条件依赖关系的结构化方法在准确率上提升6.7%，同时将有效搜索空间缩减51.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型融合成为训练大语言模型的低成本替代方案，但进化方法（特别是数据流空间融合）背后的优化挑战缺乏形式化定义和深入理解。

Method: 1) 结构化综述，将进化模型融合分为三类：参数空间融合、数据流空间融合、混合方法。2) 形式化定义DFS融合为混合二元-连续变量、高维搜索空间、含条件依赖的黑箱优化问题。

Result: 结构化方法（尊重二元-连续条件依赖）相比非结构化方法，准确率提升6.7%，有效搜索空间缩减51.4%。

Conclusion: DFS融合本质上是一个标准优化方法（如CMA-ES）无法直接处理的复杂黑箱优化问题，需要连接进化计算与模型融合社区的共同努力。

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a cost-effective alternative to training large language models (LLMs) from scratch, enabling researchers to combine pre-trained models into more capable systems without full retraining. Evolutionary approaches to model merging have shown particular promise, automatically searching for optimal merging configurations across both parameter space (PS) and data flow space (DFS). However, the optimization challenges underlying these approaches -- particularly in DFS merging -- remain poorly understood and formally underspecified in the literature. This paper makes two contributions. First, we provide a structured survey of evolutionary model merging techniques, organizing them into three categories: parameter-space merging, data flow space merging, and hybrid approaches. Second, we formally characterize the DFS merging problem as a black-box optimization problem involving mixed binary-continuous variables, high-dimensional search spaces, and conditional dependencies between variable types -- challenges that standard optimization methods such as CMA-ES are not designed to handle. We provide preliminary empirical validation using real pre-trained language models, demonstrating that a structured approach respecting the binary-continuous conditional dependency outperforms an unstructured approach by 6.7% accuracy while reducing the effective search space by 51.4%. By connecting the model merging community with the broader evolutionary computation and black-box optimization literature, we identify concrete open problems and propose research directions to address them.

</details>


### [237] [A Family of Quaternion-Valued Differential Evolution Algorithms for Numerical Function Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12362)
*Gerardo Altamirano-Gomez,Álvaro Gallardo,Carlos Ignacio Hernández Castellanos*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四元数代数的差分进化算法家族（QDE），通过设计专用变异策略利用四元数的代数与几何性质，在BBOB基准测试中相比传统实值DE实现了更快的收敛速度和更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI模型在替代数系（复数、四元数、几何代数）上的扩展已被证明能提升紧凑性和准确性，但这类扩展在生物启发优化算法中尚未得到充分探索。四元数代数作为计算智能中的新兴方向，其潜力有待挖掘。

Method: 提出四元数值差分进化（QDE）算法家族，直接在四元数空间中操作，并设计了多种专门利用四元数代数与几何特性的变异策略。

Result: 在BBOB基准测试的多个函数类别上，QDE变体相比传统实值DE算法实现了更快的收敛速度和更优的性能。

Conclusion: 四元数代数可有效扩展至差分进化算法，其代数与几何特性能够提升优化性能，为生物启发优化算法在替代数系上的研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: The numerical optimization of continuous functions is a fundamental task in many scientific and engineering domains, ranging from mechanical design to training of artificial intelligence models. Among the most effective and widely used algorithms for this purpose is Differential Evolution (DE), known for its simplicity and strong performance. Recent research has shown that adapting AI models to operate over alternative number systems-such as complex numbers, quaternions, and geometric algebras-can improve model compactness and accuracy. However, such extensions remain underexplored in bio-inspired optimization algorithms. In particular, the use of quaternion algebra represents an emerging area in computational intelligence. This paper introduces a family of novel Quaternion-Valued Differential Evolution (QDE) algorithms that operate directly in the quaternion space. We propose several mutation strategies specifically designed to exploit the algebraic and geometric properties of quaternions. Results show that our QDE variants achieve faster convergence and superior performance on several function classes in the BBOB benchmark compared to the traditional real-valued DE algorithm.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Skill Drift Is Contract Violation: Proactive Maintenance for LLM Agent Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10990)
*Linfeng Fan,Yuan Tian,Ziwei Li,Zhiwu Lu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出技能漂移检测方法，通过提取技能文档中的环境契约并验证其是否被违反，将监控从噪声信号转变为精准维护信号，在599个无漂移和硬负例案例中零误报，在49个真实技能中以86%精确率发现漂移，并使修复成功率从10%提升至78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体技能库会因外部服务、包、API和配置的演变而发生静默退化，而现有监控方法粒度不当：它们观察数值而非数值在技能中扮演的角色（如版本号在注释中是噪音，在固定依赖中则是操作义务），导致大量误报。

Method: 将技能漂移形式化为契约违反问题，提出方法从技能文档中提取可执行的环境契约，仅验证那些承载特定角色的假设是否与已知或当前条件相符，从而区分不同上下文中数值的意义。

Result: 无契约的CI探测产生40%误报，而本方法在599个无漂移和硬负例案例中零误报（Wilson 95% CI [0,0.6]%）；在已知漂移验证中，最强骨干模型实现100%精确率和76%召回率；在49个真实技能的预注册研究中，以86%保守精确率发现实时漂移；修复成功率从10%提升至78%。此外发布了包含880对的技能退化基准数据集。

Conclusion: 将技能漂移检测从值级监控提升为角色感知的契约验证，能显著降低误报率并使修复更具可操作性，为LLM智能体技能库的长期维护提供了精确、可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on reusable skill libraries, but these skills silently decay as the external services, packages, APIs, and configurations they reference evolve. Existing monitors detect such changes at the wrong granularity: they observe values, not the role those values play in a skill. A version string in a comment is noise; the same string in a pinned dependency is an operational obligation. We formulate skill drift as contract violation and introduce \sgname{}, which extracts executable environment contracts from skill documents and validates only those role-bearing assumptions against known or live conditions. This distinction turns noisy monitoring into a precision-first maintenance signal. Contract-free CI probes produce 40\% false positives, while \sgname{} raises zero false alarms over 599 no-drift and hard-negative cases (Wilson 95\% CI $[0,0.6]\%$). In known-drift verification, \sgname{} achieves 100\% precision and 76\% recall with the strongest backbone; in a pre-registered study over 49 real skills, it discovers live drift with 86\% conservative precision. Violated contracts also make repair actionable, improving one-round success from 10\% without localization to 78\%. We release \dbname{}, an 880-pair benchmark for skill degradation.

</details>


### [239] [An Execution-Verified Multi-Language Benchmark for Code Semantic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11006)
*Yikun Li,Jinfeng Jiang,Ting Zhang,Chengran Yang,Chenxing Zhong,Yin Yide,Leow Wen Bin,Eng Lieh Ouh,Lwin Khin Shar,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceEval是一个基于执行验证的多语言基准测试，用于评估大语言模型从源代码中恢复程序运行时调用结构（调用图）的能力。包含10,583个真实世界程序（Python/JavaScript/Java），通过执行追踪确保标注可靠性。最强模型Claude-Opus-4.6零样本F1达72.9%，微调Qwen2.5-Coder-32B可达71.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码基准（如HumanEval、MBPP、SWE-Bench）主要关注输出是否通过测试，但无法诊断模型能否从源代码中恢复与执行相关的程序结构（即运行时调用图）。同时，已有调用图基准依赖静态工具输出或人工标注，存在标注者分歧和标签噪声问题。

Method: (1) 从1,600+开源仓库中提取10,583个真实程序（2,129测试/8,454训练），覆盖Python、JavaScript、Java三种语言；(2) 使用LLM辅助的harness生成管道，并通过执行追踪（tracer validation）对每个正边进行机械验证，消除标注噪声；(3) 提供可复现的管道，可将任意开源仓库转换为新的已验证基准实例。

Result: 10个LLM零样本评估中，最强模型Claude-Opus-4.6在三语言平均F1为72.9%。在训练集上微调Qwen2.5-Coder系列，最大提升+55.6 F1，微调后Qwen2.5-Coder-32B达71.2%，仅比Claude-Opus-4.6低1.7 F1。

Conclusion: TraceEval是首个执行验证的、多语言的代码语义推理基准，能够更细粒度地评估LLM对程序结构的理解能力。训练集可作为有效的监督微调资源，显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Evaluating whether large language models (LLMs) can recover execution-relevant program structure, rather than only produce code that passes tests, remains an open problem. Existing code benchmarks emphasize test-passing outputs, from standalone programming tasks (HumanEval, MBPP, LiveCodeBench) to repository repair (SWE-Bench); this is useful, but offers limited diagnostic signal about which program semantics a model can recover from source. We introduce TraceEval, to our knowledge the first execution-verified, multi-language benchmark for code semantic reasoning: recovering a program's runtime call structure from source code. Unlike prior call-graph benchmarks that rely on static-tool output or hand-annotated ground truth, every positive edge in TraceEval is mechanically witnessed by validation execution, eliminating annotator disagreement and label noise for observed behavior. TraceEval consists of (i) 10,583 real-world programs (2,129 test, 8,454 train) extracted from 1,600+ open-source repositories across Python, JavaScript, and Java via an LLM-assisted harness-generation pipeline with tracer validation; and (ii) a reproducible pipeline that converts any open-source repository into new verified benchmark instances. We evaluate 10 LLMs at zero-shot on the held-out test split. The strongest model, Claude-Opus-4.6, reaches an average F1 of 72.9% across the three languages. To demonstrate the train split's utility as a supervision substrate, we fine-tune the Qwen2.5-Coder family on it: lifts of up to +55.6 F1 bring tuned Qwen2.5-Coder-32B to 71.2%, within 1.7 F1 of zero-shot Claude-Opus-4.6. We release the benchmark, pipeline, baselines, and a datasheet at https://github.com/yikun-li/TraceEva

</details>


### [240] [From Code-Centric to Intent-Centric Software Engineering: A Reflexive Thematic Analysis of Generative AI, Agentic Systems, and Engineering Accountability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11027)
*Elyson De La Cruz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过反思性主题分析和解释性现象学分析，研究生成式AI和智能体系统如何推动软件工程从以代码为中心转向以意图为中心的人机协作模式，结果表明软件工程正从独立代码创作转向监督、验证和治理人-智能体-工具-证据门的社会技术系统，速度导向的采用会积累隐藏技术债务，而有边界的自主性可以维护质量、安全、可维护性和信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注代码生成、AI结对编程和软件工程智能体，但较少了解公共技术论述和同行评审证据如何共同塑造该职业的近期转型。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用反思性主题分析（RTA）为主导、解释性现象学分析（IPA）为辅助的公共论述与文档分析方法。语料库整合了同行评审文献、技术基准、公开演讲与访谈、文章、产品技术公告以及X平台上的知名AI和软件工程人士的论述。通过语料登记表、编码手册、编码矩阵、主题-来源可追溯性表、DOI/参考文献审计和可复现性协议进行组织分析。

Result: 生成式AI降低了生成可行代码的成本，同时提高了意图规范、上下文策展、架构知识、验证、安全、溯源、治理和负责任的人类判断的重要性。软件工程正从孤立的代码创作转向监督、验证和治理人-智能体-工具-证据门的社会技术系统。

Conclusion: 速度导向的采用会积累隐藏的技术债务和问责缺口，而有边界的自主性可以维护质量、安全、可维护性和信任。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) and agentic systems are moving software engineering from code-centric production toward intent-centric human-agent work in which natural language, repository context, tools, tests, and governance shape delivery. Prior studies examine code generation, AI pair programming, and software engineering agents, but less is known about how public technical discourse and peer-reviewed evidence together frame the profession's near-term transition. This study addresses that gap through a reflexive thematic analysis (RTA) dominant and interpretative phenomenological analysis (IPA) informed public-discourse and document analysis. The corpus combines peer-reviewed software engineering and AI literature, technical benchmarks, public talks and interviews, essays, product-facing technical announcements, and X-originated discourse from prominent AI and software engineering voices. Sources were organized through a corpus register, codebook, coding matrix, theme-to-source traceability table, DOI/reference audit, and reproducibility protocol. The analysis shows that GenAI lowers the cost of producing plausible code while increasing the importance of intent specification, context curation, architecture knowledge, verification, security, provenance, governance, and accountable human judgment. The findings indicate that software engineering is becoming less about isolated code authorship and more about supervising, validating, and governing socio-technical systems of humans, agents, tools, and evidence gates. This matters because speed-focused adoption can accumulate hidden technical debt and accountability gaps, whereas bounded autonomy can preserve quality, security, maintainability, and trust.

</details>


### [241] [SHIA: A Direct SysML-Hardware Interface Architecture for Model-Centric Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11248)
*Charles Lewis,Amal Elsokary,Siyuan Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SysML硬件接口架构（SHIA），通过在SysML模型与物理硬件之间建立直接双向通信链路，使可执行SysML模型直接参与硬件验证循环，避免中间转换链或协同仿真平台。基于逻辑门案例的端到端验证表明，SysML生成的输出与硬件生成输出完全一致（卡诺图零差异）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBSE中SysML模型在硬件验证阶段被遗弃，模型与实现脱节，缺乏直接将SysML可执行模型接入硬件验证闭环的轻量级方法。

Method: 提出SHIA架构，包含SysML端服务器（IBM Rhapsody中嵌入C++实现）和硬件端服务器（Raspberry Pi运行），两者建立双向通信链路；通过状态机控制进行分阶段组件验证与集成验证。

Result: 集成系统双向消息交换正确，SysML生成输出与硬件生成输出的卡诺图比较零偏差。

Conclusion: SysML可超越静态描述角色，作为可执行层直接激励、观察和验证硬件行为，实现模型治理的验证，缩短从系统架构到硬件的数字主线。

Abstract: Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) is widely treated as the backbone of digital engineering, with languages such as the Systems Modeling Language (SysML) providing the means to capture system structure, behaviour, and verification intent. Yet once verification moves to hardware, the system model is routinely left behind. Domain-specific simulation environments, model transformations, and bespoke tool integrations take over, and the model that began as the authoritative reference drifts out of sync with the implementation it was meant to govern. This paper introduces the SysML Hardware Interface Architecture (SHIA), which keeps an executable SysML model directly inside the verification loop, exchanging messages with physical hardware without intermediate transformation chains, co-simulation platforms, or broker-mediated plugins. SHIA is realised through a SysML side server, written in embedded C++ within IBM Rhapsody, and a hardware side server running on a Raspberry Pi, together establishing a bidirectional link between the digital model and the physical system. A logic gate case study demonstrates the approach end-to-end, from hardware model construction and prototype assembly to test harness design, behavioural statechart control, and staged verification of each component before integration. The integrated system exchanged messages correctly in both directions, and Karnaugh map comparison between the SysML-generated and hardware-generated outputs showed zero discrepancy. The result shows that, when paired with a suitable interface, SysML need not remain a static description that informs downstream tools; it can serve as the executable layer through which hardware behaviour is stimulated, observed, and verified. The work demonstrates a route to model-governed verification and a shorter digital thread between system architecture and the hardware that realises it.

</details>


### [242] [An Executable Benchmarking Suite for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11030)
*Zhiqing Zhong,Zhijing Ye,Jiamin Wang,Xiaodong Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可执行的基准测试套件，通过统一的证据接纳契约，显式区分工作负载、动作生成驱动和系统级声明证据，并在WebArena Verified、SWE-Gym、MiniWoB++等环境中验证了其决策相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环工具使用智能体的基准测试报告常混淆工作负载、动作生成驱动和系统级声明所依据的证据，缺乏统一的证据接纳标准。

Method: 构建了一个可执行基准测试套件，包含通用工作负载适配器、任务清单、事件模式、重放/冻结策略、声明驱动和报告管线，通过证据接纳门控将面向论文的证据与预检、夹具、烟雾测试和诊断行分离。

Result: 在WebArena Verified的控制器研究中，干净基线和中等实时压力评估在相同工作负载和接纳契约下选择了不同的固定控制器变体，证明了门控具有决策相关性。

Conclusion: 该套件定位于基准测试套件和接纳证据，而非新智能体策略、模型排行榜、后端比较或自主SWE-bench求解器。

Abstract: Closed-loop tool-using agents are increasingly evaluated in executable web, code, and micro-task environments, but benchmark reports often conflate workloads, action-generating drivers, and the evidence admitted for systems-facing claims. We present an executable benchmarking suite that makes these objects explicit under a shared evidence-admission contract. The suite connects WebArena Verified, a SWE-Gym slice with SWE-bench-compatible verification, and MiniWoB++ through common workload adapters, task manifests, event schemas, replay/freeze policy, declared drivers, and reporting pipelines. In the canonical release, the gate separates paper-facing evidence from preflight, fixture, smoke, and diagnostic rows while preserving non-admitted artifacts for audit and onboarding. The admitted evidence records latency, invalid-action behavior, patch-generation cost, verifier metadata, replay bindings, and provenance under one auditable contract. The gate is decision-relevant rather than merely clerical: in a separate WebArena Verified controller study, clean-baseline and medium live-stressed evaluation select different fixed controller variants under the same workload and admission contract. The release is scoped as a benchmarking suite and admitted evidence, not a new agent policy, model leaderboard, backend comparison, or autonomous SWE-bench solver.

</details>


### [243] [Read, Extract, Classify: A Tool for Smarter Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11045)
*Paheli Bhattacharya,Manojit Chakraborty,Santhosh Kumar Arumugam,Rishabh Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReXCL是一个自动化需求工程工具，通过提取和分类两个模块，将半结构化需求文档处理为预定义模式并自动分类，显著提升了需求管理的效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程中提取和分类过程通常依赖人工，效率低下且容易出错，缺乏端到端的自动化方案来处理半结构化需求文档。

Method: 包含两个核心模块：1) 提取模块，利用启发式规则和预测建模将原始需求文档处理为预定义模式；2) 分类模块，通过编码器模型的自适应微调对需求进行类别标注。最终输出可导出至外部需求工程工具。

Result: 性能评估表明ReXCL在需求管理效率和准确性方面有显著提升。

Conclusion: ReXCL提出了一种新颖的自动化方法，用于半结构化需求文档的模式化处理，标志着该领域的创新突破。

Abstract: This paper presents the ReXCL tool, which automates the extraction and classification processes in requirements engineering, enhancing the software development life-cycle. The tool features two main modules: Extraction, which processes raw requirement documents into a predefined schema using heuristics and predictive modeling, and Classification, which assigns class labels to requirements using adaptive fine-tuning of encoder-based models. The final output can be exported to external requirement engineering tools. Performance evaluations indicate that ReXCL significantly improves efficiency and accuracy in managing requirements, marking a novel approach to automating the schematization of semi-structured requirement documents.

</details>


### [244] [Natural Language based Specification and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11315)
*Zhaorui Li,Chengyu Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索使用大语言模型以自然语言生成形式规范，并通过组合验证方法确保LLM生成代码的安全性，初步结果令人鼓舞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成代码日益普遍，需要防止模型产生含漏洞的实现。形式化验证提供了一条原则性路径，但现有验证流程通常需要严格形式语言编写的规范，而LLM合成此类规范的成功有限。

Method: 采用LLM同时生成规范并以自然语言表达，然后组合式地验证实现是否符合这些规范。

Result: 初步结果表明该方法是可行的，且具有前景。

Conclusion: 使用自然语言作为规范语言，结合LLM进行生成与组合验证，是降低形式化验证门槛、提升LLM生成代码安全性的有效方向。

Abstract: Recent frontier large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in identifying security vulnerabilities in large, mature open-source systems. As LLM-generated code becomes increasingly common, a natural goal is to prevent such models from producing vulnerable implementations in the first place. Formal verification offers a principled route to this objective, but existing verification pipelines typically require specifications written in rigid formal languages. Prior work has explored using LLMs to synthesize such specifications, with limited success. In this paper, we investigate a different approach: using LLMs both to generate specifications and to verify implementations compositionally when the specifications are expressed in natural language. Our preliminary results suggest that this approach is promising.

</details>


### [245] [Decaf: Improving Neural Decompilation with Automatic Feedback and Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11501)
*Alexander Shypula,Osbert Bastani,Edward Schwartz*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Decaf系统通过编译器反馈搜索机制，将神经反编译的成功率从26.0%提升至83.9%，在不牺牲源代码相似度的前提下显著改善了反编译结果的语义正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的确定性反编译器难以推断惯用语法和标识符名称，而生成式AI模型容易产生幻觉，生成不正确的编程结构和语义。作者认为与其通过更多数据和训练改进神经反编译器，不如利用编译器反馈通过搜索来提升输出结果的语义正确性。

Method: 提出Decaf（DECompilation with Automated Feedback）系统，将编译器反馈作为指导信号，通过搜索机制优化神经反编译器的输出，在不依赖额外训练数据的情况下提升反编译结果的语义正确性。

Result: 在ExeBench数据集上，反编译成功率从26.0%提升至83.9%（Real-O2分割），且不牺牲与原始源代码的相似度。该方法对较弱的神经反编译模型同样有效。

Conclusion: 编译器反馈驱动的搜索策略能够有效提升神经反编译的语义正确性，是一种比单纯增加数据和训练更高效的方法，尤其适用于弱模型的性能提升。

Abstract: Decompilers are useful tools used in reverse engineering to understand compiled source code. Reconstructing source code from compiled binaries is a challenging task, because high-level syntax, identifiers, and custom data types are generally lost as the compiler translates human-readable code to low-level machine code. Deterministic decompilers are useful tools for binary analysis, but can struggle to infer idiomatic syntax and identifier names. Generative AI models are a natural fit for reconstructing high-level syntax, identifiers, and types, but they can still suffer by hallucinating improper programming constructs and semantics. Instead of attempting to improve neural decompilers with more data and more training, we argue that compiler feedback can be used to dramatically improve the semantic correctness of neural decompiler outputs via search. Our system, Decaf (DECompilation with Automated Feedback), raises the neural decompilation rate from 26.0% on ExeBench to 83.9% on the Real -O2 split without sacrificing similarity to the original source code. We also find our automatic feedback methodology is highly effective for improving weaker neural decompilation models.

</details>


### [246] [An Extensive Replication Study of the ABLoTS Approach for Bug Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11790)
*Feifei Niu,Enshuo Zhang,Christoph Mayr-Dorn,Wesley Klewerton Guez Assunção,Liguo Huang,Jidong Ge,Bin Luo,Alexander Egyed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对ABLoTS方法进行了复现研究，发现其核心组件TraceScore在扩展数据集上表现良好，但由于原始论文中截止日期选择不当导致测试数据泄露到训练数据中，无法复现原始论文报告的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估ABLoTS方法的可复现性和实际适用性，验证其核心组件TraceScore在不同数据集上的性能表现。

Method: 对ABLoTS进行复现研究，使用原始数据集（11个Java项目，8,494个缺陷报告）以及两个扩展数据集（16个额外Java项目含25,893个缺陷报告，12个Python项目含1,289个缺陷报告），分析TraceScore组件的性能。

Result: TraceScore在扩展数据集上表现可比甚至更优，但因原始论文中截止日期选择错误导致测试数据泄露到训练数据，无法复现原始论文报告的结果。

Conclusion: 原始ABLoTS论文存在数据泄露问题，影响了性能评估的可靠性；但TraceScore组件本身在更广泛的数据集上仍有效，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Bug localization is the task of recommending source code locations (typically files) that contain the cause of a bug and hence need to be changed to fix the bug. Along these lines, information retrieval-based bug localization (IRBL) approaches have been adopted, which identify the most bug-prone files from the source code space. In current practice, a series of state-of-the-art IRBL techniques leverage the combination of different components (e.g., similar reports, version history, and code structure) to achieve better performance. ABLoTS is a recently proposed approach with the core component, TraceScore, that utilizes requirements and traceability information between different issue reports (i.e., feature requests and bug reports) to identify buggy source code snippets with promising results. To evaluate the accuracy of these results and obtain additional insights into the practical applicability of ABLoTS, we conducted a replication study of this approach with the original dataset and also on two extended datasets (i.e., additional Java dataset and Python dataset). The original dataset consists of 11 open source Java projects with 8,494 bug reports. The extended Java dataset includes 16 more projects comprising 25,893 bug reports and corresponding source code commits. The extended Python dataset consists of 12 projects with 1,289 bug reports. While we find that the TraceScore component, which is the core of ABLoTS, produces comparable or even better results with the extended datasets, we also find that we cannot reproduce the ABLoTS results, as reported in its original paper, due to an overlooked side effect of incorrectly choosing a cut-off date that led to test data leaking into training data with significant effects on performance.

</details>


### [247] [The Death Spiral of Open Source Projects: A Post-Mortem Analysis of Pull Request Workflow Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11844)
*Mohit Kaushik,Kuljit Kaur Chahal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次对1,736个已不活跃的GitHub仓库和130万个人工驱动的Pull Request进行大规模尸检分析，发现OSS项目消亡的核心原因并非PR工作流效率低下，而是项目内在价值和生态系统动态因素（如流行度和创新性）主导了生存结果，工作流摩擦、拒绝率等指标只是项目衰亡的副产品而非原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从宏观指标（如提交活动、开发者流失、生态系统依赖）考察OSS项目死亡率，但忽略了微观层面的Pull Request工作流动态。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过大规模PR工作流后验分析揭示项目死亡的内在机制。

Method: 采用混合定量方法，对1,736个不活跃GitHub仓库的130万个人工驱动PR进行分析，包括：（1）比较描述性分析；（2）演化分析；（3）解释性建模。并通过替代性不活跃阈值进行稳健性检验。

Result: （1）工作流摩擦、审查周期延长和负面惩罚是所有GitHub项目的固有特性，非活跃项目特有；（2）发现普遍存在的"死亡螺旋"现象：创新率下降、积压呈指数增长、合并延迟上升，以沉默和脱离为特征；（3）项目寿命由内在价值和生态系统动态决定，而非工作流效率；流行度和创新性是生存的强正向预测因子，摩擦、拒绝率等是衰亡的副产品。

Conclusion: 将OSS项目死亡率重新定义为一种社会技术现象：开发者流失和生态系统价值主导生存结果，而PR层面的工作流纪律仅起次要作用。

Abstract: Open Source Software projects (OSS) are central to modern technology, yet their survival rates remain low. Prior research has examined project mortality through macro-level indicators such as commit activity, developer abandonment, and ecosystem dependencies, but the micro-level dynamics of the Pull Request (PR) workflow have been largely overlooked. This study provides the first large-scale post-mortem analysis of PR workflows across 1,736 inactive GitHub repositories and 1.3 million human-driven PRs. Using a mixed-method quantitative design, we investigate three dimensions of mortality. First, our comparative descriptive analysis shows that workflow friction, extended review cycles, and negativity penalties are endemic properties of the entire GitHub platform across both active and inactive projects. Rejected PRs consistently attract higher discussion and negativity regardless of project health. Second, our evolutionary analysis identifies a universal ``death spiral" marked by declining innovation rates, exponential backlog growth, rising merge latency. The collapse was defined by silence and disengagement. Labeling formalization remained endemic throughout the lifecycle, while toxicity did not intensify. Finally, our explanatory modeling demonstrates that project lifespan is not determined by workflow efficiency but by inherent value and ecosystem dynamics. Popularity and innovation emerge as strong positive predictors of survival, while friction, rejection rates, labeling formalization, and negativity scale with longevity as byproducts rather than causes of failure. Robustness checks across alternative inactivity thresholds confirm these findings. Together, this work reframes OSS mortality as a socio-technical phenomenon in which abandonment and ecosystem value dominate survival outcomes, while PR-level workflow discipline plays a secondary role.

</details>


### [248] [StepCodeReasoner: Aligning Code Reasoning with Stepwise Execution Traces via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11922)
*Hao Wang,Rui Li,Lei Sha,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: StepCodeReasoner通过引入显式的中间执行状态监督（结构化打印语句）和双层GRPO强化学习算法，在代码推理任务上达到SOTA性能，7B模型在CRUXEval和LiveCodeBench上分别达到91.1%和86.5%，超越GPT-4o和CodeReasoner基线，同时提升了代码生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码推理方法仅监督最终代码输出，忽略中间状态，导致奖励破解问题——模型通过不一致的推理过程得到正确答案。因此需要引入执行状态的显式监督。

Method: 提出StepCodeReasoner框架：(1) 自动插入结构化打印语句作为执行轨迹锚点，将代码推理转化为可验证的逐步执行建模问题；(2) 提出Bi-Level GRPO强化学习算法，在轨迹间（比较不同执行路径）和轨迹内（根据中间准确性对下游正确性的影响分配奖励）两个层面进行结构化信用分配。

Result: 7B模型在CRUXEval达91.1%，LiveCodeBench达86.5%，REval执行轨迹基准达82.9%，均显著优于CodeReasoner-7B基线（86.0%/77.7%/72.3%）和GPT-4o（85.6%/75.1%/77.3%），且代码生成性能也有提升。

Conclusion: 显式执行建模能够同时提升代码推理和代码生成能力，验证了中间状态监督的有效性。

Abstract: Existing code reasoning methods primarily supervise final code outputs, ignoring intermediate states, often leading to reward hacking where correct answers are obtained through inconsistent reasoning. We propose StepCodeReasoner, a framework that introduces explicit intermediate execution-state supervision. By automatically inserting structured print-based execution-trace anchors into code, the model is trained to predict runtime states at each step, transforming code reasoning into a verifiable, stepwise execution modeling problem. Building on this execution-aware method, we introduce Bi-Level GRPO, a reinforcement learning algorithm for structured credit assignment at two levels: inter-trajectory, comparing alternative execution paths, and intra-trajectory, rewarding intermediate accuracy based on its impact on downstream correctness. Extensive experiments demonstrate that StepCodeReasoner achieves SOTA performance in code reasoning. In particular, our 7B model achieves 91.1\% on CRUXEval and 86.5\% on LiveCodeBench, outperforming the CodeReasoner-7B baseline (86.0\% and 77.7\%) and GPT-4o (85.6\% and 75.1\%). Furthermore, on the execution-trace benchmark REval, our model scores 82.9\%, outperforming baseline CodeReasoner-7B (72.3\%), its 14B counterpart (81.1\%), and GPT-4o (77.3\%). Additionally, our approach also improves code generation performance, demonstrating that explicit execution modeling enhances both code reasoning and code generation.

</details>


### [249] [HM-Req: A Framework for Embedding Values within CPS Human Monitoring Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12100)
*Zoe Pfister,Ruth Breu,Michael Vierhauser*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出HM-Req框架，通过受控自然语言定义人类监控需求，并整合利益相关者的人类价值观到Value Dashboard中检测冲突，以解决CPS中伦理需求与隐私问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信息物理系统中，人类监控需求对于安全高效的人机协作至关重要，但人类价值观在需求工程中常被忽视，且非结构化的自然语言需求存在歧义和模糊性，导致冲突难以解决。

Method: 提出了HM-Req需求获取框架，包括一个受控自然语言（CNL）用于定义人类监控需求，将这些需求与利益相关者的人类价值观关联并集成到Value Dashboard中，以检测潜在冲突。

Result: 将CNL应用于不同数据集进行验证，并开展问卷调查和专家访谈，结果表明CNL能够捕获多样的人类监控需求，HM-Req对需求获取活动具有实用性。

Conclusion: HM-Req框架能够有效弥补CPS设计中人类价值观缺失的问题，通过结构化的方式解决需求冲突，促进符合伦理的系统设计。

Abstract: Monitoring humans, for example, their movement or location, is essential for safe and efficient human-machine collaboration in Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS). This information allows CPS to ensure safety properties, adapt their behaviour dynamically, and coordinate with humans. To ensure that the design of a CPS respects ethical principles and the privacy of its stakeholders, system requirements, particularly those related to human monitoring, must reflect the human values of all involved stakeholders. However, human values are often underrepresented in Software Engineering -- particularly during requirements elicitation and system design, crucial phases when introducing ethically critical functionality. Stakeholder values are often implicit and conflicting, yet rarely systematically captured. Furthermore, unstructured natural language requirements introduce ambiguity and vagueness, complicating conflict resolution. To address these problems, we propose HM-Req, a novel requirements elicitation framework including a Controlled Natural Language (CNL) for defining human monitoring requirements. These requirements are then augmented with human values from relevant stakeholders and integrated into a Value Dashboard to detect potential conflicts that require further discussion and resolution. Validation results, applying the CNL to different datasets and conducting a survey and expert interview, confirms the CNL's ability to capture diverse human monitoring requirements and show HM-Req's usefulness for requirements elicitation activities.

</details>


### [250] [Property-Level Reconstructability of Agent Decisions: An Anchor-Level Pilot Across Vendor SDK Adapter Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12078)
*Oleg Solozobov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨体制的决策事件重构方法（Decision Trace Reconstructor），应用于六个商用厂商SDK体制的锚定示例，评估每个决策事件模式（DES）属性的可重构性，发现严格治理完备性在42.9%至85.7%之间分为三个层级，并识别出一个体制无关的缺口（推理轨迹）和四个体制相关的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统在事后重建其决策过程时缺乏统一的跨体制评估框架，需要知道智能体做了什么、依据谁的授权、违反了哪项政策以及基于何种推理，但目前缺乏一个属性级别的统一模式来跨体制进行可重构性度量。

Method: 将Decision Trace Reconstructor方法直接应用于六个公开商业厂商SDK体制（涵盖云智能体、可观测性、工具使用、遥测和协议追踪）以及两个对照列的锚定工作示例，对每个决策事件模式（DES）属性分类为完全可填充、部分可填充、结构上不可填充或不透明，并进行单标注者、每单元一锚定的描述性分析。

Result: 严格治理完备性在42.9%至85.7%之间分为三个层级；识别出一个体制无关的缺口（推理轨迹）和四个体制相关的缺口，以及一个混合属性。输出结果可通过校验和从可复现性包中验证。

Conclusion: 不同厂商SDK体制之间在决策可重构性上已有显著差异，严格治理完备性呈现层级分化，存在体制无关和体制相关的信息缺口，表明需要更统一的跨体制决策追踪标准。

Abstract: Agentic AI failures need post-hoc reconstruction: what the agent did, on whose authority, against which policy, and from what reasoning. Cross-regime feasibility remains unmeasured under one property-level schema. We apply the Decision Trace Reconstructor unmodified to pinned worked-example anchors from six public vendor SDK regimes spanning cloud-agent, observability, tool-use, telemetry, and protocol traces, plus two comparator columns. Each Decision Event Schema (DES) property is classified as fully fillable, partially fillable, structurally unfillable, or opaque. Per-property reconstructability of an agent decision already varies between regimes at this anchor scale. Strict-governance-completeness separates into three tiers ranging from 42.9% to 85.7%, yielding one regime-independent gap (reasoning trace), four regime-dependent gaps, and one Mixed property; the pilot is single-annotator, one anchor per cell, descriptive, with outputs checksum-verifiable from a deposited reproducibility package.

</details>


### [251] [CIDR: A Large-Scale Industrial Source Code Dataset for Software Engineering Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12153)
*Vladislav Savenkov*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CIDR是一个包含2,440个工业代码库（3.73亿行代码，138种编程语言）的大规模数据集，通过与12家工业合作伙伴直接合作收集，包含专有生产代码及其结构化元数据，支持代码智能、软件质量分析、代码语言模型训练等研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码数据集主要来自公开开源平台，缺乏真实的工业级专有代码数据，限制了代码智能研究和代码语言模型在工业场景下的适用性。

Method: 与12家工业组织建立正式数据共享协议，通过多阶段流水线处理：结构化合作方入驻、自动元数据过滤与人工代码审查相结合的两阶段质量筛选、覆盖完整版本控制历史的确定性匿名化处理。

Result: 构建了包含2,440个仓库、138种编程语言、总计3.73亿行代码的数据集，覆盖企业Web/移动开发、金融科技和定制软件咨询等应用领域，附带结构化元数据。

Conclusion: CIDR为代码智能、软件质量分析、代码语言模型预训练与微调、开发者行为研究及智能体评估基准构建提供工业级数据资源，采用受限商业许可证提供访问。

Abstract: We present Curated Industrial Developer Repository (CIDR), a large-scale dataset of real-world software repositories collected through direct collaboration with 12 industrial partner organizations. The dataset comprises 2,440 repositories spanning 138 programming languages and totalling 373 million lines of code, accompanied by structured per-repository metadata. Unlike existing code corpora derived from public open-source platforms, CIDR consists exclusively of proprietary production codebases contributed under formal data sharing agreements, covering application domains including enterprise web and mobile development, fintech, and custom software consultancy. All repositories were processed through a multi-stage pipeline encompassing structured partner onboarding, two-stage quality selection combining automated metadata filtering with manual code review, and a deterministic anonymization pipeline covering the full version control history. The dataset is intended to support research in code intelligence, software quality analysis, pre-training and fine-tuning of code language models, developer behaviour studies, and construction of agent evaluation benchmarks. Access is provided under a restricted commercial license; details are available at https://fermatix.ai/#Contact.

</details>


### [252] [ReproBreak: A Dataset of Reproducible Web Locator Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12158)
*Thiago Santos de Moura,Leon Adamietz,Samra Mehboob,Yannic Noller*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReproBreak数据集，包含449个可复现的Cypress和Playwright定位器失效案例，用于研究Web GUI测试中的定位器脆弱性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定位器失效（locator break）会导致测试因UI结构变化而失败，即使功能未变。目前缺乏大规模评估Cypress和Playwright定位器脆弱性的数据集。

Method: 分析了359个开源仓库中涉及定位器变更的提交，并在定位器变更最多的前4个项目中复现验证，最终得到449个定位器失效案例，构建了带自动复现脚本的数据集。

Result: 从359个仓库中识别出定位器变更提交，在前4个项目中复现并确认了449个定位器失效，数据集包含自动复现脚本。

Conclusion: ReproBreak可作为研究定位器脆弱性、修复技术和测试鲁棒性的有价值基准数据集。

Abstract: Automated GUI testing frameworks such as Cypress and Playwright rely on locators to find and interact with web elements. A locator break occurs when a structural change in the application under test causes a locator to no longer find its target element, resulting in test breakages even when the underlying functionality remains unchanged. Despite its impact on test maintenance, no dataset exists to evaluate locator fragility in Cypress and Playwright at scale. In this paper, we present ReproBreak, a dataset of reproducible locator breaks in web application GUI tests. We analyzed 359 open-source repositories to identify commits that contain locator changes. To confirm whether these changes are indeed locator breaks, we reproduced them in the top 4 projects with the largest number of locator changes and found 449 locator breaks, which are provided in the dataset along with scripts for automated reproduction. We believe ReproBreak serves as a valuable artifact to support research on locator fragility, repair techniques, and test robustness. The video is available at: https://youtu.be/mZByS_TnCvE. The dataset is at https://github.com/rub-sq/ReproBreak.

</details>


### [253] [Uncertainty Quantification for LLM-based Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12201)
*Senrong Xu,Yuhao Tan,Yanke Zhou,Guangyuan Wu,Zenan Li,Yuan Yao,Taolue Chen,Feng Xu,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出RisCoSet方法，利用多重假设检验为LLM代码生成构建风险可控的预测集（部分程序表示），保证高概率包含正确解，相比现有方法最多减少24.5%的代码移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PAC预测集方法存在强单调性假设和单标签分类框架的限制，严重缩小了候选程序空间，无法适应代码生成中多有效输出的特性。

Method: 采用多重假设检验构建风险控制预测集，将预测集表示为部分程序形式，保证高置信度包含正确解。

Result: 在三个LLM上的实验表明，与最先进方法相比，在相同风险水平下，代码移除量最多减少24.5%。

Conclusion: RisCoSet有效克服了现有方法在结构化生成任务中的局限性，为LLM代码生成提供了更灵活且风险可控的不确定性量化框架。

Abstract: Prediction sets provide a theoretically grounded framework for quantifying uncertainty in machine learning models. Adapting them to structured generation tasks, in particular, large language model (LLM) based code generation, remains a challenging problem. An existing attempt proposes PAC prediction sets but is limited by its strong monotonicity assumption on risk and single-label classification framework, which severely limits the space of candidate programs and cannot accommodate the multiple valid outputs inherent to code generation. To address these limitations, we propose an approach RisCoSet that leverages multiple hypothesis testing to construct risk-controlling predictions for LLM-based code generation. Given a trained code generation model, we produce a prediction set represented by a partial program, which is guaranteed to contain a correct solution with high confidence. Extensive experiments on three LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. For instance, compared with the state-of-the-art, our method can significantly reduce the code removal by up to 24.5%, at the same level of risk.

</details>


### [254] [Characterizing the Failure Modes of LLMs in Resolving Real-World GitHub Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12270)
*Yanjie Jiang,Yian Huang,Guancheng Wang,Junjie Chen,Hui Liu,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对三个先进LLM（Claude 4.5 Sonnet、Gemini 3 Pro、GPT-5）在SWE-bench Verified数据集上的900次GitHub问题修复尝试进行了系统性失败分析。通过243次失败案例的严格人工分析，构建了包含五个阶段的统一故障分类体系，发现策略制定与逻辑综合是最易出错的阶段，而定位阶段失败率最低。此外，不同模型的鲁棒性和运行成本差异显著，并揭示了现有评估框架因表面差异或隐藏约束而误判正确补丁的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM被广泛部署用于解决真实GitHub问题，但其在复杂修复任务中的具体失败模式尚不明确，缺乏对模型行为与人类开发者实践差异的系统性理解。

Method: 在SWE-bench Verified数据集上对三个SOTA模型（Claude 4.5 Sonnet、Gemini 3 Pro、GPT-5）共900次试验进行系统评估，对其中243次失败案例进行严格的人工症状与根因分析，构建统一失败分类体系。

Result: 建立了涵盖修复流水线五个阶段的统一失败分类体系；发现策略制定与逻辑综合是最易错阶段，而定位阶段失败率最低；不同模型的鲁棒性和运行成本差异显著；揭示了现有评估框架误判正确补丁的问题。

Conclusion: LLM在自动程序修复中可能擅长传统上被认为最具挑战性的故障定位任务；提出缓解失败的可操作策略；评估框架需改进以准确判断补丁正确性；为提升LLM问题修复的有效性和可靠性提供了方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed to resolve real-world GitHub issues. However, despite their potential, the specific failure modes of these models in complex repair tasks remain poorly understood. To characterize how LLM behavior diverges from human developer practices, this paper evaluates three state-of-the-art models, i.e., Claude 4.5 Sonnet, Gemini 3 Pro, and GPT-5, on the SWE-bench Verified dataset. We conduct a rigorous manual analysis of the symptoms and root causes underlying 243 failed attempts across 900 total trials. Our investigation first yields a unified failure taxonomy encompassing five distinct stages of the repair pipeline, within which we categorize typical failure symptoms and their prevalence. Secondly, our findings reveal that for all evaluated LLMs, strategy formulation and logic synthesis constitutes the most error-prone stage, followed by problem understanding, whereas localization exhibits the lowest failure rate. This suggests that LLMs may excel at fault localization, a task traditionally regarded as one of the most formidable challenges in automated program repair. Furthermore, we observe that robustness and operational costs (particularly in failure scenarios) vary significantly across different models. Finally, we uncover the root causes of these failures and propose actionable strategies to mitigate them. A particularly notable finding is that existing evaluation harnesses occasionally misjudge correct patches due to superficial discrepancies or hidden constraints. Collectively, our insights may provide promising directions for enhancing the effectiveness and reliability of LLM-based issue resolution.

</details>


### [255] [Iterative Audit Convergence in LLM-Managed Multi-Agent Systems: A Case Study in Prompt Engineering Quality Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12280)
*Elias Calboreanu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了对一个七通道LLM编排系统AEGIS（约7150行提示规范）进行九轮迭代式智能体审计的案例研究。由Claude子智能体执行基于检查表的审计，共发现51个提示规范一致性缺陷，并提出了七类事后缺陷分类法。研究观察到非单调收敛现象，且单文件审查无法发现仅通过扩展范围审计才能暴露的缺陷类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的提示规范承载着跨多个相互依赖文件的数据契约和集成逻辑，但很少受到结构化检查的严格审视，缺乏系统性的审计方法。

Method: 采用单一系统实证案例研究，对生产级七通道编排系统AEGIS进行九轮迭代的智能体驱动审计。审计由Claude子智能体执行，使用改编自Weinberg和Freedman的基于检查表的逐步审查方法。共审计约7150行提示规范（6907行通道PROMPT.md文件+245行共享票据契约）。

Result: 九轮审计共发现51个提示规范一致性缺陷，每轮缺陷数分别为15、8、12、2、8、1、4、1、0。提出了七类事后缺陷分类法（含显式编码规则），观察到与级联编辑和审计范围扩展一致的非单调收敛现象。单文件审查无法发现仅通过后期扩展范围审计才暴露的缺陷类别。

Conclusion: 研究提炼出一个审计协议，最终锁定检查表以可复现性附录形式发布。但需注意同一LLM家族同时编写和审计了这些规范，结论的泛化需要与不同模型和人类评审者进行复制研究。

Abstract: Prompt specifications for multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems carry data contracts and integration logic across many interdependent files but are rarely subjected to structured-inspection rigor. This paper reports a single-system empirical case study of iterative, agent-driven auditing applied to AEGIS (Autonomous Engineering Governance and Intelligence System), a production seven-lane orchestration pipeline whose prompt-specification surface comprises approximately 7150 lines: 6907 across seven lane PROMPT.md files and a 245-line shared Ticket Contract. Nine sequential audit rounds, executed by Claude sub-agents using a checklist-driven walkthrough adapted from Weinberg and Freedman, surfaced 51 prompt-specification consistency defects, distinct from the 51 STRIDE-categorized adversarial code findings reported in the companion preprint. Per-round counts were 15, 8, 12, 2, 8, 1, 4, 1, and 0. We report a seven-category post-hoc defect taxonomy with explicit coding rules, observed non-monotonic convergence consistent with cascading edits and audit-scope expansion, and an audit protocol distilled from the study, with the final locked checklist released as a reproducibility appendix. Single-file review missed defect classes that were surfaced only by later expanded-scope rounds in this system. The same LLM family authored and audited the specifications; replication with dissimilar models and human reviewers is required before generalization.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Automated Amortised Analysis of Skew Heaps and Leftist Heaps (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12091)
*Armin Walch,Georg Moser,Berry Schoenmakers,Florian Zuleger*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于泛型类型系统的自动化分摊资源分析方法，通过引入新的类型推断技术（路径敏感推理、数据结构不变量、分段势函数模板参数），在ATLAS系统基础上实现了对偏斜堆、左偏堆（权重/秩偏置）等纯函数式数据结构的最优代价界自动推断，并在Haskell中模块化实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化分摊资源分析系统（如ATLAS）仅能处理伸展树及其近亲数据结构，无法分析更复杂的偏斜堆和左偏堆（特别是分摊场景）；此外ATLAS使用硬编码势函数，缺乏灵活性和通用性。

Method: 1）提出泛型类型系统，支持任意类别势函数（替代硬编码方式）；2）扩展类型推断算法，集成路径敏感推理、数据结构不变量、分段势函数模板参数；3）在Haskell中实现模块化系统。

Result: 新系统支持所有已知用于分析偏斜堆和左偏堆的势函数，成功确认了已知的代价上界。

Conclusion: 通过泛型类型系统实现了更通用、模块化的自动化分摊资源分析，拓展了可分析数据结构的范围，并为精确最优代价界的推断提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: We study the fully automated amortised analysis of purely functional data structures like skew heaps, as well as weight- and rank-biased leftist heaps. For that we generalise earlier works on automated amortised resource analysis by developing a type inference based approach with a generic type system. This allows for modular reasoning and the inference of precise and optimal cost bounds.
  More specifically, we extend the work on the ATLAS system by Leutgeb et al. which was developed to cover the analysis of splay trees and some closely related data structures. To enable the analysis of skew heaps, however, and the even more challenging (amortised) analysis of leftist heaps, we have developed a range of new techniques for type-based automated analysis. By introducing a generic type system we allow for arbitrary (classes of) potential functions, compared to the use of hard-coded potential functions in ATLAS, which we have implemented in Haskell in an entirely modular way. We have also greatly enhanced the existing type inference algorithm by extensions in multiple directions, including path-sensitive reasoning, data structure invariants, and template parameters for piecewise defined potential functions. We show how our newly developed system supports the use of all known potential functions for analysing skew heaps and leftist heaps, confirming the known bounds.

</details>


### [257] [Harness Engineering as Categorical Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12239)
*Bogdan Banu*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出将ArchAgents框架中的范畴论架构三元组(G, Know, Phi)作为LLM Agent系统层的形式化理论基础，通过将记忆、技能、协议和工程支柱映射到该三元组，并实现编译器验证机制，证明范畴论可以形式化描述Agent harness工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的Agent harness（包含提示、工具、记忆和编排逻辑的系统层）设计缺乏形式化理论支撑，没有统一的组合原理、属性保持机制或跨框架比较方法，亟需建立严谨的形式化理论基础。

Method: 将Agent externalization的四个支柱（记忆、技能、协议、Harness工程）映射到ArchAgents框架的范畴论架构三元组(G, Know, Phi)，通过编译器函子实现结构保证的保持验证，并在Swarms、DeerFlow、Ralph、Scion、LangGraph五个框架上实现参考系统。

Result: 四个配置编译器以身份保持或重放方式保留三种命名的证书类型；LangGraph通过共享的每阶段执行路径保持相同证书；端到端升级实验证实质量驱动的升级控制路径是模型参数的。

Conclusion: 范畴论架构可以作为Agent harness工程的形式化理论，为Agent系统层的设计、组合和验证提供统一的理论基础。

Abstract: The agent harness, the system layer comprising prompts, tools, memory, and orchestration logic that surrounds the model, has emerged as the central engineering abstraction for LLMbased agents. Yet harness design remains ad hoc, with no formal theory governing composition, preservation of properties under compilation, or systematic comparison across frameworks. We show that the categorical Architecture triple (G, Know, Phi) from the ArchAgents framework provides exactly this formalization. The four pillars of agent externalization (Memory, Skills, Protocols, Harness Engineering) map onto the triple's components: Memory as coalgebraic state, Skills as operad-composed objects, Protocols as syntactic wiring G, and the full Harness as the Architecture itself. Structural guarantees-integrity gates, quality-based escalation, supported convergence checks-are Know-level certificates whose preservation is structural replay: our compiler checks identity and verifier replay, not output-layer correctness or model behavior. We validate this correspondence with a reference implementation featuring compiler functors targeting Swarms, DeerFlow, Ralph, Scion, and LangGraph: the four configuration compilers preserve three named certificate types by identity or replay, and LangGraph preserves the same certificates through its shared per-stage execution path. The LangGraph compiler creates one node per stage using the same per-stage method as the native runtime, providing LangGraph-native observability without reimplementing harness logic. An end-to-end escalation experiment with real LLM agents confirms that the quality-based escalation control path is model-parametric in this two-model, one-task experiment. The result positions categorical architecture as the formal theory behind harness engineering.

</details>
